*Pranshu Rastogi,Madhav Mathur,Ramaneswaran S,Kshitij Mohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对社交媒体恶意评论检测，本文通过集成模型与语言预处理方法，提出了一种能显著降低误报率的检测方案，旨在兼顾内容审核与言论自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交媒体普及，恶意评论问题日益严重。如何开发一种既能精准检测恶意内容，又不损害用户言论自由（即降低误报率）的系统，成为当务之急。

Method: 采用了基于语言学的预处理技术（language-based preprocessing）以及多种机器学习模型的集成方法（ensemble of models）进行恶意评论检测。

Result: 提出了一套优化的处理流水线，在实验中能够有效降低将正常评论误判为恶意行为的误报率。

Conclusion: 通过构建一种以降低误报率为优先目标的系统流水线，可以在有效识别恶意评论的同时，保护用户的言论自由，平衡了平台安全性与表达自由。

Abstract: In recent years social media has become an increasingly popular tool for communication. People use it to share their ideas, exchange information, and discuss thoughts. Given its prevalence and widespread reach, social media must remain a safe space for people. Content generated on social media can be abusive and it has become increasingly important to detect such content. In this paper, we use a language-based preprocessing and an ensemble of several models and analyze their performance of abusive comment detection. Through extensive experimentation, we propose a pipeline that minimizes the false-positive rate (marking non-abusive as abusive) so that these systems can detect abusive comments without undermining the freedom of expression.

</details>


### [39] [Unified Data Selection for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22389)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Chengpeng Li,Fengbin Zhu,Yiyao Yu,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HES的无需训练评价指标，通过计算推理样本中超高熵Token的熵之和，高效筛选高质量推理数据，从而在SFT/RFT/RL训练范式中以显著更低的算力成本提升LLM的复杂推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）进行复杂长思维链（Long-CoT）推理训练时，面临高质量推理数据极度缺乏、现有质量评估方法计算昂贵或评估不准确的瓶颈。

Method: 提出了“高熵求和”（High-Entropy Sum, HES）指标，通过仅对每个推理样本中前0.5%高熵标记（tokens）的熵进行求和来量化推理质量，并配套相应的筛选策略应用于SFT、RFT和RL三种训练范式。

Result: 实验证明HES具备跨范式的一致有效性：在SFT中，仅使用前20% HES数据即可达到全量训练效果；在RFT中显著优于基线；在RL中，基于HES选出的成功轨迹能引导模型习得高效的推理模式，且大幅降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: HES是一个强健且无需训练的度量标准，为开发具备高级推理能力的LLM提供了一种统一、有效且高效的解决方案。通过HES筛选数据，可以平衡计算代价与模型性能。

Abstract: Effectively training Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, long-CoT reasoning is often bottlenecked by the need for massive high-quality reasoning data. Existing methods are either computationally expensive or fail to reliably distinguish high- from low-quality reasoning samples. To address this, we propose High-Entropy Sum (HES), a training-free metric that quantifies reasoning quality by summing only the entropy of the top (e.g., 0.5\%) highest-entropy tokens in each reasoning sample. We validate HES across three mainstream training paradigms: Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT), Rejection Fine-tuning (RFT), and Reinforcement Learning (RL), with extensive results demonstrating its consistent effectiveness and significantly reduced computational overhead. In SFT, training on the top 20\% HES-ranked data matches full-dataset performance, while using the lowest-HES data degrades it. In RFT, our HES-based training approach significantly outperforms baseline methods. In RL, HES-selected successful trajectories enable the model to learn strong reasoning patterns, significantly surpassing other compared methods. Our findings establish HES as a robust, training-free metric that enables a unified, effective, and efficient method for developing advanced reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [40] [DeferMem: Query-Time Evidence Distillation via Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22411)
*Jianing Yin,Tan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 长程记忆检索噪声大、效率低的问题，提出 DeferMem 框架，通过“检索+蒸馏”双阶段处理，利用强化学习（DistillPO）将杂乱的对话历史转化为纯净、精准的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 记忆系统在面对长记忆 QA 任务时，往往在已知查询前预处理记忆，且仅依赖相似度检索，导致检索结果包含大量噪声，增加了下游模型处理和去噪的难度。

Method: 提出了 DeferMem 框架，包括：1) 基于 segment-link 结构的轻量级高召回率候选检索；2) 使用 DistillPO 强化学习算法进行证据蒸馏，将噪声候选片段转化为忠实且针对查询的证据。DistillPO 引入了分解门控奖励流水线和结构对齐优势分配技术。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 基准测试中，DeferMem 在 QA 准确率上超过了现有主流基准，且运行速度最快，同时实现了记忆操作零商业 API token 成本。

Conclusion: DeferMem 通过将长程记忆处理解耦为“广度检索”与“查询条件下的证据蒸馏”，有效解决了长对话历史中信息碎片化和噪声干扰的问题，在提升 QA 准确率的同时显著提高了系统效率并降低了成本。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents still struggle with long-term memory question answering, where answer-supporting evidence is often scattered across long conversational histories and buried in substantial irrelevant content. Existing memory systems typically process memory before future queries are known, then retrieve the resulting units based on similarity rather than their utility for answering the query. This workflow leaves downstream answerers to denoise retrieved candidates and reconstruct query-specific evidence. We present DeferMem, a long-term memory framework that decouples this problem into high-recall candidate retrieval and query-conditioned evidence distillation. DeferMem uses a lightweight segment-link structure to organize raw history and retrieve broad candidates at query time. It then applies a memory distiller trained with DistillPO, our reinforcement learning algorithm for distilling the high-recall but highly noisy candidates into a set of faithful, self-contained, and query-conditioned evidence. DistillPO formulates post-retrieval evidence distillation as a structured action comprising message selection and evidence rewriting. It optimizes this action with a decomposed-and-gated reward pipeline and structure-aligned advantage assignment, gating reward components from validity to quality checks while exposing task-level correctness feedback early and assigning each reward to its responsible output span. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S, DeferMem surpasses strong baselines in QA accuracy and memory-system efficiency, achieving the highest QA accuracy with the fastest runtime and zero commercial-API token cost for memory operations.

</details>


### [41] [Assisted Counterspeech Writing at the Crossroads of Hate Speech and Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22435)
*Genoveffa Martone,Helena Bonaldi,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索了利用LLM同时应对仇恨言论与虚假信息的方法，发现整合事实核查与NGO准则的混合策略效果最好，且专家的人类反馈是提升LLM生成质量的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论和虚假信息常在线上共存并相互助长，虽然目前利用大语言模型（LLM）撰写反仇恨言论（CS）的研究已有进展，但此前工作多将二者割裂处理。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索在仇恨言论与虚假信息交织情境下，如何高效生成应对策略。

Method: 研究人员提出了三种基于知识驱动的生成策略：仅使用事实核查资料、仅使用NGO指导方针、以及两者的混合策略。随后，由23位专家对生成内容进行修订，并通过人类评估与自动评估指标对其性能进行了分析。

Result: 仅有40%的LLM生成内容能直接达到“合格”标准；经过专家修订后的CS在自然度、全面性和指导方针依从性上大幅提升。评估显示，“混合策略”在纠正事实错误、缓解刻板印象及提供情感共鸣方面表现最为出色。此外，研究还公开了包含专家审核CS和辅助知识的数据集。

Conclusion: LLM辅助生成 counterspeech（反仇恨言论）时，采用结合事实核查信息与NGO指导方针的“混合策略”效果最优；尽管LLM能生成合格内容，但人类专家的修改对于提升生成内容的自然度、全面性和合规性至关重要。

Abstract: Hate speech and misinformation frequently co-occur online, amplifying prejudice and polarization. Given their scale, using Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist expert counterspeech (CS) writing has gained interest, yet prior work has addressed these phenomena separately. We bridge this gap by studying CS generation in contexts where both hate and misinformation co-occur. We test three knowledge-driven generation strategies: first we prompt an LLM with fact-checkers' guidelines and fact-checking articles; secondly, with NGOs' guidelines and reports; thirdly, we create a mixed strategy that combines guidelines and documents from both. 23 experts revise the generated CS, which are assessed via human and automatic metrics. While LLMs produce adequate CS in 40% of cases, expert edits substantially improve naturalness, exhaustiveness, and adherence to guidelines. Based on the post-edited CS, the mixed strategy proves to be the most effective in crowdsourcing evaluation, pairing strong factual correction with stereotype mitigation and empathetic engagement. We release a dataset of hateful and misinformed claims with expert-verified CS and supporting knowledge.

</details>


### [42] [Cohesion-6K: An Arabic Dataset for Analyzing Social Cohesion and Conflict in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22447)
*Aisha Ali Al-Athba,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Cohesion-6K 数据集，研究了阿拉伯语社交媒体中冲突与凝聚话语的动态平衡，发现冲突性内容获得的用户互动量远高于凝聚性内容，凸显了在线极化的传播机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于显性毒性内容的检测，而对在线话语中“冲突性”与“凝聚性”叙事间细微动态交互的研究不足。旨在通过量化手段探讨阿拉伯社交媒体中社会凝聚力的表现及分裂叙事的传播机制。

Method: 构建了 Cohesion-6K 数据集（包含 6,000 条关于以色列占领巴勒斯坦问题的阿拉伯语 Facebook 帖子）；采用“专家人工判定 + ChatGPT辅助预标注 + 人工校验”的混合标注方法，涵盖 5 类从冲突到凝聚的动态范畴；使用 Cohen's kappa 系数（0.85）评估标注一致性，并通过定量分析探讨帖子类型与用户互动率之间的关系。

Result: 实验发现存在显著的“互动差距”：冲突导向的帖子所获得的用户互动量是解决导向帖子的 2 至 4 倍（p < 0.01），证明了极化话语在社交媒体算法和用户行为中更具可见度。

Conclusion: Cohesion-6K 为研究在线凝聚力与极化提供了可靠的新数据集资源。研究证实了阿拉伯语社交媒体中存在显著的互动差距，即冲突导向的内容比旨在促进和谐的内容获得更多关注，这直接揭示了在线空间中分裂性叙事如何获得不成比例的高曝光度。

Abstract: The study of online discourse has become central to understanding societal polarization. While much research has focused on detecting overt toxicity, the subtle dynamics of social cohesion, meaning the interaction between divisive and unifying narratives, remain computationally underexplored (Bail, 2021; Gonzalez-Bailon and Lelkes, 2023). This paper presents Cohesion-6K, a manually and ChatGPT-assisted annotated dataset of six thousand Arabic public Facebook posts related to the Israeli Occupation of Palestine. Each post is assigned to one of five discourse categories that represent a continuum from conflict to cohesion: Conflict, Resolution, Community Engagement, Supportive Interactions, and Shared Values. The annotation process combines expert human judgment with model-assisted pre-labeling verified by trained annotators, achieving substantial inter-annotator agreement (Cohens kappa = 0.85). Quantitative analysis reveals a consistent engagement gap, where conflict-oriented posts receive between two and four times more user interaction than resolution-oriented ones (p < 0.01). This pattern illustrates how divisive discourse tends to attract disproportionate visibility in Arabic social media spaces. Cohesion-6K provides a transparent and reproducible resource for the study of online cohesion and polarization. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and preprocessing code will be released for research use under an open license, supporting future work in computational social science, digital communication, and Arabic natural language processing.

</details>


### [43] [From Correlation to Cause: A Five-Stage Methodology for Feature Analysis in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22462)
*Caleb Munigety*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结合因果验证、稳健性测试与部署经济学的五阶段方法论，深入解析GPT-2模型内的IOI任务电路，发现特征的选择性与其因果效应并不等同，并证明了该框架在构建高效模型监控系统中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决单一评估方法对Transformer语言模型内部机制解释的局限性，特别是在区分特征的表征能力与其实际因果影响能力方面的差异，并探索如何利用这些发现构建高性价比的监管监控方案。

Method: 提出了一套五阶段分析方法：1. 探针设计；2. 特征提取；3. 因果验证（激活修补与消融）；4. 稳健性测试（分布偏移）；5. 部署集成（基于成本的监控策略）。并在GPT-2 Small模型处理间接宾语识别（IOI）任务中进行了端到端验证。

Result: 1. 激活修补恢复了IOI电路；2. 稀疏自动编码器（SAE）提取了高选择性特征；3. 因果验证显示这些特征仅部分因果（消融15个特征后准确率仍达98%）；4. 证实了选择性比率与因果力量存在负相关（r = -0.56）；5. 部署评估表明，利用这些发现设计的监控逻辑可节省99.1%的潜在错误成本。

Conclusion: 本文提出的五阶段因果特征分析方法论能有效发现Transformer模型内部的因果电路。研究发现，虽然自动编码器能高效提取特征并解释极高比例的方差，但这些特征的“选择性”与“因果效应”之间存在反相关；此外，检测层面的稳健性并不等同于因果层面的稳健性，综合各阶段分析对于完整理解模型内部机理至关重要。

Abstract: We propose a five-stage methodology for causal feature analysis in transformer language models (probe design, feature extraction, causal validation, robustness testing, and deployment integration) and demonstrate it end-to-end on GPT-2 small performing the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. Activation patching recovers the canonical IOI circuit (layer-9 head 9 alone gives recovery +1.02). A sparse autoencoder recovers per-name selective features with effect sizes of 30 to 50 activation units. Causal validation finds these features specifically but only partially causal: ablating fifteen of them leaves the model accurate on 98% of prompts. Two NLA-inspired evaluations strengthen this picture: the fifteen selective features explain only 31% of activation variance versus the SAE's 99.7%, and selectivity ratio anticorrelates with causal force (r = -0.56). Robustness testing under three distribution shifts finds that the circuit transfers cleanly but feature ablation effects degrade substantially, exposing a gap between detection robustness and causal robustness. A cost-based deployment evaluation (assumed $50/FN, $0.42/FP, 2% error rate) finds an optimal monitor configuration yielding $8.96 per 1000 queries against a $1000 baseline, a 99.1% saving. Optimal composition strategy varies with cost ratio and base rate. The conjunction of stages produces findings no single stage would.

</details>

*Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Mahedi Hasan,Md. Tashin Parvez,Azizul Haque Noman,Md. Shafayet Hossain Ovi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多语言大模型在处理孟加拉语时缺乏文化语境感知的问题，本文提出了BLADE数据集及基准测试，通过LoRA微调有效提升了模型在孟加拉语语用生成上的准确性和文化契合度。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言大模型（MLLMs）在处理低资源语言（如孟加拉语）时，存在严重的“语用差距”，无法良好处理结构差异、区域习语以及复杂的敬语系统。

Method: 1. 构建BLADE数据集：包含4,196对经过人工精心标注的、涵盖复杂语用语境的孟加拉语交互数据；2. 模型微调：基于DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B架构；3. 技术手段：采用LoRA（参数高效微调）结合4位NF4量化技术进行训练与评估。

Result: 实验表明，使用BLADE微调后的模型在孟加拉语的“结构保真度”（structural fidelity）和“敬语对齐”（honorific alignment）方面表现出显著提升，在低资源语境下的语用生成能力显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 针对低资源语言（如孟加拉语）构建高质量、文化对齐的微调数据集，能有效缓解多语言大模型在语用习惯、区域习语和敬语一致性方面的局限性。BLADE数据集及其基准测试为提升多语言模型在社交语境下的表现提供了标准化方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multilingual Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced cross-lingual conversational capabilities, yet modeling culturally nuanced and context-dependent communication remains a critical bottleneck. Specifically, existing state-of-the-art models exhibit a severe pragmatic gap when handling structural variations, regional idioms, and honorific consistencies in low-resource contexts like Bangla. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, culturally aligned instruction-tuning dataset for \textbf{BangLa Application and DialoguE generation - BLADE} and benchmarking framework comprising $4,196$ meticulously curated interaction pairs. We leverage this resource to systematically fine-tune and evaluate leading open-weight architectures, including DeepSeek-8B and LLaMA-3.2-3B, utilizing parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA adapters in a 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) quantization framework. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our dataset yield substantial improvements in structural fidelity and honorific alignment, providing a rigorous benchmark for bridging pragmatic disparities in low-resource multilingual text generation. Code and dataset: https://github.com/ashuvo25/Bangla_Application_LLM/tree/main

</details>


### [45] [Scene Abstraction for Lexical Semantics: Structured Representations of Situated Meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22542)
*Yejin Cho,Katrin Erk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“场景抽象”框架，通过大模型构建词汇的结构化情境化语义表示，有效弥合了词汇在不同使用语境下的隐性语义与人类直觉感知之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算语义表示缺乏对词汇在不同语境下所唤起的隐性“情境维度”（如不同氛围、情感关联）的捕捉能力，导致语义理解不够全面。

Method: 提出了“场景抽象”（Scene Abstraction）框架，通过大语言模型（LLM）的少样本提示（few-shot prompting）来提取词汇的“情境场景”（事件、实体、环境）和“表达配置文件”（参与事件、可泛化属性、唤起情绪）；构建了 COCA-Scenes 数据集并进行了两项人类评估实验。

Result: 实验证明场景具有高度的一致性（准确率达 82.4%，比文本嵌入高 11.8 个百分点）；且生成的场景描述在三个语义维度上的人类偏好度达到 86.4%，优于基于 ATOMIC 的分析方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，Scene Abstraction 框架能有效捕捉词汇的情境化语义信息，其生成的场景配置文件在理解词义的细腻偏差上显著优于现有的文本嵌入方法及基于常识库（ATOMIC）的替代方案。

Abstract: Coffee and tea share many properties, yet they evoke strikingly different situations, atmospheres, and affective associations. These situated dimensions of word meaning are real and systematic, but they remain implicit in most computational representations of lexical meaning. We propose Scene Abstraction, a framework for constructing structured representations of the interpretive scenes that words participate in across usage contexts. Each scene consists of a Contextual Scene (Events, Entities, Setting) and an expression-centered Expression Profile (Engaged events, Generalizable properties, Evoked emotions), operationalized through few-shot prompting of a large language model. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) a structured representation framework for situated lexical meaning; (2) COCA-Scenes, a dataset of 520 usage instances across 26 keywords for distinct scene identification; and (3) empirical evidence from two experiments suggesting that scenes are reliably identifiable across human observers (82.4% accuracy, +11.8 pp over text-only embeddings) and that our scene profiles more closely align with human interpretation of words in context than ATOMIC-based alternatives (86.4% preference across three semantic dimensions).

</details>


### [46] [SynAE: A Framework for Measuring the Quality of Synthetic Data for Tool-Calling Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22564)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Aadyaa Maddi,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SynAE框架，用于定量评估用于测试工具调用智能体的合成数据集，通过多维度指标分析其对真实数据特性的还原能力与增强效果，以解决合成数据质量难以量化的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具调用智能体开发中，真实生产数据集往往存在隐私、稀缺或难以利用的问题，迫使开发者转而使用合成数据。然而，目前缺乏衡量合成数据与真实数据关系及其质量的有效工具。

Method: 提出了SynAE评估框架，通过四个维度（任务指令与中间回答、工具调用、最终输出、下游评估）来衡量合成数据集的有效性（Validity）、保真度（Fidelity）和多样性（Diversity）。

Result: SynAE能够成功检测到数据有效性、保真度和多样性方面的细微差异，并通过实验证明了多轴评估对于全面准确评估合成数据质量的必要性。

Conclusion: 目前没有单一指标足以完全表征合成数据质量，因此必须采用多维度的评估方法来衡量合成数据在智能体测试中的有效性。

Abstract: Today, tool-calling agents are commonly evaluated or tested on static datasets of execution traces, including input commands, agent responses, and associated tool calls. However, internal production datasets are often insufficient or unusable for testing; for example, they may contain sensitive or proprietary data, or they may be too sparse to support comprehensive testing (especially pre-deployment). In these settings, practitioners are increasingly replacing or augmenting real datasets with synthetic ones for evaluation purposes. A key challenge is quantifying the relation between these synthetic datasets and the real data. We introduce SynAE, an evaluation framework for assessing how well synthetic benchmarks for multi-turn, tool-calling agents replicate and augment the characteristics of real data trajectories. SynAE assesses the validity, fidelity, and diversity of synthetic data across four metric categories: (i) task instructions and intermediate responses, (ii) tool calls, (iii) final outputs, and (iv) downstream evaluation. We evaluate SynAE using recent agent benchmarks and test common synthetic data failure modes via realistic and controlled generation schemes. SynAE detects fine-grained variations in data validity, fidelity and diversity, and shows that no single metric is sufficient to fully characterize synthetic data quality, motivating a multi-axis evaluation of synthetic data for agent testing. A demo of SynAE is available at https://synae-2026-synae-demo.static.hf.space/index.html, with code at https://github.com/wsqwsq/SynAE.

</details>


### [47] [LANG: Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Reasoning with Language-Adaptive Hint Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22567)
*Yuchun Fan,Bei Li,Peiguang Li,Yilin Wang,Yongyu Mu,Jian Yang,Xin Chen,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大模型多语言推理中“推理能力与语言一致性”难以兼顾的问题，本文提出 LANG 框架，利用条件提示引导及动态衰减机制，在提升多语言推理表现的同时有效防止了语言漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在多语言多步推理中存在权衡困境：提升推理能力往往导致语言漂移（偏向英语），而强制语言一致性则会损害推理质量。现有方法无法兼顾两者。

Method: 提出了名为 LANG 的框架，核心包括：1. 语言条件提示（Language-conditioned hints）辅助探索；2. 提示衰减机制（Progressive decay schedule）以减少后期对提示的依赖；3. 语言自适应切换器（Language-adaptive switch）针对不同语言困难程度调整学习水平。

Result: 在多语言数学基准测试中，LANG 显著提升了推理性能且保持了高语言一致性；实验表明该框架具有良好的泛化能力，能增强模型层面的跨语言一致性。

Conclusion: LANG 框架通过语言条件提示与精细化的强化学习策略，成功地在保持跨语言一致性的前提下提升了多语言推理能力，并证明了其在数学任务之外的普适性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for enhancing multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet its benefits have not fully translated to multilingual contexts. Existing methods struggle with a fundamental trade-off: prioritizing input-language consistency severely hampers reasoning quality, while prioritizing reasoning often leads to unintended language drift toward English. We address this challenge with LANG, a novel framework that leverages language-conditioned hints to guide exploration in non-English reasoning tasks. Our method incorporates two key mechanisms to prevent dependency on these hints: a progressive decay schedule that gradually withdraws scaffolding, and a language-adaptive switch that tailors learning horizons to specific language difficulties. Empirical results on challenging multilingual mathematical benchmarks reveal that LANG substantially enhances reasoning performance without compromising language consistency. Moreover, we show that our framework generalizes beyond mathematics, fostering more consistent language alignment across model layers

</details>


### [48] [Agentic CLEAR: Automating Multi-Level Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22608)
*Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic CLEAR 是一款针对智能体（Agent）的自动评估框架，通过多粒度文本洞察及直观界面，解决了现有评估工具灵活性差、难以适配多样化领域的难题，并被证明具有高准确度和预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体自主性增强，现有的评估工具缺乏灵活性（依赖手动定义的错误分类），难以应对多样化的智能体环境与复杂行为，迫切需要一种通用且动态的评估方案。

Method: 提出 Agentic CLEAR 评估框架，建立在可观测层之上，通过自动化的数据驱动方式，在系统、跟踪和节点三个粒度层级上生成行为洞察，并提供直观的用户界面。

Result: 在四个基准测试、七个智能体设置及数万次 LLM 调用实验中表现出色，生成的评估反馈具有高质量和数据驱动的特性，与人工标注的错误高度对齐，且能有效预测任务成功率。

Conclusion: Agentic CLEAR 成功构建了一个自动、动态且易于使用的智能体评估框架，通过提供多层级（系统、跟踪、节点）文本洞察，有效解决了当前评估工具在灵活性和深度上的不足，且与人类评估高度一致。

Abstract: Agentic systems are becoming more capable: agents define strategies, take actions, and interact with different environments. This autonomy poses serious challenges for overseeing and assessing agent behavior. Most current tools are limited, focusing on observability with basic evaluation capabilities or imposing static, hand-crafted error taxonomies that cannot adapt to new domains. To address this gap, we present Agentic CLEAR, an automatic, dynamic, and easy-to-use evaluation framework. It produces textual insights into the agent behavior on three levels of granularity: system, trace, and node. Agentic CLEAR operates above the observability layer, enabling seamless integration and featuring an intuitive UI that makes agent evaluation highly accessible. In our experiments on four benchmarks, seven agentic settings, and tens of thousands of LLM calls, we show that Agentic CLEAR produces high-quality, data-driven, insightful feedback. Our analysis shows strong alignment with human-annotated errors and the ability to predict task success rate.

</details>


### [49] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究构建了一个大规模中文感官运动规范数据库，验证了具身属性对词汇加工的预测效力，并证明了纯语言表征中实质上编码了关于感官体验的具身知识，为具身智能研究提供了重要支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补非印欧语系（特别是中文）在具身认知研究中大规模感官运动规范化资源的空白，并探讨机器系统是否能在缺乏直接感官输入的情况下，仅通过语言分布数据习得具身知识。

Method: 1. 构建包含3,000个中文词汇的11维感官运动及具身属性评分数据库；2. 通过词汇判断任务（Lexical Decision Task）验证PSE（感知具身强度）指标与复合变量的预测效力；3. 通过回归模型和表征相似性分析（RSA），评估纯语言表征（Distributional semantics）恢复感官运动信息的程度。

Result: 1. 该数据集具有高信度和跨规范有效性；2. PSE-Sensorimotor和Minkowski-3是预测词汇加工表现的最佳指标；3. 语言模型可以较高程度（平均Spearman r = .62）恢复感官运动评分，且视觉/听觉维度高于化学感官维度；4. 感官运动空间的几何结构可以被部分恢复（r = .540）。

Conclusion: 本文提供的3,000个中文概念的感官运动范式数据库为研究具身认知提供了关键资源。研究证实了感官运动信息对词汇加工的促进作用，并发现纯语言表征中蕴含了相当一部分具身信息。这支持了分布语义模型能够部分编码具身概念结构的观点，为具身智能研究提供了经验证据。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [50] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，政治文本中的价值检测并不单纯依赖于模型规模或上下文长短。通过对比实验发现，检索式道德辅助比单纯增加上下文更有效，且应针对特定价值采取联合优化策略，而非依赖模型参数暴力扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治文本中Schwartz价值（Schwartz values）的检测具有挑战性，常依赖隐性线索和细微的价值区分。现有研究尚不明确上下文和显性道德知识在何种条件下能有效辅助句级价值检测。

Method: 采用ValuesML/Touché ValueEval评测框架，对比了不同上下文范围（句子、窗口、文档）、RAG（有无检索及不同融合策略）以及多种模型（DeBERTa-v3-base/large、12B-123B参数的零样本LLM）。

Result: 1. 增加上下文并不总是有效：全文档上下文提升了监督学习模型（DeBERTa）的性能，但对零样本LLM帮助不稳定；2. 检索道德知识在模型和上下文条件下具有一致的积极作用；3. 模型规模扩大（如参数增加或从base到large）并不保证性能提升；4. 早融合（early fusion）优于其他检索增强策略（晚融合、交叉注意力）。

Conclusion: 在价值敏感的自然语言处理（NLP）领域，上下文、外部知识及模型规模并非总是“越多越好”。研究强调应将它们联合评估，针对具体的价值类型和模型架构进行优化，而非盲目追求长输入或大模型。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>


### [51] [Seeing the Poem: Image-Semantic Detection of AI-Generated Modern Chinese Poetry with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22654)
*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现代汉语诗歌检测，创新性地提出了图像语义引导的多模态检测方法，有效解决了纯文本检测的局限，实现了优于RoBERTa及基准LLM的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究表明LLMs作为检测器效果不佳，且现有研究领域缺失对现代汉语诗歌的针对性探讨。

Method: 提出一种图像语义引导的诗歌检测方法（Image-semantic guided poetry detection），通过实例驱动（example-driven）方式，将反映诗歌内容的图像信息（意义、意象、情感）与文本信息相结合，形成互补判断。

Result: 所提方法在多个LLM生成的数据集上均表现优异，超越了基于纯文本的LLM检测器及传统RoBERTa检测器，Gemini模型的Macro-F1分数达到85.65%（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 引入图像语义引导的检测方法能有效提升大语言模型（LLM）对现代汉语诗歌的检测能力，证明了多模态辅助在诗歌鉴别中的重要性，其中Gemini模型表现达到SOTA水平。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [52] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究证明了利用LLM进行机器翻译是扩展非英语语言道德价值研究的一条切实可行的路径，能够有效弥补数据资源匮乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有文化依赖性和微妙性，难以准确翻译；现有道德价值观分类数据集主要局限于英语，导致其他语言的道德价值研究严重滞后。

Method: 构建了一个包含四种方法的验证管道：1. LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度分析；2. 中心核对齐（CKA）；3. LLM作为评估者（LLM-as-a-judge）；4. 深度学习分类器性能（AUC）对比测试。使用波兰语社交媒体数据集进行实证。

Result: 实验表明翻译后的数据平均余弦相似度达到0.86，跨语言AUC差距仅为0.01-0.02；微调语言模型后进一步缩小了性能差距，证明翻译数据足以用于机器学习任务。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是弥补道德价值研究中语言资源匮乏的实用且经济的手段，即使在处理俚语或文化特有表达时存在不足，翻译仍能有效保留道德线索，支持跨语言机器学习分析。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [53] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPD通过梯度引导的低秩映射技术，从LLM自身生成中精准解耦并提纯特定能力，无需外部反馈即可实现显著的任务表现和跨域泛化能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法要么依赖昂贵的外部信号（如执行反馈、奖励模型），要么缺乏挑选地使用所有原始输出，且这些方法未能解除任务能力与模型特有噪声（如风格、格式错误）的耦合。

Method: 提出“自策略蒸馏”（Self-Policy Distillation, SPD）：1. 通过模型在正确性定义token上的梯度提取出低秩能力子空间；2. 在自生成过程中将KV激活投影至该子空间；3. 对处理后的原始输出进行标准交叉熵损失微调。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多选题QA任务中，SPD相比最先进的无外部信号自蒸馏方法提升最高达13%，相比预训练基线提升最高16%；在跨域泛化设置下表现出15%的性能优势。

Conclusion: SPD是一种无需外部信号的高效自蒸馏框架，通过特征子空间投射实现了对LLM特定能力的精准提取与增强，在多个任务上优于现有的自蒸馏方法和预训练基线，且泛化能力极强。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [54] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST 是一种新的子词标记化方法，通过引入递归拆分树和基于整数规划的词汇表优化，在大词汇表场景下显著提高了压缩效率，并提升了下游语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的子词标记化方法（如 BPE、WordPiece）在词汇表优化和压缩比方面存在局限，ToaST 旨在通过直接优化压缩率，提高 Token 效率并提升模型性能。

Method: ToaST（Tokenization with Split Trees）：1. 使用字节 N-gram 计数将预标记（pretoken）递归拆分为二叉树；2. 采用递归推理程序，路径向下寻找首个在词汇表内的节点；3. 词汇表选择建模为整数规划（Integer Program）问题，通过线性规划松弛求解，能最小化总 Token 数。

Result: 在 40,960 及以上词汇表大小下，ToaST 比 BPE、WordPiece 和 UnigramLM 减少了超过 11% 的 Token 数量；在 1.5B 参数模型实验中，CORE 得分达到最高，在 22 项单项任务中 13 项表现最优。

Conclusion: ToaST 是一种高效的子词标记化方法，通过优化递归压缩实现，能在主流词汇表大小下显著降低 Token 数量，从而延长模型有效上下文长度，并在语言模型任务中展现出比 BPE 等主流方法更优的综合性能。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [55] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有医学知识图谱缺乏时间维度的问题，本文提出了 ChronoMedKG，这是一个包含时间属性的生物医学知识图谱及其评测集 ChronoTQA，显著增强了医疗大模型处理纵向临床推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物医学知识图谱（如 PrimeKG, Hetionet 等）将疾病关联视为静态事实，忽略了临床推理中至关重要的时间维度（如症状随年龄、病程阶段的演变），限制了其在长期临床推理及 RAG 系统中的应用。

Method: 构建了一个基于疾病自治的多智能体（multi-agent）管道，利用多个前沿大模型从 PubMed 和 PMC 文献中提取信息；通过多模型共识机制、可信度过滤及本体对齐确保数据质量；并研发了包含 3,341 个问题的 ChronoTQA 基准测试集。

Result: ChronoMedKG 包含 46 万条高质量时间关联三元组，覆盖 1.3 万余种疾病，与 Orphadata 的一致性达到 92.7%；在 ChronoTQA 测试中，该图谱能帮助大模型挽回 47-65% 的时间敏感型长尾失效案例，远超现有的 HPOA-RAG 方法。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG 通过引入时间维度，填补了现有生物医学知识图谱在纵向临床推理方面的空白。该图谱显著提升了 LLM 在处理时间敏感型临床问题时的准确性，为检索增强生成（RAG）系统提供了核心的时间轴支持。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [56] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，改变大模型的预训练数据顺序（按时间顺序排序而非乱序），能在保持通用能力的同时，显著提升模型获取时效性事实的能力和时间精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型通常基于乱序语料库进行训练，导致模型知识在训练结束后即“冻结”，且模型对时间维度的认知（Temporal Grounding）和对随时间变化的事实掌握能力较弱。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含7,000多个时间敏感事实的问题基准测试（Benchmark）及其评估协议；2. 在6B参数模型上，对比“按时间顺序排列的Common Crawl快照库”与“标准乱序语料”两种预训练方案，分析其性能差异。

Result: 按时间顺序训练的模型在保持与乱序模型相当的通用语言理解能力基础上，表现出更强的事实新鲜度（Factual Freshness）和时间精确度；相比之下，乱序训练模型在处理较旧数据时表现更好，这可能与乱序训练中事实的重复出现频率更高有关。

Conclusion: 按时间顺序对预训练语料进行排序是提升大模型事实知识实效性与时间精确度的有效手段。该研究证明了在不牺牲通用语言能力的前提下，可以通过优化训练数据的动态顺序来增强模型对时效性知识的获取，为大模型的持续学习（Continual Learning）奠定了研究基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [57] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在政治议题中存在的“隐蔽偏见”，提出了量化评估指标及名为“政治一致性训练”（PCT）的强化学习方案，成功在保持模型性能的同时显著缓解了政治立场不对称问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在敏感政治议题中应用增加，研究发现模型在处理对立观点时表现出系统性的不对称性（即隐蔽政治偏见），缺乏有效的量化评估和校准方法。

Method: 1. 定义了“隐蔽政治偏见”（Covert Political Bias）及其7种运作机制；2. 提出了量化评估框架：“情感一致性”（Sentiment Consistency）和“有用性一致性”（Helpfulness Consistency）；3. 设计了“政治一致性训练”（PCT）方法，结合强化学习，通过情感和有用性一致性训练减轻偏见。

Result: 实验证明，PCT方法能显著降低大语言模型的隐蔽政治偏见，同时保持模型的整体有用性，且在未训练的基准测试中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究认为大语言模型的政治偏见不仅存在于显性表达，更存在于隐蔽的修辞和框架选择中。通过引入PCT方法，可以在不牺牲模型有用性的前提下有效减轻这种隐蔽偏见，为构建更中立、客观的AI系统提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [58] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了6款AI聊天模型处理全球突发新闻的能力，发现模型虽然在事实提取上表现良好，但严重依赖检索基础设施，且对非英语语种存在偏见，在处理含错误假设的复杂查询时极其脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏针对AI系统（集成搜索与检索-合成流水线）处理不同语言与地区突发事实准确性的系统性评估，亟需验证其在处理实时全球新闻时的表现及潜在缺陷。

Method: 通过为期14天的实证评估，对比了6款AI聊天模型（Gemini、Grok、Claude、GPT系列）在处理来自BBC新闻6个区域服务（英语、阿拉伯语、法语、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）的2100个实时事实性问题时的表现，并采用多项选择与自由回答两种模式进行测试，同时分析了模型在面对错误诱导问题时的响应。

Result: 1. 顶级模型的多项选择准确率超90%，但自由回答准确率下降16-17%；2. 存在显著语言偏见，印地语表现最差；3. 超过70%的错误源于检索偏差（Retrieval failure）而非推理失败；4. 模型在应对含误导假设的问题时表现极差，且识别误导信息的能力与回答准确性并不挂钩。

Conclusion: 尽管AI聊天机器人在突发新闻信息检索方面表现出极高的准确率，但仍存在系统性的语言/地区偏见（尤其是非英语语种表现较差）、过度依赖检索质量而非推理能力，以及在面对诱导性错误假设问题时极其脆弱，这表明当前模型在处理真实世界复杂信息时仍存在显著局限性。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [59] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 ConvexTok 的新型词条化算法，通过线性规划和凸优化而非传统的贪心策略构建词表，显著提升了语言模型的压缩效率与词表质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的词条化算法（如 BPE、Unigram）本质上是贪心算法，仅作局部决策，无法从全局角度构建最优词表空间。

Method: 将词条化构建过程建模为一个线性规划（Linear Program）问题，并利用凸优化工具进行求解。这一方法克服了传统 BPE 和 Unigram 等贪心算法局部最优的局限，从全局视角优化词表。

Result: ConvexTok 在内在词条化指标和语言模型 Bits-per-byte (BpB) 指标上均有提升；在下游任务中表现有所优化且效果更稳定；并能在常见词表规模下将偏离最优解的程度控制在 1% 以内。

Conclusion: ConvexTok 提供了一种基于凸优化的词条化（Tokenization）方法，证明了通过线性规划构建词表能有效提升语言模型在压缩率（BpB）和部分下游任务上的表现，并具备评估当前方案距离理论最优程度的可控性。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>

### [60] [Bridging the Cold-Start Gap: LLM-Powered Synthetic Data Generation for Natural Language Search at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21812)
*Wendy Ran Wei,Hao Li,Weiwei Guo,Xiaowei Liu,Xueyin Chen,Dillon Davis,Malay Haldar,Soumyadip Banerjee,Kedar Bellare,Huiji Gao,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Airbnb利用LLM合成高质量查询与标签，克服了搜索系统的冷启动瓶颈，实现了在缺乏真实数据情况下的高效模型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决搜索系统在冷启动阶段缺乏真实用户查询和关联标注数据的痛点，以支持模型训练和性能评估。

Method: 提出一套基于LLM的合成数据框架：1. 查询生成：结合预订会话中的对比列表对与用户研究种子查询，平衡真实性与多样性；2. 标签生成：采用对比生成（Contrastive Generation）获取主题标签，并引入虚拟裁判（Virtual Judge）实现更广泛的覆盖。

Result: 性能显著优于InPars基线：在查询长度上，KL散度从12.03降低至0.66（7.5倍提升）；在属性分布上，KL散度仅为0.04（优于基准种子查询）；测试集比基线更具挑战性（判别准确率从97%下降至79%），显著提升了评价指标的判别力。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于LLM的合成查询与标签生成框架，Airbnb成功解决了自然语言搜索系统的冷启动问题。该方法生成的合成数据在分布特征上与真实用户数据高度一致，且具备更强的判别力，能够有效支撑检索与排序模型的训练及评估。

Abstract: Deploying natural language search systems presents a critical cold-start challenge: no real user queries to learn linguistic patterns, and no relevance labels to train ranking models. We present a framework for generating synthetic queries and labels using large language models (LLMs), powering model training and evaluation for Airbnb's natural language search.
  For query generation, we combine contrastive listing pairs from booking sessions with seed queries from user research to balance realism and diversity, enabling a cold-to-warm start transition as real user data becomes available. For label generation, we introduce contrastive generation that produces topicality labels by construction, and Virtual Judge (VJ) labeling for broader coverage.
  We compare our approach against a no-seed contrastive baseline and an InPars-style baseline. For query length, the InPars baseline produces verbose queries with KL divergence of 12.03 vs. real users; our seed-guided approach achieves 0.66, a 7.5x improvement. For attribute type distributions, our approach achieves the lowest KL divergence (0.04), outperforming even seed queries (0.09). Experiments show our approach produces harder evaluation examples than the no-seed baseline (79% vs. 97% pairwise accuracy), providing discriminative signal for model improvement. We deploy production pipelines generating synthetic examples daily for embedding-based retrieval and ranking evaluation.

</details>


### [61] [Reinforced Preference Optimization for Reasoning-Augmented Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21967)
*Jingtong Gao,Zeyu Song,Chi Lu,Xiaopeng Li,Derong Xu,Maolin Wang,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RPORec框架通过引入推理增强建模与强化学习对齐两阶段机制，有效解决了LLM应用于推荐系统时的推理与个性化目标适配难题，实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有基于推理的推荐系统在集成过程中存在的结构冲突，以及大模型生成的自由文本难以转化为精准物品预测的对齐难题。

Method: 提出了RPORec框架，包含两个阶段：1. 推理增强建模：利用高质量思维链（CoT）指导推荐头（Rechead）学习表示；2. 高级推理优化与对齐：利用Rechead产出的可验证奖励（Verifiable Rewards）通过强化学习微调LLM，提升推理质量与任务相关性。

Result: RPORec在公开数据集上表现优异，并在大规模在线部署中表现出强劲的实际应用效果，提升了推理的一致性、准确性及推荐的可解释性。

Conclusion: RPORec通过强化学习实现了LLM推理能力与推荐目标的高度对齐，在学术基准测试和大规模线上部署中均显著优于现有SOTA方法，验证了推理增强推荐在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Recommender systems are critical for delivering personalized content across digital platforms, and recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to enhance them with richer world knowledge and explicit reasoning capabilities. With the help of reasoning knowledge, recommendations can better infer users' underlying intents, adapt to evolving preferences, and leverage semantic relationships for improved accuracy and interpretability. However, existing reasoning-based recommendation methods often fail to fully align the LLM's reasoning process with recommendation-specific objectives due to structural disruption during integration and difficulties in translating free-form generation into accurate item predictions. In this paper, we introduce RPORec, a reinforced preference optimization framework that unifies an LLM backbone's reasoning ability with a dedicated recommendation head (Rechead) for precise item retrieval. RPORec comprises two stages: (1) Reasoning-Augmented Recommendation Modeling, where high-quality Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning is generated and used as auxiliary knowledge to guide the Rechead in learning recommendation-specific representations; and (2) Advanced Reasoning Refinement and Alignment, in which the trained Rechead produces verifiable rewards to fine-tune the LLM backbone via reinforcement learning, enhancing reasoning quality, structural consistency, and task relevance. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and large-scale online deployments show that RPORec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based recommendation methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of reasoning-augmented recommendation modeling in real-world systems.

</details>


### [62] [LLM Retrieval for Stable and Predictable Ad Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21969)
*Vinodh Kumar Sunkara,Satheeshkumar Karuppusamy,Hangjun Xu,Sai Deepika Regani,Kshitij Gupta,Gaby Nahum,Sneha Iyer,Jean-Baptiste Fiot,Yinglong Guo,Xiaowen Guo,Atul Jangra,Yucheng Liu,Jinghao Yan,Vijay Pappu,Benjamin Schulte,Deepak Chandra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对广告推荐系统预测不稳定的问题，本文提出了一种基于微调LLM的语义候选生成框架，通过语义增强确保广告投放的可预测性与一致性，在工业级测试中实现了性能与稳定性的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广告推荐系统过于关注点击率（CTR）或转化率（CVR）的准确性，忽视了预测的稳定性和可预测性。在生成式AI环境下，缺乏稳定性会导致广告投放的不可重复性、冷启动问题及探索不足，影响广告主体验。

Method: 提出了一种衡量广告推荐系统稳定性和可预测性的评估框架；设计了一种基于微调LLM的语义候选生成框架，通过提取广告创意的分层语义属性，利用图检索技术进行扩展，确保语义变体的一致性。

Result: 在工业级大规模广告推荐系统中进行了实验，在线及离线A/B测试显示，该方法在显著提升预测稳定性和可预测性的同时，也改善了传统的指标（如召回率和NDCG）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了引入大语言模型（LLM）的语义属性提取方法可以有效提升广告推荐系统的预测稳定性和预测能力，同时兼顾传统的推荐性能指标。该框架具有通用性，可推广至其他大规模检索和推荐系统。

Abstract: Traditional ads recommendation systems have primarily focused on optimizing for prediction accuracy of click or conversion events using canonical metrics such as recall or normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG). With the hyper-growth of ads inventory and liquidity with generative AI technologies, the prediction stability and predictability is becoming increasingly critical. Intuitively, prediction stability and predictability can be defined to quantify system robustness with respect to minor/noisy input (ads, creatives) perturbations, the lack of which could lead to advertiser perceivable problems such as repeatability, cold start and under-exploration. In this paper, we introduce a new evaluation framework for quantifying stability and predictability of an ads recommender system, and present an online validated semantic candidate generation framework powered by fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) that showed significant improvement along these metrics by fundamentally improving the semantic-awareness of the system. The approach extracts hierarchical semantic attributes from ad creatives to obtain LLM representations, which serve as the foundation for graph-based expansion, ensuring the retrieved candidates encapsulate semantic variants of an ad, guaranteeing that small creative variants from the advertiser yield consistent and explainable delivery results to the user. We tested this LLM ads retrieval framework in a large-scale industrial ads recommendation system, demonstrating significant improvements across offline and online A/B experiments, showcasing gains in both predictability and traditional performance metrics. Although evaluated in the ads stack, this is a general framework that can be applied broadly to any large-scale recommendation and retrieval systems facing similar scaling and predictability challenges.

</details>


### [63] [Generative Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21987)
*Sixiao Zhang,Mingrui Liu,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有对话推荐系统推荐与对话脱节的问题，本文提出了一种将两者统一在单一自回归框架下的生成式架构，实现了端到端的联合优化，显著提升了推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话推荐系统通常将“推荐”与“对话生成”模块分离，或依赖基于检索的流水线，导致两者融合度低，难以准确捕获用户意图，从而产生次优的建模效果。

Method: 提出了一种完全生成式的框架，将商品表示为离散的语义ID，并将其嵌入到自回归生成过程中。同时，引入了一种结构化生成范式，将对话推荐拆解为“意图预测-推荐目标选择-响应生成”的序列决策过程，并配合受限解码技术以确保推荐的准确性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在推荐性能上表现优异，Recall@1指标较强基线模型提升高达29%，同时保持了良好的对话质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的端到端生成式对话推荐框架，通过将推荐与对话生成整合为统一的自回归建模任务，有效解决了传统方法中两者脱节的问题，在保持高质量对话的同时显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems aim to provide personalized recommendations via natural language interactions. However, existing approaches either decouple recommendation from dialog generation or rely on retrieval-based pipelines, limiting the integration between recommendation and response generation and leading to suboptimal modeling of user intent. In this paper, we propose a fully generative conversational recommender system that unifies recommendation and dialog generation within a single autoregressive framework. Our approach represents items as discrete semantic IDs and integrates them directly into the generation process, enabling joint prediction of items and responses via next-token modeling. We further introduce a structured generation paradigm that factorizes conversational recommendation into a sequence of interdependent decisions, where the model first predicts the response intent and the recommendation target, and then generates the response conditioned on them. This design enables end-to-end optimization, enforces a more coherent dependency structure, and supports faithful item generation via constrained decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves recommendation performance, achieving gains of up to 29% on Recall@1 over strong baselines, while maintaining competitive dialog quality.

</details>


### [64] [Integrating Chain-of-Thought into Generative Retrieval: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22358)
*Wenhao Zhang,Ruihao Yu,Yi Bai,Zhumin Chen,Pengjie Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ThinkGR 是首个为生成式检索引入思维链（CoT）的框架，通过混合解码和两阶段训练，赋予模型显式的复杂查询推理能力，显著提升了多跳检索的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式检索（GR）模型直接将查询映射为文档标识符，缺乏中间推理环节，难以应对需要多步推理的复杂检索查询。

Method: 1. 提出 ThinkGR 框架，交替执行思维链推理与文档标识符（docid）生成；
2. 设计混合解码策略，实现自由推理与受限目标检索的动态切换；
3. 采用两阶段训练方法：首先通过监督微调对齐思维-检索模式，随后利用基于检索结果的强化学习优化推理质量。

Result: 在四个多跳检索基准测试中，ThinkGR 表现优异，平均性能提升 6.86%，达到当前最优（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: ThinkGR 通过在生成式检索流程中引入思维链（CoT），有效地提升了处理复杂多跳查询的能力，为生成式检索任务提供了一种具备显式推理能力的强效范式。

Abstract: While generative retrieval (GR) demonstrates competitive performance on standard retrieval benchmarks, existing approaches directly map queries to document identifiers (docids) without intermediate deliberation, limiting their effectiveness for complex queries that require multi-step reasoning. As a preliminary study on integrating chain-of-thought (CoT) into generative retrieval, we introduce ThinkGR, a unified framework that interleaves CoT with docid generation, enabling iterative thinking and retrieval within a single generative process. To bridge the gap between free-form thought generation and structured retrieval targets, we design (1) a hybrid decoding strategy that dynamically switches between unconstrained thought generation and constrained docid decoding, and (2) a two-phase training approach that first aligns thought-retrieval patterns through supervised fine-tuning, then optimizes thought quality via retrieval-grounded reinforcement learning. Experiments on four multi-hop retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkGR achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average improvement of +6.86\%. Our work opens new avenues for enhancing generative retrieval with explicit deliberation capabilities, with promising implications for retrieval tasks requiring complex reasoning.

</details>


### [65] [Diversed Model Discovery via Structured Table Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22766)
*Zhengyuan Dong,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有模型搜索依赖文本导致结果单一的问题，本文提出了 StructuredSemanticSearch，利用模型卡中的结构化表格数据和基于“金块”的评估协议，提高了模型检索的准确性、覆盖率与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型搜索系统过于依赖文本语义相似度，导致结果同质化；本文认为模型搜索本质上是比较性的，需要基于模型卡中简洁、高质量的结构化证据（如性能表、配置表）来辅助决策。

Method: 提出 StructuredSemanticSearch 框架，利用 table discovery 算子（如 unionability, joinability）发现并整合模型卡中的结构化表格数据，结合语义基线进行综合检索；同时引入一个基于“金块（nugget）”的评估协议来衡量证据覆盖率和多样性。

Result: 在 597 个模型推荐查询上的实验表明，StructuredSemanticSearch 的结构感知检索管道相比纯语义基线提供了更好的“金块（nugget）”覆盖率。

Conclusion: StructuredSemanticSearch 框架通过结合结构化表格数据和语义搜索，能显著提高模型检索的覆盖率与多样性，证明了利用模型卡中的高价值结构化证据对于实现有效的比较式模型搜索至关重要。

Abstract: Model cards describe model behavior through a mixture of textual descriptions and structured artifacts, including performance, configuration, and dataset tables. Existing model search systems rely predominantly on semantic similarity over text, which can produce homogeneous result sets and limit exploration of alternatives. We argue that model search is inherently comparative: users want models that are task-aligned yet differentiated in measurable ways. We hypothesize that this balance requires retrieval over condensed, high-quality evidence rather than verbose descriptions, and much of that evidence is concentrated in structured tables. We present StructuredSemanticSearch, a table-driven model search framework built on the ModelTables benchmark. Given a query, StructuredSemanticSearch combines a semantic baseline for task alignment with a structure-aware pipeline that discovers query-related model-card tables using table discovery operators such as unionability, joinability, and keyword search. Retrieved tables are mapped back to model cards under a controlled top-k budget, enabling fair comparison between text-based and table-based retrieval. Beyond retrieval, StructuredSemanticSearch adapts table integration to the model-table domain through orientation-aware integration, producing compact integrated views of tables from partially overlapping and sometimes transposed evidence tables. For evaluation, we introduce a nugget-based, auditable protocol that extracts compact evidence items from model cards, matches queries to condition- or intent-specific nuggets, and measures evidence coverage and diversity over retrieved model-card candidate sets. This protocol also provides a scalable path toward approximate, evidence-based labeling in dynamic model lakes. Experiments on 597 model-recommendation queries show improved nugget coverage for the structure-aware pipeline than semantic baseline

</details>

### [66] [Near-Optimal Generalized Private Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21601)
*Anamay Chaturvedi,Monika Henzinger,Jalaj Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的“广义阈值机制”（GTM），解决了差分隐私框架下的广义私有测试难题，实现了近乎最优的准确性与样本效率，并通过该机制建立了连续观测设置到批处理设置的黑盒规约，支持动态阈值调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差异化隐私私有测试（如稀疏向量技术）不足以处理更复杂的广义私有测试问题，特别是在需要处理不确定序列或需要动态调整测试标准（如超参数优化）的场景下缺乏通用的解决框架。

Method: 引入了广义阈值机制（GTM），该机制适用于任何序列化的（ε_t, δ_t）-DP机制，能够保持纯ε-DP特性。通过设计特定的拒绝阈值函数和分析样本复杂度上限，证明了其机制的有效性，并提供了相应的下界验证。

Result: GTM机制在实现差分隐私（纯ε-DP）的同时，提供了基于概率置信度约束的准确性保证，并给出了关于测试序列次数的近似最优上界。通过GTM，作者成功实现了从连续观测设置到批处理设置的差分隐私优化黑盒规约，首次为多种最大化问题提供了DP-CO算法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的广义阈值机制（GTM）在广义私有测试问题中实现了近乎最优的准确性和样本复杂度，并成功解决了个性化超参数优化中的动态阈值适应性问题，为差异化隐私下的连续观测设置提供了有效的通用还原方法。

Abstract: In differential privacy (DP), the generalized private testing problem was introduced by Liu and Talwar (STOC 2019). Given a dataset $X \in \mathcal{X}$ and a sequence of black-box $\varepsilon_t$-DP mechanisms $M_t:\mathcal{X}\to\{+1,-1\}$, the analyst must accept the first mechanism whose success probability $p_t=\Pr[M_t(X)=+1]$ exceeds a given threshold $p^*\in(0,1)$, while achieving DP. Accuracy is measured by the gap between $p^*$ and a rejection threshold $\bar{p}$, such that with probability $1-β$ for all $t\geq1$, if $p_t\leq\bar{p}$, then $M_t$ is rejected, and if $p_t\geq p^*$, then it is accepted. This generalizes the standard private testing problem, whose solution, the Sparse Vector Technique, is ubiquitous in DP.
  We introduce the Generalized Thresholding Mechanism (GTM) for generalized private testing. For $\varepsilon>0$ and any sequence of $(\varepsilon_t,δ_t)$-DP mechanisms $M_t$, the GTM is pure $\varepsilon$-DP. For $θ>0$, $γ\in(1,2]$, and $β\in(0,1)$, $\bar{p}_t=\max(p^*/γΛ_t, 1 - γΛ_t(1-p^*))-δ_t/\varepsilon_t$ for $Λ_t=(5t\ln^3(t+2))^{(2+θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}(4/β)^{(3+θ+2/θ)\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon}$. With probability $1-β$, the number of evaluations of $M_t$ is at most $O((\ln(t/β)/(γ-1)^2)\max(Λ_t/p^*,(1-p^*)^{-1}))$ for all $t\geq 1$. Our lower bounds prove near-optimality of our accuracy and sample complexity guarantees.
  Via the GTM, we give a black-box reduction for DP optimization from the continual observation (CO) setting to the batch setting. This gives us the first DP-CO algorithms for many maximization problems. Further, the GTM permits an adaptive choice of acceptance thresholds $(p^*_t)_{t\geq1}$, addressing a challenge mentioned in prior work on using generalized private testing for hyperparameter optimization (Papernot and Steinke (ICLR 2022)).

</details>


### [67] [Finding a Solution to the Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv Theorem in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21753)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理子序列的确定性线性时间算法，通过高效处理素数模下的子集和问题并结合乘法归约，提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理的现有算法在计算复杂度上仍有改进空间（此前最优为 O(n log log log n)），旨在寻找一种达到理论下界的确定性线性时间算法。

Method: 提出了一种用于素数模下的“目标子集和问题”的线性时间算法。该算法利用紧凑的算术级数表示可达和，通过合并相交级数并利用 Frobenius 区间进行扩展，在素数情形下求出目标和，随后通过标准的乘法归约技术将其扩展至任意模数 n。

Result: 实现了对Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理中目标子序列寻找问题的线性时间算法突破，优于之前所有已知的确定性算法。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种计算Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv定理中子序列的线性时间算法，通过将问题简化为素数模下的子集和问题并利用算术级数的表示方法，显著优化了该问题的计算复杂度。

Abstract: The Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv theorem states that every sequence of 2n - 1 integers contains a subsequence of length n whose sum is divisible by n. Choi, Kang, and Lim gave a simple deterministic O(n log n) algorithm for finding such a subsequence, and Leung recently improved this to O(n log log log n). We give a deterministic linear-time algorithm. The core is a linear-time algorithm for the following prime target subset-sum problem: given p - 1 nonzero residues in Z_p and a target residue, find a subset with the prescribed sum. Our algorithm maintains a compact arithmetic-progression representation of reachable sums. When two progressions intersect, a bounded Frobenius interval in their sum allows them to be merged into one longer progression, with enough growth to pay for the update. When the representation either contains a full progression or covers all nonzero residues, the target residue is recovered constructively. The standard multiplicative reduction then extends the prime algorithm to arbitrary moduli.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the classic cake cutting problem in the Robertson-Webb model, with the objective of proportional fairness. We show that any randomized algorithm must use $Ω(n \log n)$ queries.

</details>


### [69] [Optimal $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$-Approximation of the Permanent of Positive Semidefinite Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21946)
*Nima Anari,Farzam Ebrahimnejad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了埃尔米特半正定矩阵永久式计算的近似复杂度问题，证明了确定性多项式时间内可达到的最优近似比为 $e^{(\gamma+o(1))n}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找埃尔米特半正定矩阵永久式计算在多项式时间内的最佳确定性近似界限，以解决计算复杂度理论中的这一公开问题。

Method: 利用熵论证方法（entropy argument）结合 Wick 积分公式（Wick integral formula），证明了永久式的具体夹逼定理 $e^{-\gamma n}\widehat P(A)\le \operatorname{per}(A)\le \widehat P(A)$，并利用凹规划求解 $\widehat P(A)$。

Result: 提出了一个确定性的多项式时间近似算法，其近似比为 $e^{(\gamma+\varepsilon)n}$，并与已知的 $e^{(\gamma-\varepsilon)n}$ 硬度结论结合，确立了该问题的最优近似界。

Conclusion: 本文确定了埃尔米特半正定矩阵计算永久式的最优确定性多项式时间近似比为 $e^{(\gamma+o(1))n}$（其中 $\gamma$ 为欧拉-马斯刻若尼常数），并在假设 $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$ 的前提下证明了该界限的紧致性。

Abstract: We determine, up to lower-order terms in the exponent, the best possible deterministic polynomial-time approximation ratio for the permanent of a Hermitian positive semidefinite matrix. If $A\succeq 0$ has no zero diagonal entry, $d=\operatorname{rank}(A)$, $A=VV^\dagger$ with $V\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times d}$ full column rank, and $v_1,\ldots,v_n$ are the rows of $V$, define \[
  Φ(V)=\max_{X\succ 0}
  \left\{\sum_{i=1}^n \log(v_i^\dagger Xv_i)+\log\det X-\operatorname{tr} X+d\right\},
  \qquad
  \widehat P(A)=e^{Φ(V)}. \] We prove the exact sandwich \[
  e^{-γn}\widehat P(A)\le \operatorname{per}(A)\le \widehat P(A). \] Here $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant. Since the maximization is concave, this gives a deterministic polynomial-time $e^{(γ+\varepsilon)n}$-approximation for every $\varepsilon>0$. Combined with the previous $e^{(γ-\varepsilon)n}$-hardness of approximation for positive semidefinite permanents, this resolves the optimal exponential approximation ratio for deterministic polynomial-time algorithms as $e^{(γ+o(1))n}$, assuming $\mathrm{P}\ne\mathrm{NP}$. The proof is an entropy argument applied to the standard Wick integral formula for $\operatorname{per}(A)$; the loss is exactly $γ$ per factor because $\mathbb{E}[\log T]=-γ$ for $T\sim\operatorname{Exp}(1)$.
  The result was obtained through interactions with GPT 5.5 Pro Extended: the first author's interaction was one-shot, while the second author's was a separate multi-turn interaction with high-level guidance. Both authors verified the theorem and proof. Codex was used to assemble and typeset the manuscript.

</details>


### [70] [A Coalgebraic Dijkstra Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22149)
*Takahiro Sanada,Yoàv Montacute,Kittiphon Phalakarn,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种余代数最短路径问题（CSPP）框架，并针对该框架开发了余代数Dijkstra算法，实现了多种最优化问题的统一处理，并明确了算法高效运行所需的必要充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Dijkstra算法变体众多，缺乏统一的理论框架来描述这些算法在状态转换系统上的最优化性质，且对其加速效果的适用条件研究不足。

Method: 引入余代数最短路径问题（CSPP）作为统一框架，并在此框架下设计了一种余代数Dijkstra算法。

Result: 通过CSPP框架，成功统一了最短路径、最宽路径、双人博弈及最短二叉树等问题；证明了该算法的正确性条件，并在保证渐进复杂度与经典Dijkstra算法相当的前提下提供了通用解法。

Conclusion: 通过提出余代数Dijkstra算法，作者建立了Dijkstra风格加速的必要充分条件，为解决广泛的最优化问题提供了理论基础和高效算法支撑。

Abstract: The Dijkstra algorithm is a classical method for solving the shortest path problem on weighted graphs. There are several variations of the Dijkstra algorithm, including algorithms for the widest path problem and for two-player games. In this paper, we introduce the coalgebraic shortest path problem (CSPP), a unifying framework for a broad class of optimization problems on state-transition systems. This framework encompasses not only the aforementioned problems but also new ones such as the shortest binary tree problem. We further present a coalgebraic Dijkstra algorithm for solving the CSPP efficiently under a suitable condition. Our condition is necessary and sufficient for the algorithm to return correct solutions, thereby providing a precise criterion for when Dijkstra-style acceleration is possible. We also show that the proposed algorithm achieves asymptotic complexity comparable to that of the classical Dijkstra algorithm.

</details>


### [71] [The Secretary Problem with a Stochastic Precursor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22653)
*Franziska Eberle,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的在线算法增强范式：仅利用预测信号的“到达时间”而非其具体内容来优化策略。研究表明，异步的时间前兆能在随机和对抗性顺序下显著提升秘书问题的获胜概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的学习增强算法通常依赖于预测信号提供的具体内容（如值估计、解的建议等）。本研究旨在探索单纯的“时间信息”（即信号何时到达）是否也能作为有效的辅助信息，从而摆脱对预测内容准确性的依赖。

Method: 采用理论分析框架，研究了带有“随机前兆”（stochastic precursor）的秘书问题。该前兆被定义为一个不含具体内容、但在最优项之前到达的信号。作者分别在“随机到达顺序”（random-order）和“对抗性到达顺序”（adversarial-order）模型下推导并刻画了最优策略。

Result: 1. 在随机到达顺序下，仅需一个均匀分布的时间信号，成功概率至少达到 1/2（超过了经典的 1/e 基准），随着信号延迟，成功概率趋于 1。2. 在对抗性到达顺序下，通过足够集中的时间信号，可以实现常数级的成功保证（而在传统模型中通常难以实现）。

Conclusion: 异步的时间信息在在线决策中是一种独特且强大的建议形式。该研究证明了即使不包含具体信息内容的预测信号，仅凭其到达时间也能显著改善秘书问题的竞争比，为提升在线算法性能提供了新的维度。

Abstract: In learning-augmented online algorithms, predictions are usually valued for what they say: a value estimate, a solution, or an algorithmic recommendation. This paper shows that predictions can also be valuable solely due to their arrival time. We study the fundamental secretary problem augmented with a stochastic precursor: a content-free signal that is guaranteed to arrive no later than the best item, but is otherwise stochastically timed. The signal does not carry any additional information; nevertheless, its timing alone changes the structure of optimal stopping. We characterize optimal policies in the random-order and adversarial-order models. In random order, a single uniformly timed precursor already gives success probability at least $\frac12$, improving on the classic $\frac1e$ benchmark. With increasingly late precursors, the success probability approaches $1$. In adversarial order, for which traditional models do not admit strong guarantees, sufficiently concentrated precursors recover constant success guarantees. Our results show that such novel forms of asynchronous temporal information are a distinct and powerful form of advice in online decision making and may also be effective for other problems.

</details>


### [72] [On the Parameterized Complexity of Min-Sum-Radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22804)
*Pankaj Kumar,Haiko Müller,Sebastian Ordyniak,Melanie Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Min-Sum-Radii (MSR) 聚类问题在图度量空间下的参数化复杂度，证明了其在多种参数组合下具有 W[1]-hard 性，并给出了针对 treewidth + Delta 的 FPT 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSR 问题已知在一般度量空间下是 NP-hard 的，本文旨在探讨其在图度量空间下的参数化复杂度，特别是针对不同参数（如 k、Delta、treewidth、顶点覆盖数等）的计算难易性。

Method: 通过参数化复杂度分析（Parameterized Complexity），利用归约方法证明 W[1]-hardness，并结合已知算法框架证明特定参数组合下的 FPT 性。

Result: 1. 加权二分图上，MSR 针对 k + Delta 是 W[1]-hard 的；2. 加权二分图上，MSR 针对顶点覆盖数 + k 是 W[1]-hard 的；3. 在团和完全二分图上，MSR 针对 k + Delta 是 W[1]-hard 的；4. MSR 针对 treewidth + Delta 是 FPT 的。

Conclusion: MSR 问题在加权图上的参数化复杂度边界被划定：它在 treewidth + Delta 参数下是 FPT 的，但在 treewidth + k、顶点覆盖数 + k 以及 k + Delta（即使在团或完全二分图中）等参数下是 W[1]-hard 的。

Abstract: In the Min-Sum-Radii (MSR) clustering problem, we are given a finite set X of n points in a metric space. The objective is to find at most k clusters centered at a subset of these points such that every point of X is assigned to one of the clusters, minimizing the sum of the radii of the clusters. The problem is known to be NP-hard even on metrics induced by weighted planar graphs and metrics with constant doubling dimension, as shown by Gibson et al. (SWAT 2008). In this work, we investigate the parameterized complexity of MSR on metrics induced by undirected graphs. We distinguish between weighted graph metrics (with positive edge weights) and unweighted graph metrics (where all edges have unit weight).
  Weighted Graph Metrics: We show that MSR is W[1]-hard on metrics induced by weighted bipartite graphs, when parameterized by the combined parameter k (the number of clusters) and Delta (the cost of the clustering). We then investigate the structural parameterized complexity of the problem. Drexler et al. (arXiv:2310.02130) showed that the MSR problem admits an XP algorithm on metrics induced by weighted graphs when parameterized by treewidth, and asked whether this can be improved to fixed-parameter tractability. We first answer their question in the negative, and more strongly show that MSR stays W[1]-hard on metrics induced by undirected weighted bipartite graphs when parameterized by the vertex cover number plus k. We then turn our attention to parameters for dense graphs and show that MSR remains W[1]-hard when parameterized by k+Delta even on cliques and complete bipartite graphs. On the positive side, we employ the known XP algorithm parameterized by treewidth, to show that the MSR problem is FPT when parameterized by the parameter treewidth plus Delta.

</details>


### [73] [Optimal Testing of Reed-Muller Codes with an Online Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22813)
*Esty Kelman,Uri Meir,Kai Zhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文通过引入“半样本测试器”这一混合测试模型，有效解决了在线擦除模型下Reed-Muller码及提升仿射不变码的高效属性测试难题，并实现了最优查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在线擦除模型（online-erasure model）下，对手在查询后可能擦除数据点导致标准测试失效的问题，并优化Reed-Muller码在受限条件下的查询复杂度。

Method: 定义并分析了“半样本测试器（semi-sample-based testers）”，即一种先选择定义域子集S，随后在S内进行均匀随机查询的混合测试模型。通过优化该模型下的可靠性分析来实现对Reed-Muller码的测试。

Result: 在在线擦除模型下实现了Reed-Muller码的最优查询复杂度，性能优于现有研究（Minzer and Zheng, SODA 2024），并成功应用于提升仿射不变码的测试。

Conclusion: 证明了半样本测试器在处理在线擦除模型中的Reed-Muller码测试是有效的，并实现了最优查询复杂度；同时将该方法扩展至提升仿射不变码（lifted affine-invariant codes），提供了该类编码在在线擦除模型下的首个测试器。

Abstract: Motivated by applications to property testing in the online-erasure model of Kalemaj, Raskhodnikova, and Varma (ITCS 2022 and Theory of Computing 2023), we define and analyze {\em semi-sample-based testers} for Reed-Muller codes. The task in Reed-Muller testing is to determine whether an input function $f: \F^n \to \F$ belongs to the Reed-Muller code or is far from it, using as few point queries to $f$ as possible. Reed-Muller testing is a well-studied task with its roots in both the Property Testing and Probabilistically Checkable Proofs literature. The online-erasure model introduces a twist: after each query made, an adversary may erase up to $t$ points of the input function, potentially thwarting any test in which the queries follow a predictable pattern.
  Semi-sample-based testers are a hybrid between sample-based testers -- which can only make uniformly random queries to the input function -- and standard testers, which can choose their queries freely. They are designed with the online-erasure model in mind and operate by first choosing some subset $S$ of the domain and then making their queries uniformly at random inside of $S$. We describe semi-sample-based testers for the Reed-Muller code and give an optimal analysis of their soundness.
  Consequently, we show that semi-sample-based testers are indeed effective in the presence of online erasures, and thereby achieve optimal query complexity for testing the Reed-Muller code in the online-erasure model. This result improves upon prior work of Minzer and Zheng (SODA 2024). As an added bonus, we show that semi-sample-based testers also exist for the lifted affine-invariant codes of Guo, Kopparty, and Sudan (ITCS 2013), thereby providing the first known testers for these codes in the online-erasure model.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [74] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过MOOD基准发现传统安全评估模型在应对OOD对齐失效时表现不佳，提出并验证了将防护模型与OOD探测技术结合的监控架构，显著提升了安全风险的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在处理分布外（OOD）情境时常出现对齐失效，现有的监控管道难以有效检测这些未预见的安全风险。

Method: 1. 构建了Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD)基准测试，通过限制训练集来模拟未见过的对齐失效情况；2. 测试了四种OOD检测器；3. 提出将安全防护模型（Guard Models）与OOD检测器（如马氏距离、困惑度指标）相结合的监控方案。

Result: 结合安全防护模型与马氏距离/困惑度OOD检测器，使异常检测召回率从39%提升至45%；此外，研究发现该组合方法在提升召回率方面的表现优于将防护模型参数扩大20倍的效果。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为大型语言模型监控中的关键组件，且结合OOD检测比单纯依赖更大规模的监督模型更具效能，为解决模型对齐失效问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>
