Based on these requirements, we design KAPPS, a knowledge-based architecture that uses an ontology-grounded knowledge graph as a unifying data backbone, combined with a semantic interface layer to enable consistent data and information integration, reasoning, and communication across heterogeneous systems and services, turning the knowledge graph from an integration layer into the factories authoritative write-time state. KAPPS incorporates modules for constraint enforcement and event-driven planning, enabling incremental adaptation of execution plans under uncertainty and human-machine knowledge exchange. The applicability of KAPPS is demonstrated through two implemented use cases: (i) Anomaly detection and learning through knowledge graph mediated services and (ii) runtime constraint enforcement in a modular conveyor system. Subsequently, the architecture is evaluated against the 14 requirements (ed. abstract shortened)

</details>


### [112] [Compiling Agentic Workflows into LLM Weights: Near-Frontier Quality at Two Orders of Magnitude Less Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22502)
*Simon Dennis,Rivaan Patil,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了为何开发者偏好复杂的Agent编排框架而非将流程微调进小模型的“地下智能体”方法，通过三项实证任务证明了后者不仅可行，还能解决隐私保护和成本控制等核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agent编排框架（如LangGraph等）虽然流行，但存在推理延迟高、成本高、暴露隐私流程及上下文窗口占用等问题。尽管已有将流程编译进小型微调模型的技术（地下智能体），但开发者仍倾向于使用编排框架。本文旨在识别并解决阻碍“地下智能体”采纳的三个核心感知障碍。

Method: 通过实证研究，针对旅游预订（14个节点）、Zoom支持（14个节点）和保险索赔（55个节点）三个复杂场景，验证了“地下智能体”方法的可行性，并对比分析了各方案的优劣。

Result: 实证结果显示，“地下智能体”能够有效处理复杂的工作流，且在效果上并未劣于编排方法，成功消除了编排框架带来的成本和隐私风险。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过微调建立“地下智能体”（Subterranean Agents）是可行的且在性能上足以匹敌复杂的编排框架（Orchestration），同时具备更强的隐私保护、更低的延迟和成本优势。应当推动开发者从依赖编排框架转向采用这种编译式的方法。

Abstract: Agent orchestration frameworks have proliferated, collectively exceeding 290,000 GitHub stars across LangGraph, CrewAI, Google ADK, OpenAI Agents SDK, Semantic Kernel, Strands, and LlamaIndex. All follow the same pattern: an external orchestrator above the LLM, injecting instructions and routing decisions every turn. Recent work has shown this architecture is dominated for procedural tasks by simply providing the procedure in a frontier model's system prompt [Dennis et al., 2026a], at the cost of consuming the context window, requiring a frontier model for every conversation, and exposing proprietary procedures to third-party providers. Compiling the procedure into the weights of a small fine-tuned model -- creating a subterranean agent -- should resolve all of these concerns, and prior work (SimpleTOD, FireAct, SynTOD, WorkflowLLM, Agent Lumos) has shown the technique works. Yet developer adoption has overwhelmingly favored orchestration. We identify three perceived barriers and address each empirically across travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom support (14 nodes, product-specific knowledge), and insurance claims (55 nodes, 6 decision hubs).

</details>


### [113] [Towards Direct Evaluation of Harness Optimizers via Priority Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22505)
*Kai Tzu-iunn Ong,Minseok Kang,Dongwook Choi,Junhee Cho,Seungju Kim,Seungwon Lim,Geunha Jang,Minwoo Oh,Bogyung Jeong,Sunghwan Kim,Taeyoon Kwon,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对Agent Harness优化器评估缺乏过程指标的问题，提出了“优先级排序”评估方法及Shor数据集，实现了无需昂贵部署即可直接量化优化器能力的低成本评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Harness优化评估仅关注最终性能增益（端到端），忽略了中间步骤的准确性，难以区分优化器是依靠信息驱动还是盲目试错；此外，直接评估缺乏标准（Oracle）。

Method: 引入了“优先级排序”（Priority Ranking）评估方法，要求优化器对工具组件对性能的影响进行排序，而非仅依赖最终性能反馈；同时构建了包含182个人工验证场景的Shor基准数据集。

Result: 实验表明，基于优先级排序的评估结果与多步优化下的实际性能提升存在显著正相关，验证了该评估方法的有效性与预测能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于“优先级排序”的直接评估框架，并构建了Shor基准数据集，证明了该方法能够有效且低成本地量化Agent调整指令（Harness）优化器的能力，并成为优化性能的可靠预测指标。

Abstract: Harness optimization enables automated agent creation by having an optimizer agent iteratively update the harness of target agents. Despite its success, current studies evaluate optimizers solely by observing target agents' performance gains. This indirect end-improvement evaluation neglects optimizers' actions at intermediate steps, which are often erroneous and hinder agent performance. Therefore, it is unclear whether harness optimization is driven by optimizers' informed update actions or simply trial-and-error. This necessitates direct evaluation of harness optimizers. However, evaluating harness optimizers directly is non-trivial and costly due to the lack of oracle harnesses. To address this, we present a simple, low-cost design to directly evaluate them, namely priority ranking. By asking harness optimizers to rank components (e.g., tools) in a given harness by their potential to improve/hinder agent performance when updated, our design quantifies optimizer ability at the step level without expensive rollouts or manual examination. More importantly, optimizers' ranking performance correlates with their ability to improve agents in actual multi-step harness optimization, establishing priority ranking as a reliable predictor of optimization ability. Priority ranking is enabled by Shor, a collection of 182 human-verified optimization scenarios spanning across domains, designs, and time stages. Codes and data can be found at https://github.com/k59118/Harness_Optimizer_Evaluation.

</details>


### [114] [A Subjective Logic-based method for runtime confidence updates in safety arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22530)
*Benjamin Herd,Jessica Kelly,Clarissa Heinemann,João-Vitor Zacchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用主观逻辑（SL）的动态定量安全保障方法，通过整合设计时证据与运行时的安全性能指标，在无需精确贝叶斯计算的情况下，实现了系统安全置信度的实时更新与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统静态安全案例在面对复杂、动态运行环境（特别是包含机器学习组件的系统）时，无法反映实时安全状态及置信度变化的问题。

Method: 采用基于主观逻辑（Subjective Logic, SL）的保障案例框架；整合设计时证据与窗口化运行时安全性能指标（SPI）；设计了一种启发式更新规则，在无违规时逐步增加置信度，在出现违规时实施即时惩罚，以提升系统的安全性响应速度。

Result: 通过在建筑工地辅助功能的仿真场景中验证，证明了该方法能够实时跟踪基于机器学习的检测组件的置信度，置信度会根据运行时的SPI证据进行动态演化，表现出良好的安全性响应特征。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了一种将设计时证据与运行时SPI动态结合的框架，证明了在无需追求精确贝叶斯后验更新的前提下，利用主观逻辑（SL）可以有效地在动态系统中提供可演进的安全保障。

Abstract: We present a method for dynamic quantitative assurance that enhances static safety cases with continuous, runtime-driven confidence updates. The method quantifies and propagates confidence across the development lifecycle by integrating design-time evidence and windowed runtime Safety Performance Indicators (SPIs) within a single Subjective Logic (SL)-based assurance case. At runtime, SPI evidence is continuously evaluated, and targeted claims are updated using a rule that increases confidence in the absence of violations and imposes prompt penalties when violations occur. This design prioritizes safety-relevant responsiveness over exact classical Bayesian posterior updates. We demonstrate the method using a simulation-based construction zone assist function, focusing on an ML-based construction cone detection component, and show how confidence evolves as SPI evidence is observed in operation.

</details>


### [115] [Think Thrice Before You Speak: Dual knowledge-enhanced Theory-of-Mind Reasoning for Persuasive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22602)
*Minghui Ma,Bin Guo,Runze Yang,Mengqi Chen,Yan Liu,Jingqi Liu,Yahan Pei,Xuehao Ma,Qiuyun Zhang,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM在说服性对话中推理心理状态不足的问题，本文引入BDI框架与TTBYS推理方法，并构建了ToM-BPD数据集，显著提升了模型在心理状态预测与说服策略上的准确性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在说服性对话中缺乏对心理状态（ToM）之间内在依赖关系的推理能力，导致推理过程碎片化且不稳定。

Method: 1. 提出基于信念-欲望-意图（BDI）框架的ToM-PD任务；2. 构建了大规模标注数据集ToM-BPD；3. 提出名为“Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS)”的知识增强逐步推理框架。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上应用TTBYS框架后，在欲望、信念和说服策略预测任务上分别超越GPT-5达1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过BDI框架建模心理状态依赖性并结合TTBYS框架，能够显著提升大语言模型在说服性对话中的心理理论（ToM）推理能力、可解释性及逻辑一致性。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue requires reasoning about others' latent mental states, a capability known as Theory of Mind (ToM). However, due to reliance on simple prompting strategies and insufficient ToM knowledge, existing LLMs often fail to capture the intrinsic dependencies among mental states, leading to fragmented representations and unstable reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce the ToM-based Persuasive Dialogue (ToM-PD) task, grounded in the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) framework, which explicitly models the sequential dependencies among mental states in multi-turn dialogues. To facilitate research on this task, we construct a large-scale annotated dataset, ToM-based Broad Persuasive Dialogues (ToM-BPD), capturing fine-grained mental states and corresponding persuasive strategies. We further propose Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS), a knowledge-enhanced stepwise reasoning framework that leverages both explicit and implicit prior experiences to improve LLMs' inference of desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies. Experimental results demonstrate that Qwen3-8B equipped with TTBYS outperforms GPT-5 by 1.20%, 22.80%, and 16.97% in predicting desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies, respectively. Case studies further show that our approach enhances interpretability and consistency in reasoning.

</details>


### [116] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AtelierEval 基准，用于量化评估提示词编写者（人类或AI）的提示词能力，并引入 AtelierJudge 自动评分系统，揭示了当前提示词生成技术应向图像增强策略发展的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文生图评测仅关注图像生成模型本身，忽略了作为“上游组件”的提示词编写者（人类或多模态大模型）在用户意图转化和Prompt优化方面的能力评估。

Method: 1. 构建 AtelierEval 基准：涵盖360个专家制作的任务，分为三个类别，模拟真实挑战；2. 开发 AtelierJudge：基于技能、具备记忆增强的智能体评估器；3. 实施对比实验：对8个多模态大模型（MLLM）和48名人类用户在4个文生图后端上进行测评。

Result: AtelierJudge 与人类专家评估的相关性达到 0.79；实验发现现有大模型在提示词生成上更依赖模仿策略，而非规划策略。

Conclusion: AtelierEval 作为首个量化提示词能力的基准，揭示了当前提示词生成策略中“模仿”优于“规划”的洞察，并建议未来的提示词生成器应向图像增强方向发展。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [117] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab 将自动化研究从“黑盒脚本”升级为交互式多智能体实验室，通过 Claw-Code Harness 实现研究流程的闭环可控，提升了科研产出的质量与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动研究往往局限于单一智能体或固定的串行流程，难以满足复杂研究对交互性、可控性、协作性及实验完整性的需求。

Method: 提出了一种实验室原生自主研究平台，支持自定义角色、多智能体协作、实时监控及回滚/恢复功能；核心组件 Claw-Code Harness 连接了本地代码库、数据集与实验执行，实现了闭环反馈与实验完整性管理。

Result: 在五个AI研究案例评估中，Claw AI Lab 在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文展示质量方面均优于基准系统 AutoResearchClaw。

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab 代表了向“可交互、可靠的科学基础设施”范式转变的初步尝试，通过将自动化从单一流程转变为多智能体协作实验室，显著提升了科学研究的效率与质量。

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [118] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在处理具超线性增长和尾部风险的预测任务时表现出“反向缩放”现象（能力越强效果越差），并指出传统单阈值评估指标掩盖了这一性能退化，呼吁使用连续无界的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到大型语言模型（LLM）在特定类型的预测任务（具有超线性增长和制度变换尾部风险，如金融和流行病学）中，模型能力越强，分布预测效果反而越差，旨在探究这一反常现象及其评估上的局限性。

Method: 1. 构建了无污染的模拟基准测试集ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim)；2. 对比了合成SIR流行病模型与线性对照组；3. 在COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场和恶性通货膨胀等真实数据集上进行验证；4. 使用分位数分解分析模型输出；5. 对Llama-3.1系列进行模型规模与后训练影响的对比研究。

Result: 1. 确认存在“反向缩放”现象：更强的模型在处理上述任务时预测偏差更大；2. 错误集中在分布的上尾部，表现为对超线性增长进行过度推断；3. 模型规模增加和后训练均独立加剧了该效应；4. 现有的单阈值基准评估指标会错误地呈现“能力越强效果越好”的假象，掩盖了模型在极端尾部预测上的失效。

Conclusion: LLM在处理具有超线性增长和尾部风险的预测任务时表现出“反向缩放”现象。现有常用的基于单阈值的评估指标掩盖了这一问题，因此研究者建议在LLM预测评估中必须结合使用连续且无界的准确性度量。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [119] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，当前AI模型在预测科学进步方面存在显著局限，难以可靠地预测科研突破的实现及其时间节点，这表明AI目前的预测能力尚未触及逻辑推理层面，无法作为科学预测的可靠工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨人工智能是否能预测未来的科学进步，探究AI在前沿科学演进中的预测能力及其应用局限性。

Method: 提出了名为CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）的多学科、事件级基准测试框架。通过控制知识获取范围，在可行性评估、机械推理、生成式解决方案设计及时间预测四个维度上对大模型进行时序性评估。

Result: 模型在4,760个科学事件中表现出系统性和领域依赖性的缺陷；模型难以预测科学突破的实现及其时间；AI领域进展的预测准确度高于生物、化学和物理领域；模型表现出严重的过度自信和响应偏差，且对训练截止日期（cutoff）表现出不敏感性。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统尚无法作为预测科学进步的可靠工具。尽管模型能识别潜在研究方向，但无法准确预测科研成果实现的可能性及具体时间，其局限性不仅源于训练数据限制，更反映了推理能力的深层不足。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22707)
*Stephanie Rosenthal,Shamsi Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈发现，AI 的广泛应用在提升协作效率的同时，弱化了职场中关键的隐形文化实践（如指导与反馈），进而可能损害员工的长期职业成长。建议企业通过显性化职场贡献及针对性的领导策略来平衡效率与文化建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 在企业中的普及，传统职场角色边界趋于模糊。研究旨在探讨 AI 如何重塑工作流程，特别是它对非正式文化实践（如导师制、职业成长）带来的深层影响。

Method: 对一家大型科技公司的 24 名产品相关人员进行了深度访谈，旨在了解 AI 对其个人工作、团队协作及职业互动的影响。

Result: AI 既能促进同行间的协作，也带来了风险，主要体现为：传统职业成长路径（如获得反馈、领导力培养、职场导师制）受阻，关键的隐性职场社交和技能沉淀受到侵蚀。

Conclusion: AI 在提升工作效率的同时，对企业文化中的隐性实践（如指导、反馈、职业成长）构成了潜在威胁。企业应采取措施使这些隐含的“隐形工作”显性化，并引导领导者在 AI 转型中维护多元思维与协作文化。

Abstract: An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.

</details>


### [121] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型在进行连续评估任务时会受到历史对话极性的显著影响（AMEL效应），尤其在模型不确定时及面对负面信息时更为严重。单纯提升模型规模无法解决此问题，建议在评估流程中采用独立上下文或平衡历史记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型常被用作自动化评估器（如代码审查、内容审核），研究旨在探究连续对话历史的极性是否会偏向后续的评估判断，即“对LLM判断的累积消息效应（AMEL）”。

Method: 在4个提供商的11个LLM上进行了75,898次API调用。通过对比在独立环境下与在充满正/负面评价的历史环境中呈现相同测试项的结果，通过统计指标（如Cohen's d、Spearman相关性等）量化模型判断的偏移程度，并分析了模型不确定性、上下文长度、评估项位置及模型规模对该偏差的影响。

Result: 1. 模型会倾向于对话的主导极性（d = -0.17）；2. 当模型在基准情况下表现出高熵（不确定）时，偏差更明显；3. 偏差程度不随上下文长度增加而增长；4. 存在负面偏见不对称性，负面历史诱导的偏差是正面的1.62倍；5. 模型规模的提升能减轻但无法消除此现象。

Conclusion: 模型在处理连续评估任务时会受到历史对话极性的影响（AMEL效应），这在评估管道中引入了偏差。该偏差无法通过扩大模型规模完全消除。最有效的缓解措施是为每个评估项使用独立的上下文，或在无法避免批处理时平衡历史记录的极性。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [122] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的参数化模块逻辑程序形式，旨在为一阶ASP引入模块化特性，提升程序的结构化与复用能力，并与clingo的集合控制特性达成了一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一阶ASP中缺乏有效的模块化构建机制的问题，以改善大规模ASP程序的结构化、复用性及实例化能力。

Method: 引入了“参数化模块逻辑程序”（parametric modular logic programs）这一新形式化定义，该定义支持子程序的参数化与内涵性声明，并将其与传统非模块化ASP进行关联。

Result: 成功建立了一套模块化ASP的理论基础，并证明了该框架能够捕捉clingo语言中集合控制特性的语义。

Conclusion: 本文为一阶回答集程序（ASP）中的模块化提供了理论基础，并证明了参数化模块逻辑程序可以有效描述带有集合控制（collective control）的程序，为ASP的结构化建模提供了形式化框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [123] [Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22720)
*Andrii Kryshtal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了主流AI模型在武装冲突语境下的安全性，发现模型普遍存在未能识别种族灭绝或制造虚假等同的风险，且易被引导对既定事实产生错误认知，作者发布了专项评估框架以改善此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型已广泛应用于受武装冲突影响的地区，但缺乏对模型输出是否会加剧冲突的有效评估手段。研究旨在揭示模型在冲突语境下的对齐失效问题及其潜在的社会危害。

Method: 构建了一个包含90个多轮对话场景的评估框架，测试了来自四家公司（OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI）的九种模型配置，重点考察模型在假等同、否定种族灭绝及未能识别民族歧视用语等方面的表现。

Result: 各模型失败率在6%至47%之间；在被要求对国际法院已定性的案件“寻求平衡”时，九种模型中的五种失败率高达80%-100%，显示出模型对事实真相的认知易受操纵。

Conclusion: AI模型在冲突语境下的表现存在显著的安全风险，模型选择对于减少负面社会影响至关重要。建议将提出的评估框架纳入模型对齐评估流程中，以确保AI在敏感地缘政治环境下的可靠性。

Abstract: AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.

</details>


### [124] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决多智能体LLM系统中通过KV缓存进行隐式通信带来的敏感信息泄露问题，本文提出了LCGuard框架，利用对抗性训练对缓存表示进行安全转换，在保障通信安全的同时维持了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中基于KV缓存的隐式通信虽然高效，但其作为不透明通道，可能导致智能体间的敏感上下文信息泄露，缺乏显式控制。

Method: 引入了LCGuard框架，将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存传输前进行表示层转换。通过对抗性训练机制（Adversarial training）使转换后的表示保留任务相关语义，同时最小化对抗解码器重构敏感信息的能力。

Result: 在多个模型系列和多智能体基准测试中，LCGuard显著降低了重构式的攻击成功率和信息泄露风险，同时保持了与标准KV共享基准相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard通过对抗性训练有效地在保证多智能体任务性能的同时，降低了KV缓存中嵌入的敏感信息泄露风险，为隐式通信的安全保护提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [125] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对线性注意力模型中记忆更新灵活性不足的问题，Gated DeltaNet-2 通过解耦通道维度的擦除与写入门控，显著提升了模型的信息维护与更新能力，在长文本任务中表现优于 Mamba 系列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Delta 规则模型（如 Gated DeltaNet 和 KDA）使用单一标量门控同时控制记忆的擦除与写入，限制了其对压缩记忆编辑的灵活性，导致难以在保持历史记忆的同时有效更新新信息。

Method: 提出 Gated Delta Rule-2 机制，通过将通道维度的“擦除门” (erase gate) 与“写入门” (write gate) 解耦，并引入通道维度的衰减；同时推导了快速权重更新视角的实现方法、带有块状 WY 变换的高效并行训练算法及门控感知的反向传播。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数量及 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens 的规模下，Gated DeltaNet-2 在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务中优于 Mamba-2、KDA 和 Mamba-3 变体，尤其在长文本 RULER 的多键 (multi-key) 检索中优势明显，且兼容循环与混合推理模式。

Conclusion: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过解耦擦除与写入门控，提升了线性注意力模型对压缩记忆的管理能力，在语言建模、推理及长文本检索任务中展现出比 Mamba-2、KDA 等模型更优的性能。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [CompPow: A Case for Component-level GPU Power Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21847)
*Shaizeen Aga,Mohamed Assem Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对GPU的高功耗问题，提出了CompPow组件感知管理方案，通过软硬件协同设计，在提升能效（10%）的同时优化了性能（5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML驱动的数据中心中GPU功耗巨大，且现有优化多集中于集群层面，缺乏对GPU内部各组件能耗的细致关注与管理。

Method: 提出了一种名为CompPow的组件感知（component-aware）方法，对GPU内部各组件的功耗进行细粒度分析与管理，而非仅着眼于数据中心层面的整体功耗控制。

Result: 在多种机器学习运算和执行模型中，CompPow成功实现了10%的能效提升以及5%的性能改善。

Conclusion: 通过组件感知的软硬件协同设计，可以在现代GPU中实现显著的能效提升，并为未来的电源管理优化提供了方向。

Abstract: The ever increasing demand for ML-driven intelligence in a wide spectrum of domains has led to ubiquity of GPUs. At the same time, GPUs are notorious for their power consumption needs and often dominate power allocation in a typical ML datacenter. While datacenter-level power optimizations which focus on collection of GPUs are promising, in this work, we take a different tack -- namely, we take a closer look at power consumption inside a GPU. Specifically, as modern GPUs are comprised of integrated components, we make a case for component-awareness, termed CompPow in this work, for improved power management in modern GPUs. We demonstrate for a variety of ML operations and execution patterns, CompPow has the potential to deliver higher energy efficiency (10%) and even improved performance (5%). We conclude with recommendations on how component-aware software-hardware co-design can extract additional energy efficiency from modern GPUs.

</details>


### [127] [Emerging memory technologies at room/cryogenic temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21912)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文综述了从室温到低温环境下各类新型易失性与非易失性存储技术的原理及性能权衡，旨在解决高性能计算在能效、延迟和扩展性方面的瓶颈挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缩放比例定律面临物理和功耗限制，导致存储延迟、能效与数据搬运成为现代计算系统的性能瓶颈。同时，新兴工作负载（机器学习、超导/量子计算）对存储器的带宽、延迟、密度的需求激增，迫切需要对室温及低温存储技术进行全面评估。

Method: 概述并系统梳理了多种易失性与非易失性存储技术（如SRAM、DRAM、RRAM、MRAM、FeFET等）及其在室温和低温环境下的适用性，同时探讨了特定的低温存储技术（如UTBB-SOI pseudo-static和JJFET）。分析维度涵盖工作原理、读写机制、数据保持特性以及面积、功耗与性能权衡。

Result: 提供了一份关于各类存储技术的系统性研究概览，明确了各技术在不同应用环境下的性能指标与设计权衡，为构建适应未来的室温及低温计算存储架构提供了理论依据与技术参考。

Conclusion: 通过对比不同存储技术的特性及挑战，强调了针对不同温度环境（室温与低温）优化存储架构对于打破当前计算瓶颈的重要性，并为未来高性能计算存储系统的发展指明了方向。

Abstract: As conventional technology scaling approaches physical and power limitations, modern computing systems increasingly face performance bottlenecks arising from memory latency, energy consumption, scalability constraints, and data movement overheads. Simultaneously, emerging workloads such as machine learning, graph analytics, and scientific computing demand memory technologies with higher bandwidth, lower latency, improved energy efficiency, and greater storage density. These challenges have motivated extensive research into both room-temperature memories and cryogenic memory systems targeted toward superconducting and quantum computing platforms.
  This chapter presents an overview of volatile and non-volatile memory technologies operating across room-temperature and cryogenic environments. The discussion includes SRAM, DRAM, embedded DRAM (eDRAM), NAND/NOR Flash, Resistive Random Access Memory (RRAM), Magneto-resistive Random Access Memory (MRAM), and Ferroelectric Field-Effect Transistor (FeFET)-based memories. In addition, cryogenic technologies including UTBB-SOI-based pseudo-static storage circuits and Josephson Junction Field-Effect Transistor (JJFET)-based devices are discussed in the context of ultra-low-temperature computing systems. The chapter highlights the operational principles, read/write mechanisms, retention behavior, and tradeoffs among area, performance, scalability, and energy efficiency across these memory technologies, while examining challenges and opportunities for future room-temperature and cryogenic computing architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决多机器人系统在未知环境下无法自适应及难以理解操作员意图的问题，本文提出了一种VR共享控制框架，通过结合运动基元规划器与导纳控制器，实现了人机协作的实时高效无人机群导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自治多机器人系统难以适应非结构化环境中的突发情况，且难以精准捕捉操作员的意图，导致在复杂环境下导航效率低下。

Method: 提出一种基于虚拟现实（VR）的共享控制框架，核心包括：（1）一种基于运动基元的路径规划器，可根据操作员输入实时计算无碰撞轨迹；（2）引入导纳控制器（Admittance Controller）以平滑响应操作员指令；（3）构建了支持物理与虚拟混合现实的交互界面，提供双向反馈（引导信号输入与状态视觉反馈）。

Result: 实验表明，该系统能显著改善障碍物回避性能，维持编队间距，并降低操作员的任务负荷及操作难度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于VR的共享控制框架有效地结合了人类的直觉引导与机器人的自主导航，在各种约束及未知环境下实现了高效、安全的无人机编队协作，证明了人机协同导航的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [129] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机群在受限环境下的多重覆盖问题，本文提出了一种无需全局调度的分布式算法，实现了低算力与有限通信条件下的高可靠监控覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的无人机集群多重覆盖（即每个目标需被多个机器人监控）方案多依赖集中式整数规划，难以应对现实中通信范围有限、计算资源受限及部分系统故障导致的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 开发了一种分布式多重覆盖算法，该算法仅依赖机器人的局部感知能力、局部通信能力，且无需任何全局协调机制。

Result: 该方法在分布式约束下，有效实现了对关键资产的冗余覆盖，并具备良好的故障容忍能力和可扩展性，满足了大规模集群在实际应用中的实时任务需求。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分布式多重覆盖算法能有效解决大规模机器人集群在资源受限环境下的鲁棒性监测需求，验证了通过局部感知与协同即可实现复杂的全局覆盖任务。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>


### [130] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过闭环强化学习，成功将单纯在无摩擦仿真中训练的微纤维操纵策略迁移至现实世界，利用实时视觉反馈补偿了微尺度下的非建模交互影响，实现了高精度的形状控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微尺度表面和界面相互作用难以精确建模，限制了传统模型控制及Sim-to-Real学习在微操纵（micromanipulation）领域的应用。

Method: 提出一种闭环视觉反馈的Sim-to-Real强化学习方法。在无摩擦的简化仿真环境中训练策略，利用40Hz的实时视觉反馈在部署时实时修正现实环境中的非建模表面作用力影响。

Result: 在包含多种直径（50-120μm）和长度（10-20mm）的丝绸微纤维实验中，策略从未经重新训练或微调的情况下实现了平均约270μm的形状误差，具备高鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过闭环视觉反馈，在简化仿真训练的强化学习策略可以直接迁移至现实世界，有效克服微尺度表面相互作用带来的建模困难，实现高精度的微纤维形状控制。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [131] [Motion Design for Grasp-Based Dynamic Locomotion in Microgravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21704)
*Chaerim Moon,Joohyung Kim,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对微重力环境下多肢体机器人的抓取式运动，本文提出了参数化规划框架，证明了扩大接触空间和优化全身动力学协调对提升移动性能的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境下的抓取式移动依赖稀疏且不规则的支撑点，需要精细的全身协调与锚点交互控制，现有研究缺乏对多肢体系统相关动力学与运动学约束的综合设计指导。

Method: 提出了一种参数化的运动规划框架，支持对步态模式、步幅、运动速度和基准姿态等进行调节；采用物理仿真实验，对比评估了两种具代表性的四足机器人形态。

Result: 研究表明，优化接触配置以扩大可行力矩空间，以及通过动态调节减弱全身运动中的冲击效应，能够显著增强运动的稳定性和降低执行机构的负载需求。

Conclusion: 在微重力环境下，通过扩大可行接触力矩空间并衰减脉冲式的全身动力学影响，可以有效提升多肢体机器人的抓取式动态运动性能。这些发现为微重力环境下机器人的接触配置选择及全身协调控制提供了设计准则。

Abstract: Locomotion in microgravity often relies on sparsely and irregularly arranged anchors, motivating grasp-based mobility with multiple limbs. In this setting, dynamic locomotion is feasible only through deliberate regulation of both anchored interactions and whole-body coordination under coupled dynamic and kinematic constraints. This paper presents design insights for grasp-based dynamic locomotion with multi-limbed robotic systems in microgravity, targeting scenarios that require 6D limb manipulation to establish contacts with candidate anchors. The investigated design parameters include gait pattern, stride length, locomotion speed, and nominal posture. A parameterizable locomotion planning framework is proposed to support variations of these parameters and to evaluate the resulting locomotion performance in terms of stability and actuation demand. Two representative quadruped morphologies are adopted for evaluation in physics-based simulation. The results demonstrate that enlarging the feasible contact wrench space and attenuating impulsive whole-body dynamics improve locomotion performance. These findings inform strategies for contact configuration selection and whole-body coordination in microgravity locomotion with multi-limbed systems.

</details>


### [132] [PGDG: Physically Grounded Data Generation for Robust Bimanual Policy Learning from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21710)
*Cunxi Dai,Haoran Chang,Aditya Nisal,Rahul Kumar,Guofei Chen,Tao Chen,Yuzhe Qin,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGDG 是一种无需额外人工标注的数据生成框架，它能将单次演示自动扩展为包含多样化恢复行为的鲁棒性数据集，显著提升了双臂机器人在接触丰富任务中的操控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富型双臂操作中，高质量演示数据获取昂贵，且系统在偏离状态轨迹时缺乏监督，导致行为克隆模型在遇到扰动时鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出 PGDG 框架，通过物理驱动的采样器与数据集策展人（Curator）之间的迭代循环生成数据；利用采样器生成多样化轨迹，策展人筛选高信息量、非冗余的恢复行为，辅以基于短时界限的采样控制进行动作重标注。

Result: 在四项双臂操作任务中，PGDG 表现优于传统的空间增强方法。在 RotateBox-Pitch 任务中，仿真成功率由 38% 提升至 93%，真实世界成功率由 35% 提升至 82%；微调 GR00T 模型后的成功率从 46% 提升至 77%。

Conclusion: PGDG 通过将单次演示扩展为高质量的恢复行为数据集，解决了接触丰富型双臂操作中数据稀缺和扰动敏感的问题，有效提升了模型在仿真和真实环境中的任务成功率，并能显著增强基础模型（如 GR00T）的微调效果。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation remains challenging because diverse demonstrations are expensive to collect, and even small disturbances can push the system into off-manifold states where no recovery supervision is available. We propose PGDG, a data generation framework with zero-shot curation that expands a single demonstration into a compact dataset of physically plausible, successful, and diverse recovery behaviors without additional human labeling. PGDG iterates between a physics-grounded sampler and a dataset curator, where the curator selects informative, non-redundant, and recoverable behaviors to update the sampling distribution toward under-covered recovery modes, and the sampler draws physically plausible rollout candidates from this updated distribution and retains successful trajectories. To further improve data quality, PGDG applies short-horizon sampling-based control to relabel selected risky states with corrective actions. Across four bimanual manipulation tasks, PGDG consistently outperforms spatial-only augmentation in both simulation and zero-shot real-world transfer. On RotateBox-Pitch, success improves from 38% to 93% in simulation and from 35% to 82% in the real world. PGDG also enables effective foundation models fine-tuning such as GR00T, increasing success from 46% to 77%. Additional results are available in our website: https://cunxid.github.io/PGDG/.

</details>


### [133] [Mind the Gaps: Multi-Robot Feedback-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21719)
*Thales Costa Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多机器人未知环境覆盖问题，提出了一种自适应遍历搜索新方法。通过在线实时更新环境参数模型来引导轨迹优化，实现了对环境高兴趣区域的动态高效探测与资源均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遍历搜索算法在已知目标分布的情况下表现良好，但在处理环境先验分布未知的情况下效率低下，难以实现动态高效的区域探索与数据采样。

Method: 提出了一种基于实时环境模型反馈的自适应覆盖策略。该方法改进了传统的遍历搜索（Ergodic Search）算法，通过在线构建并修正环境参数化模型来动态调整目标空间信息分布，从而优化机器人的控制策略。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该框架能够使机器人动态地优先探测高兴趣区域，在未知环境先验的条件下，显著提升了覆盖效率，并优化了多机器人的资源分配。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应覆盖策略通过在线更新环境参数模型，有效解决了多机器人系统在未知环境下的覆盖效率问题，证实了该框架在资源分配和探索性能上的优势。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of multi-robot adaptive coverage, where teams of robots perform dynamic sampling by continuously adjusting their positions to collect data in an environment. This task can be challenging, particularly when robots must be efficiently allocated to new sampling locations over time. Ergodic search methods optimize robot trajectories by ensuring that the robots' time-averaged spatial distribution aligns with the spatial distribution of environmental information. While these methods promote effective exploration provided a target distribution, they often fail to account for unknown prior distributions of the environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an adaptive coverage strategy that utilizes real-time feedback from an environmental model to adjust robot sampling behavior in response to unknown conditions. Our approach enhances traditional ergodic trajectory optimization by constructing a target spatial information distribution based on parametric models of the environment, which are updated online. This strategy assumes that the environment is either static or changes slowly compared to the robot's motion. Our framework allows robots to dynamically prioritize regions of high interest, improving coverage efficiency, synthesizing effective control policies for individual agents, and optimizing resource use in settings with unknown prior distributions. We validate our approach through simulations, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing coverage and resource allocation.

</details>


### [134] [Safe and Steerable Geometric Motion Policies for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21811)
*Albert Wu,Riccardo Bonalli,Thomas Lew,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafePBDS是一种新的几何一致性框架，通过拉回控制屏障函数确保机器人在复杂任务流形上的安全性，并支持高层策略注入残差动作以实现高效的灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作需要在不同的几何空间（如$\mathbb{R}^7, \mathrm{SE}(3), \mathbb{R}$）之间协调目标和约束，现有的方法难以在保持几何一致性的同时确保复杂任务的安全性和灵活性。

Method: 1. 引入“拉回控制屏障函数”（Pullback CBF），将任务空间的安全约束转化为配置空间的线性加速度约束；
2. 构建“任务空间动作接口”，允许高层策略注入低维残差动作，在保留自主行为的同时实现探索或特定控制。

Result: 在23自由度的Franka Panda-Allegro Hand上验证，抓取成功率达92.5%；支持低维动作接口控制（如3指抓取成功率94.4%）；成功实现了高难度的模型引导式手掌内物体旋转（>360度）。

Conclusion: SafePBDS 通过拉回控制屏障函数（Pullback CBF）和任务空间动作接口，实现了复杂多任务流形上的几何一致且安全可证明的机器人灵巧操作，证明了其在高自由度机械臂上的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous manipulation requires continuously reconciling objectives and constraints defined on heterogeneous geometric spaces: a robot controlled on a $\mathbb{R}^7$ configuration manifold may need to track end effector poses on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ while satisfying obstacle avoidance margins in $\mathbb{R}$. We present Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS), a geometrically consistent framework that computes optimal, certifiably safe configuration manifold accelerations from objectives and safety requirements on arbitrary task manifolds. SafePBDS builds on prior work that combines predefined task manifold dynamical systems to produce autonomous motion. Its first innovation is a pullback control barrier function construction, which converts task manifold safety conditions into linear constraints on configuration manifold accelerations. The second innovation is a task manifold action interface that allows a high-level policy to inject low dimensional residual motions; zero input recovers the autonomous behavior, while safety is preserved under arbitrary inputs. This lets high-level policies efficiently steer exploration while leaving precise motion to the autonomous behavior. We validate SafePBDS in simulation and on a 23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand platform. On dexterous grasping, SafePBDS achieves a $92.5\%$ success rate across 20 household objects and 120 trials. Using the action interface, the method can exclude any one of the four fingers during grasping via a one-dimensional action, achieving $94.4\%$ 3-finger grasp success across 3 objects and 36 trials. The efficient planning and safety guarantee of SafePBDS also enables the first model-based, fully actuated palm-down in-hand reorientation, exceeding $360^\circ$ of yaw rotation in both directions under varying object weight and wrist motion. Demo video and details: https://tml.stanford.edu/safe-pbds

</details>


### [135] [Analytical and Experimental Force Analysis of a Soft Linear Pneumatic Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21836)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对线性软套驱动器（LSSA）建立了准静态力学模型，揭示了压力、几何参数和轴向刚度对输出力的耦合影响，并通过实验证实了其力生成随位移增加而减小的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的软套驱动器（SSA）缺乏对其力生成行为的深入解释，特别是在延伸过程中的力变化、外部负载影响以及轴向刚度的机械作用方面。

Method: 开发了一个准静态分析模型，将净轴向力表示为端盖和折叠壁的压力贡献减去轴向刚度造成的阻力。并通过规定延伸实验和静态负载实验验证了模型。

Result: 在125 kPa压力下，LSSA产生的力随轴向延伸（0至40 mm）显著下降（从约112 N降至近0 N）；静态负载会延迟力生成并降低输出力，尤其在低压和中压阶段。

Conclusion: 线性软套驱动器（LSSA）的力产生由内部压力、驱动器几何形状、轴向位移、外部负载以及轴向刚度共同决定。该分析模型有效地解释了力随延伸增加而减小的现象，并为设计此类柔性驱动器提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Soft sleeve actuators (SSAs) have recently been developed as a pneumatic actuation approach for wearable and assistive robotic systems. By integrating the actuation structure into a sleeve-like geometry, these actuators can reduce reliance on external attachment layers and transmission mechanisms while maintaining compliance with limb-shaped surfaces. However, the force-generation behavior of SSAs remains insufficiently explained, particularly with respect to the variation of output force during extension, the influence of external loading, and the mechanical role of axial stiffness. This paper presents an analytical and experimental force analysis of a linear soft sleeve actuator (LSSA). A quasi-static analytical model was developed by expressing the net axial force as the pressure-generated contribution from the cap and folded walls, reduced by the force associated with axial stiffness. The model incorporates internal pressure, projected pressure areas, folded wall geometry, axial displacement, and an experimentally fitted axial stiffness relation. Prescribed-extension and static-load experiments were conducted to evaluate the actuator response. At 125 kPa, the generated force decreased from approximately 112 N at zero extension to nearly zero at 40 mm. Static loading delayed measurable force generation and reduced force output, particularly at low and intermediate pressures. The results show that LSSA force generation is governed by coupled effects of pressure, geometry, displacement, loading, and axial stiffness.

</details>


### [136] [EvoScene-VLA: Evolving Scene Beliefs Inside the Action Decoder for Chunked Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21862)
*Chushan Zhang,Ruihan Lu,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoScene-VLA 引入了一种具备持久、动作感知场景状态的循环机制，使机器人能够动态追踪动作导致的物理环境变化，从而显著提升了视觉-语言-动作模型的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型通常仅依赖单一当前视觉帧进行预测，忽略了机器人动作导致的接触、遮挡和物体运动所带来的场景几何变化，缺乏跨决策步的场景状态保持。

Method: 提出了一种循环场景前缀（recurrent scene prefix）机制。VLM 在每个决策步结合当前视觉观测与上一时间块的动作更新先验。动作解码器在生成动作的同时输出紧凑的场景更新，作为下一时刻的先验。训练时引入场景预测器和几何锚点以对齐 3D 信息，部署时移除。

Result: 在 31 项 RoboTwin 任务中，固定评估环境下成功率从 87.2% 提升至 89.1%，随机评估环境下从 86.1% 提升至 88.5%；在 Galaxea R1-Lite 现实机器人平台上表现优于所有基线。

Conclusion: EvoScene-VLA 通过引入基于动作的持久场景先验，克服了传统 VLA 在处理机器人动作引起场景变化时的局限性，在仿真和真实环境下均表现出优于现有基线的性能。

Abstract: Chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict multi-step robot controls, conditioning each update on the current visual observation alone. Yet robot actions cause contact, occlusion, and object motion, and the geometry that later decisions depend on can change before the next visual update arrives. Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [137] [Higher Order Reasoning for Collaborative Communicationless Mobile Robot Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21901)
*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无通信环境下，通过高阶认知推理、贝叶斯更新和MPPI控制实现多机器人隐式协作与长周期规划的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无通信环境下，传统的多机器人协调策略难以维持运行，系统急需一种能够通过推理实现隐式协作与长周期规划的方案。

Method: 提出了一种动态认知规划框架，核心组件包括：1. 基于高阶信念粒子（higher-order belief particles）的推理机制；2. 利用贝叶斯推断更新环境信念；3. 结合行为树（behavior tree）进行动作选择，预测队友行为；4. 利用时间感知模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器进行低层轨迹执行。

Result: 通过仿真和物理实验验证，该方法在任务响应速度上优于一阶推理基准模型，证明了其在部分可观测及通信受限场景下的协调能力。

Conclusion: 将认知逻辑（epistemic logic）应用于无通信多机器人系统是实现稳健协作的一种有效途径，能够显著提升长周期任务的协调效率。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>


### [138] [Non-Contact Vibration-Based Damage Detection of Civil Structures Using a Cost-Effective Autonomous UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21914)
*Javier Becerril,Maximiliano Vargas,Jennifer Herrera,Joanna Gutierrez,Jorge Rios,Mohsen Amjadian,Constantine Tarawneh,Jinghao Yang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用低成本自主无人机通过视觉运动追踪进行非接触式结构振动监测与损伤识别的方法，并证实了其在低成本前提下实现有效结构健康诊断的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统接触式结构健康监测在特定场景下测量困难的问题，探索利用低成本无人机平台实现高效、非接触式的结构损伤检测。

Method: 提出了一种非接触式结构损伤检测方法：利用定制的低成本无人机进行视频采集，通过基于计算机视觉的运动跟踪技术从视频中提取振动信号，并在频域分析其固有频率的变化，最后与接触式加速度计及有限元模型进行对比验证。

Result: 实验证明该系统（包括智能手机、USB相机及定制无人机）均能成功捕捉结构固有频率及其因损伤产生的频移。尽管无人机平台因受扰动影响误差略高（最高5.7%），但仍能有效识别损伤；同时，该方案相比商用无人机显著降低了成本。

Conclusion: 低成本、自主式无人机为缺乏接触式传感条件的结构健康监测提供了一种实用、灵活且可扩展的解决方案，并具备多架无人机协作监测的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation. A laboratory-scale frame structure is evaluated under healthy and simulated-damage conditions. The proposed system is validated through an experimental study involving two smartphones, a USB camera, and a custom-built low-cost UAV equipped with an onboard camera and an autonomous alignment system for operation in GPS-denied environments. The displacement time is extracted and analyzed in the frequency domain and compared to reference measurements from contact accelerometers and a finite element model. Experimental results show that all platforms successfully capture the fundamental frequency and its shift due to damage. Although the UAV exhibits slightly higher errors (up to 5.7%) due to platform-induced disturbances and sensing limitations, it reliably detects damage-induced frequency changes. Compared to commercial UAV systems, the proposed platform achieves comparable inspection performance at significantly lower cost. These results demonstrate that low-cost autonomous UAVs provide a practical, flexible, and scalable solution for structural health monitoring, particularly in scenarios where contact-based sensing is impractical. The findings also support the potential for the deployment of multiple cooperative UAVs to further enhance inspection coverage and robustness.

</details>


### [139] [Learning to Evolve: Multi-modal Interactive Fields for Robust Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21935)
*Peifeng Jiang,Hong Liu,Jin Jin,Wenshuai Wang,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIF是一种专为人形机器人设计的多模态交互场系统，通过将不确定性感知3DGS、空间记忆更新和交互安全几何约束相结合，解决了机器人运动引起感知失真及环境动态变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图与场景图系统难以应对人形机器人运动带来的感知畸变、环境动态变化以及操作层面的几何安全约束。

Method: 提出了多模态交互场（MIF），整合了三类特征场：1) 不确定性感知3D高斯溅射（3DGS）外观场（抑制步态模糊）；2) 空间场（维持拓扑记忆）；3) 几何场（支持交互位姿安全IPS）。引入了差异检测评分机制，仅更新局部不一致区域，并利用特征蒸馏技术优化内存占用。

Result: 在Unitree-G1人形机器人实机测试中，将非静态环境下的重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，并将语义内存占用降低了91.4%，实现了高效的在线运行。

Conclusion: MIF为具身智能提供了高效、鲁棒的场景记忆解决方案，证明了通过将不确定性感知与交互约束相结合，可以在高动态环境下实现人形机器人的可靠导航与操作。

Abstract: Safe manipulation-oriented navigation for humanoid robots requires scene memory that remains reliable under locomotion-induced perceptual distortion, environmental changes, and interaction-level geometric safety constraints. Existing semantic mapping and scene-graph systems are difficult to deploy directly in this setting because they often assume stable camera trajectories, static environments, or coarse object geometry. We introduce the Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF), a humanoid-oriented system that integrates confidence-aware semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting, discrepancy-triggered spatial memory updates, and task-driven geometric reconstruction within a closed-loop perception-adaptation pipeline. MIF couples three fields: an uncertainty-aware 3DGS Appearance Field that suppresses gait-induced blur, a Spatial Field that maintains topological memory, and a Geometry Field that supports Interaction Pose Safety (IPS) before manipulation. A discrepancy detection score is introduced to separate locomotion-induced false-positive changes from persistent changes and updates only locally inconsistent regions. On a Unitree-G1 humanoid in a real dynamic office, MIF improves relocation success in non-static environments from 12% to 94% compared with static scene-graph memory, while reducing semantic memory footprint by 91.4% through feature distillation for practical online operation. Project page and code: https://ziya-jiang.github.io/MIF-homepage/

</details>


### [140] [A Visitation Grid for Complete Coverage Foraging in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21947)
*Qi Arturo Gonzalez,Yifeng Gao,Li Zhang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于网格状态追踪的随机搜集策略，通过动态引导机器人向访问频率低的区域移动，有效解决了机器人群在任务末期搜集效率低下的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群集搜集算法在任务末期（搜集剩余少量资源时）效率低下，导致总任务时间过长，且未能有效避免冗余搜索。

Method: 提出一种基于网格的随机搜集策略（grid-based stochastic foraging strategy）：利用轻量级中心服务器维护环境网格地图，记录访问密度；机器人根据邻域内的访问计数概率性地选择未被充分探索的区域进行搜集。

Result: 仿真实验显示，与经典CPFA算法相比，该方法将总搜集时间减少了高达33%，末期搜集效率提升超过48%。

Conclusion: 所提算法在保持可扩展性的前提下，显著提升了资源搜集末期的效率，是解决机器人群在未知环境下完全搜集资源任务的有效方案。

Abstract: The complete collection of sparse resources in large, unknown environments remains a challenging problem for autonomous robot swarms. Previous studies have shown that a substantial portion of total mission time is consumed during the final stage of collection, where only a small fraction of randomly scattered resources remain. Consequently, many existing swarm foraging algorithms (search and collection) focus on collecting most resources within a limited time window, rather than improving end-stage efficiency for collecting all resources. We propose a grid-based stochastic foraging strategy that explicitly reduces redundant visits and accelerates late-stage collection. The unknown search area is partitioned into a grid map, which is maintained by a lightweight central server. To maintain scalability, both robots and the server operate within limited memory and computational constraints. The server updates the grid-level visitation counts based on robot-reported locations, producing a global estimate of the exploration density. For each new foraging trip, a robot selects its next search area from a local 3 X 3 neighborhood of grids probabilistically with the lowest visitation count, thus biasing exploration toward under-visited regions while maintaining stochasticity. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently outperforms the canonical centrally placed baseline foraging algorithm (CPFA). Compared to CPFA, the proposed method reduces the total collection time by up to 33% and improves collection efficiency by more than 48% during the final stage of the mission. These results indicate that the proposed strategy is robust, flexible, and scalable for near-complete and complete resource collection in robot swarms and can serve as a general enhancement for stochastic swarm foraging methods under limited onboard resources.

</details>


### [141] [TacO: Benchmarking Tactile Sensors for Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21976)
*Anya Zorin,Zilin Si,Myungsun Park,Junsung Park,Alexiy Buynitsky,Sachin Bhadang,Taejun Park,Sohee John Yoon,Yong-Lae Park,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel,Michael T. Tolley,Sha Yi,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性的任务驱动评估，揭示了四种不同模态的触觉传感器在复杂机器人操作任务中的差异化表现，并提出了针对具体任务选择合适传感器方案的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于视觉的演示学习在机器人操纵领域取得了成功，但在处理复杂、富含接触信息的操纵任务时仍存在局限性。目前业界缺乏关于如何针对特定操作任务选择最合适触觉传感器的系统性评估与实证研究。

Method: 提出了一套基于任务驱动的触觉传感器评估框架；针对视觉、声学、磁学和电阻式四种不同模态的触觉传感器，在三种典型任务（未知质量抓取、物体重新定向、插头插入）中训练独立的操纵策略，并分析空间分辨率、剪切感知和触觉表示等因素对任务性能的影响。

Result: 研究表明，并没有一种“万能”的触觉传感器，不同模态的传感器在不同任务中的表现存在显著差异。触觉传感器对任务性能的提升效果受限于传感器物理属性（如分辨率）与任务特性（如接触复杂度、材料摩擦）的匹配程度。

Conclusion: 触觉感知的有效性并非普适，而是高度依赖于传感器模态、物体材料特性以及具体的操作任务。该研究提供了选择触觉传感器的系统性指南，而非单一的最优方案。

Abstract: Vision-based learning from demonstrations has achieved remarkable success in enabling robots to perform manipulation tasks and high-level semantic reasoning, yet it remains insufficient for complex, contact-rich manipulation. While there is broad agreement that tactile sensing improves manipulation, there is no empirical guidance on which tactile sensors are best suited for which manipulation tasks. In this paper, we provide a systematic, task-driven evaluation of tactile sensors for robot manipulation and propose a framework for selecting and evaluating sensors based on manipulation policy performance. Separate manipulation policies are trained for tactile sensors of four distinct modalities: visual, acoustic, magnetic, and resistive, across three tasks: pick-and-place with unknown mass, object reorientation, and plug insertion. For each task, an analysis of how sensor properties such as spatial resolution, shear sensing, and tactile representation, and the inherent material friction affect task performances is done. Rather than tactile sensing being universally beneficial in the same way, our results show that the usefulness of tactile information depends strongly on sensor modality, material properties, and the specific manipulation tasks. All of the tactile sensors, code, data, and hardware setup will be publicly available on the project website.

</details>


### [142] [Industrial Dual-Arm Box Handling via Online Inertial Estimation and Convex Wrench Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22021)
*Kenzhi Iskandar Wong,Lin Yang,Qian Ying Lee,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于未知惯性物体处理的摩擦感知双臂搬运框架，通过在线估计物体参数并利用SOCP进行接触力优化，实现了稳定、无需微调的物体抓取搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人搬运物体时，物体的质量与质心往往未知，导致力矩平衡难以精确控制，从而引发打滑、跌落或过大挤压力等问题。如何在大惯性参数不确定性的情况下实现稳定搬运是本文解决的关键挑战。

Method: 1. 在线估计：根据测得的接触扳手（力与力矩）在线估计物体的质量及质心；2. 轨迹优化：包含离线轨迹修正阶段以降低几何约束下的碰撞风险；3. 最优控制：利用二阶锥规划（SOCP）在椭球摩擦极限面约束下计算各接触点的力与扭矩。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在不同质心配置下均能成功提升物体，且在保持稳定摩擦接触的同时，消除了对打滑规避参数和过大挤压力参数进行单独调节的需求。

Conclusion: 本文提出的摩擦感知双臂搬运框架能有效处理未知惯性参数的物体，通过将摩擦可行性作为硬约束并优化接触力，实现了稳定的抓取与搬运。

Abstract: Industrial robotic object handling often involves boxes and packages whose mass and center of mass are not known in advance. These uncertainties affect the force--moment balance required for stable lifting, and improper regulation of contact wrenches can lead to slip, object drop, orientation deviation, or excessive squeezing. This paper presents a friction-aware dual-arm box-handling framework for objects with unknown inertial properties. The proposed approach estimates the object mass and center of mass online from measured contact wrenches, and computes friction-feasible contact forces and torsional moments through a second-order cone program (SOCP) under ellipsoidal friction-limit-surface constraints. An offline trajectory refinement stage is also included to reduce undesired object--environment contact when geometric constraints are present. By enforcing friction feasibility as a hard constraint and minimizing contact effort within the feasible region, the framework achieves stable lifting without treating slip avoidance and excessive squeezing as separately tuned objectives. Experiments on a real dual-arm robotic system under different center-of-mass configurations demonstrate that the method lifts objects with unknown inertial properties while maintaining stable frictional contact.

</details>


### [143] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA是一种用于力主导机器人装配的自适应框架。它摒弃了基于参数的传统方法，转而学习6D语义接触特征，通过Transformer模块实现实时闭环适应，显著改善了装配任务在真实环境中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人自适应方法（如RMA）在处理力主导的装配任务时，利用原始模拟参数进行适应在现实环境中表现不佳（模拟与现实存在偏差），因此需要一种更稳健、基于语义信息的适应接口。

Method: 提出CoRMA框架，用6D语义接触上下文（接触发生、侧向啮合、引导过渡、接触方向、卡滞）替代原始的模拟器参数适应。使用因果Transformer适配器，通过历史力感应、本体感觉和动作数据在线推断上下文，并结合语义回归与力状态对比学习目标进行训练。

Result: 在Isaac Lab/Sim环境及Marvin机械臂上测试（PegInsert, GearMesh, NutThread任务），相比FORGE基线，CoRMA在存在目标姿态噪声的情况下，能显著提升在真实硬件上的成功率。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断是机器人装配任务中一种有效的可重用适应接口，有助于解决模拟到现实（Sim2Real）中的性能退化问题。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim~5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Pixels: Learning Invariant Rewards for Real-World Robotics From a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22123)
*Tengye Xu,Yangting Sun,Ziju Shen,Guanqi Chen,Zhen Fu,Chen yizhou,Hua Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从少量演示中学习不变符号奖励函数的框架，通过聚焦任务级行为不变性而非像素特征，有效解决了机器人强化学习在开放世界中的泛化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉的奖励模型容易过拟合到特定的像素分布，导致难以在开放世界环境（对象、位置、视角变化）中泛化，限制了强化学习在机器人操控中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出了一种包含两个耦合组件的框架：1. 结构化奖励公式：用于编码任务级策略和物理约束，并保持最优策略不变性；2. 混合符号-数值程序：能够在无需在线交互的情况下，从演示数据中提炼出这些行为不变量。

Result: 在8项Meta-World任务和3项Franka操控任务中，相比基准模型，该方法实现了更优的流程对齐和策略评估能力；在现实世界的分布外（OOD）实验中，实现了对位置、视角和对象变化的零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 该框架通过学习任务层面的“行为不变性”而非视觉特征拟合，证明了仅需极少量演示（5个）即可学习到通用的符号化奖励函数，且该奖励在开放世界操控任务中表现出极强的零样本泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Designing reward functions that generalize beyond controlled laboratory settings remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning for robotics. In open-world manipulation problems, a single task can appear in numerous variants through different object instances, positions, and camera viewpoints. Recent vision-based reward models tend to memorize specific pixel distributions and fail to generalize beyond their training conditions. To address this, we propose a framework that learns invariant symbolic reward functions from as few as five demonstrations. The insight is to shift from visual feature-fitting to the discovery of behavioral invariants: task-level properties that remain constant across diverse visual instantiations. The framework has two coupled components: a structural reward formulation that encodes task-level strategies and physical constraints while preserving optimal policy invariance, and a hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure that distills these invariants from demonstrations without online interaction. Experiments on eight Meta-World tasks and three Franka manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method achieves stronger process alignment and policy rollout ranking abilities compared to baselines, accelerating downstream policy learning. Three real-world out-of-distribution experiments further show that the same learned reward generalizes zero-shot to position, viewpoint, and object variations, enabling a single reward representation to be reused across diverse task variants in practice.

</details>


### [145] [Action with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22183)
*Weilong Guo,Yuchen Wang,Renping Zhou,Yunfeng Zhang,Rui Fang,Yue Meng,Wenda Xu,Yuan He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种 VLA 架构 AVP，通过将视觉感知与动作生成解耦，利用视觉原语作为中间表示，解决了传统端到端模型感知与控制耦合带来的效率及泛化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端 VLA 模型将指令理解、场景感知与运动控制强行合并在单一目标中，导致模型需要重复学习基础认知能力，限制了学习效率与泛化性。

Method: 提出 AVP (Action with Visual Primitives) 架构：将视觉语言模型（VLM）与流量匹配（flow-matching）运动执行器解耦。VLM 负责推断下一阶段目标并输出视觉原语 token，运动执行器基于这些 token 和末端执行器运动学反馈生成动作。

Result: 在真实机器人取放任务中，AVP 的成功率较 pi_0.5 提高了 27.61%，并在数据效率、空间组合泛化及物体级迁移方面表现优异。

Conclusion: AVP 通过引入视觉原语（Visual Primitives）作为接口，有效解耦了高级认知功能与底层运动控制，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率、数据效率及泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. A common design in current architectures maps language instructions and visual observations to actions in a single forward pass. While conceptually simple, this formulation entangles instruction comprehension, spatial scene understanding, and motor control within a single learning objective. As a result, the action expert must implicitly relearn cognitive and perceptual capabilities already present in the pretrained VLM, which can limit both learning efficiency and generalization. We introduce AVP (Action with Visual Primitives), an end-to-end architecture that implements this visual-primitive-centric interface: the VLM infers the next-stage target and emits visual-primitive tokens that condition a flow-matching action expert, with supervision derived from end-effector kinematics. Real-robot experiments on general pick-and-place tasks show that AVP improves the success rate by 27.61% over pi_0.5 and outperforms other recent methods, with consistent gains in data efficiency, spatial-compositional generalization, and object-level transfer.

</details>


### [146] [Learning A Unified Risk Map for Autonomous Driving in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22189)
*Jie Jia,Yaofeng Su,Zeyu Bao,Yun Hong,Bingzhao Gao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的风险地图框架，利用扩散模型生成对抗性遮挡场景并进行风险建模，显著提升了自动驾驶在遮挡环境下的风险预测与规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中遮挡区域的不确定性是巨大挑战；现有方法要么高估遮挡风险，要么无法在遮挡不确定性下准确预测轨迹。

Method: 1. 提出一种集成了交通流风险与碰撞风险的统一风险地图建模与学习框架；2. 引入基于扩散模型的场景生成框架，用于补充缺乏遮挡交互的场景，并生成对抗性场景以提高模型的鲁棒性；3. 将风险地图融入风险感知规划中。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上的实验表明，相比目前最先进的遮挡感知基准，该方法在最小避撞时间（TTC）上提升了0.78倍，平均TTC提升了1.67倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的统一风险地图建模与学习框架，有效解决了自动驾驶在部分可观测环境下的遮挡感知与风险评估问题，为风险感知规划提供了全面且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Occlusion-aware prediction remains a critical challenge in autonomous driving due to the inherent uncertainty of unobserved regions. Existing approaches either overestimate risk based on reachable states or struggle to predict accurate trajectories under high occlusion uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified risk map modeling and learning framework for partially observable environments. Our method integrates traffic flow risk and collision risk through spatiotemporal modeling, enabling fine-grained assessment of occlusion-induced hazards. To address the scarcity of scenarios involving occluded interactions, we introduce a diffusion-based scenario generation framework that produces realistic yet adversarial scenarios. We integrate the modeling and learning of a unified risk map into a framework that supports risk-aware planning under partial observability. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art occlusion-aware baseline, improving minimum time-to-collision by 0.78 times and average time-to-collision by 1.67 times. The proposed framework offers a comprehensive and practical solution for risk-aware planning in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [147] [Imagine2Real: Towards Zero-shot Humanoid-Object Interaction via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22272)
*Jiahe Chen,ZiRui Wang,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Xiaojie Niu,Weishuai Zeng,Tianfan Xue,Xiaowei Zhou,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine2Real是一个无需几何模型的零样本人形机器人交互框架，通过将交互建模为4D点轨迹并利用基础模型引导关键点追踪，有效规避了传统方法中的重定向难题与几何不对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人与物体交互（HOI）方法严重依赖高质量的3D数据，且在基于视频先验的方法中存在几何表达不一致（CAD模型依赖）以及重定向过程中的形态适配复杂问题，导致难以高效扩展到物理交互中。

Method: 1. 将机器人与物体的运动统一建模为4D点云轨迹；2. 采用关键点追踪器（Keypoints Tracker）仅针对关键部位（基座、手部、物体）进行追踪，跳过复杂的重定向流程；3. 利用行为基础模型（BFM）的潜空间作为搜索域，保证稀疏控制信号驱动下的步态自然度；4. 引入渐进式训练策略以简化奖励函数设计。

Result: Imagine2Real框架能够实现零样本（zero-shot）的物理部署，无需繁琐的几何模型建模或形态重定向，且能在稀疏控制信号下生成稳健且自然的交互行为。

Conclusion: Imagine2Real 通过统一的4D点轨迹表示和基于关键点的追踪策略，成功解决了全身体感交互（HOI）中的表示不对齐和重定向复杂性问题，实现了零样本（zero-shot）条件下的物理部署，为机器人交互学习提供了一种无需几何建模的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Whole-body Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data. While video generative priors offer a promising alternative, existing methods suffer from \textit{Representation Misalignment} due to their reliance on geometric priors (e.g., explicit CAD models), and \textit{Retargeting Complexity} arising from intensive morphing and morphological mismatch. We propose Imagine2Real, a zero-shot HOI framework for flexible, geometry-free interaction. To resolve misalignment, we formulate robot and object motions as unified 4D point trajectories. To overcome retargeting complexity, our Keypoints Tracker tracks only sparse critical points (base, hands, and object), entirely bypassing the error-amplifying retargeting process. To maintain natural gaits despite these sparse signals, we utilize the latent space of a Behavior Foundation Model (BFM) as the tracker's search domain. Using a progressive training strategy, Imagine2Real learns robust behaviors with simple tracking rewards, enabling zero-shot physical deployment within a motion capture(mocap) system.

</details>


### [148] [Spatial Memory for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22283)
*Pengteng Li,Weiyu Guo,He Zhang,Tiefu Cai,Xiao He,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型处理视野外目标的瓶颈，SOMA框架通过构建持久化空间记忆（Spatial Memory），使机器人能够跨越当前视觉限制进行推理，从而在复杂操作任务中实现更强的一致性和更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型多假设任务相关目标始终处于视野内，导致模型在处理视野外目标时表现出极强的被动性和脆弱性，难以完成多步或复杂的真实世界操作任务。

Method: SOMA框架由三大核心组件构成：1) 空间记忆构建（Spatial Memory Construction）：通过扫描方式将多视角观测整合为统一的空间语义表征；2) 动态记忆更新（Dynamic Memory Refinement）：确保长时程空间语义的全局一致性；3) 上下文记忆检索（Contextual Memory Retrieval）：在操作任务中按需检索相关的空间位置提示。

Result: 在5个挑战性的真实世界视野外操作任务中表现优异，显著提高了成功率。此外，在RoboCasa GR1和SimplerEnv基准测试中，SOMA在全观察设定下依然保持了优越性，并实现了更快的定位、更少的寻找动作及接近一次成功的抓取表现。

Conclusion: SOMA框架通过引入持久化空间记忆，有效解决了VLA模型在处理视野外目标时的局限性，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率与智能化水平，为处理复杂、长时程的真实世界机器人操作任务奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce SOMA, the Spatial Memory framework for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Most existing VLAs implicitly assume that task-relevant objects are always visible, leading to brittle and reactive behaviors when targets fall outside the camera's field of view. SOMA addresses this limitation by equipping VLAs with a persistent spatial memory constructed from multi-view observations acquired via a movable head camera, enabling reasoning beyond the current visual frustum. The framework consists of three components: Spatial Memory Construction, which aggregates angular-wise observations into a unified spatial-semantic representation through scanning; Dynamic Memory Refinement, which maintains global consistency over time; and Contextual Memory Retrieval, which activates instruction-relevant spatial cues during manipulation.
