We evaluate SOMA on five challenging real-world out-of-vision manipulation tasks, including multi-step and dual-arm scenarios where target objects are initially invisible. Experimental results show that SOMA not only improves task success rates, but also induces qualitatively different manipulation behaviors, with faster target localization, reduced viewpoint search, and near one-shot grasping under partial observability. Additional experiments on RoboCasa GR1 and SimplerEnv further validate the effectiveness of SOMA's memory design under conventional fully observable settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [149] [How can reasoning capability empower the AI copilot robot in endoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22322)
*Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将基于视觉-语言-动作模型的推理能力引入内镜手术副手机器人，旨在提升机器人的认知协作水平，减轻外科医生负担并增强手术安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尽管通用人工智能的推理能力已在复杂逻辑推演和机器人决策方面取得显著进展，但其在内窥镜手术领域的应用及潜力尚未得到充分探索；手术中迫切需要能够缓解外科医生认知负荷并处理术中不确定性的认知型机器人系统。

Method: 提出一种基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的人工智能副手机器人架构，旨在通过跨模态整合与意图理解实现手术中的逻辑推理。

Result: 探讨了推理能力在内镜手术中的应用价值，重点在于通过整合多模态线索、解读手术意图及推断隐性组织动态，解决术中复杂任务的决策瓶颈。

Conclusion: 在内镜手术中引入基于推理的自主性，有望将人工智能副手从简单的“被动执行者”转变为“认知协作伙伴”，从而提高临床实践的精度、安全性和可持续性。

Abstract: Reasoning capability has significantly advanced complex logical inference and robotic decision-making in general domains. However, its potential in the Artificial Intelligence (AI) copilot robot-particularly implemented based on the Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model-remains unexplored in endoscopic surgery. Effective reasoning should enable AI copilot robots to integrate multimodal cues, interpret surgical intent, and infer hidden tissue dynamics, thereby alleviating intraoperative uncertainty and cognitive burden on surgeons. Properly implemented, reasoning-driven autonomy can transform AI copilot robots from reactive executors into cognitive collaborators, enhancing precision, safety, and sustainability in clinical practice.

</details>


### [150] [Real-Time Auto-Optimization in Unknown Environments via Structure-Exploiting Dual Control for Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22431)
*Shiying Dong,Haoyang Yang,Qiwei Liu,Wen-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对DCEE计算复杂度高的问题，本文利用其目标函数的“凸复合”结构，开发了一种基于推广高斯-牛顿法的快速数值求解算法，实现了微秒级的在线快速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双重控制用于探索与利用（DCEE）方法计算负担过重，未能充分利用其目标函数的特殊数值结构，难以满足实时在线自优化需求。

Method: 提出了一种结合凸内层损失函数与非线性残差映射的“凸复合”结构（Convex-over-nonlinear structure），利用推广的高斯-牛顿 Hessian 近似方法，仅对非线性残差进行线性化，将DCEE求解转化为结构化的凸优化问题，支持快速在线计算。

Result: 在车辆巡航自优化应用中，该方法相比现有方法性能更优，计算速度提升约一个数量级，在车载嵌入式CPU上的最大计算时间仅为83微秒。

Conclusion: 本文提出的结构化数值求解方法显著降低了DCEE的计算负担，证明了高性能、低延迟的在线自优化控制在车辆嵌入式系统中的可行性。

Abstract: This paper develops a fast numerical dual control for exploration and exploitation (DCEE) method to address auto-optimization problems in unknown environments. In auto-optimization problems, the optimal operating condition is unknown a priori and may vary with the environment. As in classical dual control techniques, computational burden remains a major concern in DCEE for active learning. Existing DCEE methods provide a principled exploration-exploitation objective, but mainly realized through standard optimization packages or explicit gradient-type update laws, where the numerical structure of the DCEE has not been fully exploited. This paper shows that the reward function in DCEE has an inherent convex-over-nonlinear structure, where the exploitation and exploration terms form a unified nonlinear residual map equipped with a convex outer loss. Benefiting from this structure, a structure-exploiting numerical method is developed by linearizing only the nonlinear residual map while preserving the convex outer loss. Thus, each subproblem is transformed into a structured convex form that can be solved reliably. The resulting generalized Gauss-Newton Hessian approximation is positive semidefinite and depends only on first-order derivatives, thereby supporting fast online computation. The proposed method is evaluated on a vehicle cruising auto-optimization problem and compared with existing methods. Simulation and hardware-in-the-loop experimental results show that the proposed method improves control performance and achieves a speedup of approximately one order of magnitude, with a microsecond-level maximum computation time of only 83 μs on a typical vehicle embedded CPU.

</details>


### [151] [Terminal Constraint Model Predictive Control for Image-Based Visual Servoing of UAVs with Kalman Filter-Based Moment Loss Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22443)
*X. Wang,Y. Cao,W. L. W. Leong,Y. R. Tan,S. Huang,S. H. R. Teo,C. Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对UAV在IBVS控制中面临的约束限制与特征丢失问题，本文提出了一种融合终端约束MPC与卡尔曼滤波的控制策略，有效增强了系统的稳定性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于图像的视觉伺服（IBVS）在无人机（UAV）应用中存在两个主要缺陷：一是在输入/状态约束下靠近目标时难以保持闭环稳定性；二是剧烈运动导致特征观测间歇性丢失时引发控制失效。

Method: 1. 提出终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）框架，通过引入终端状态约束和终端代价来处理控制与状态约束，确保闭环稳定与收敛性。2. 引入基于卡尔曼滤波器（KF）的状态预测机制，在视觉特征短时丢失时估计图像矩，保障控制延续性。

Result: 通过实时无人机视觉伺服实验证明，该方法提升了视觉伺服的收敛性能、闭环稳定性，并在视觉信息部分缺失的情况下保持了控制的连续性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了一种结合终端约束MPC和卡尔曼滤波的IBVS框架，有效解决了UAV视觉伺服中的约束处理和观测缺失问题，并验证了其在实际系统中的鲁棒性与稳定性。

Abstract: Image-Based Visual Servoing (IBVS) provides an efficient vision-guided control paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) by directly regulating image-space errors. However, conventional IBVS controllers are vulnerable to two critical issues: loss of closed-loop stability near the target due to input and state constraints, and control failure caused by intermittent loss of moment-based visual features under aggressive motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a terminal-constraint model predictive control (TC-MPC) framework for IBVS, integrated with a Kalman filter (KF)-based state-prediction mechanism. The TC-MPC explicitly incorporates terminal-state constraints and a terminal cost into the IBVS error dynamics, ensuring recursive feasibility, improved convergence behavior, and closed-loop stability under control and state constraints. In parallel, the Kalman filter predicts the temporal evolution of image moments during short-term visual degradation, enabling the controller to preserve control continuity when moment measurements are partially unavailable. The proposed approach is validated through real-time UAV visual servoing experiments.

</details>


### [152] [Steins;Gate Drive: Semantic Safety Arbitration over Structured Futures for Latency-Decoupled LLM Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22456)
*Anjie Qiu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SteinsGateDrive通过将LLM的语义预测与底层实时运行环境解耦，利用“世界线”预生成和运行时安全契约验证，在消除推理延迟的同时确保了自动驾驶的实时安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端部署的大模型（LLM）推理延迟过高，超过了自动驾驶车辆所需的实时控制窗口，且现有的学习型世界模型通常将预测与决策过程深度耦合，难以兼顾语义推理与实时响应的要求。

Method: 提出了一种名为SteinsGateDrive的架构，将LLM规划与实时控制解耦。主要机制包括：1. 预先生成三种类型的“世界线”（alpha标称未来、beta交互反事实、gamma危险压力测试）；2. 将选定的预测封装为具有有效期和中止条件的“战略预测”（StrategicForecast）；3. 运行时的原子谓词（atom-predicate）检查器实时监控安全契约有效性，当契约失效时触发重规划。

Result: 在标准高速公路测试中，使用GPT-5.4 mini模型，该架构将有效延迟从1秒视野下的+3.07秒降低至4秒视野下的-0.01秒，同时保持了无碰撞的安全边界。

Conclusion: SteinsGateDrive通过将LLM推理与实时控制解耦，有效解决了云端LLM在高实时性自动驾驶中的延迟瓶颈。该架构证明了利用语义预测和实时安全契约验证，可以在保证无碰撞安全的前提下，显著优化规划的时效性。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted LLM driver agents provide useful semantic judgments, but their inference latency exceeds stepwise vehicle-control windows. Learned world models predict futures, but they usually keep future generation and action selection inside large coupled loops. We present SteinsGateDrive, a latency-decoupled planner-runtime architecture in which the worldline metaphor from the eponymous story names one plausible consequence of an intervention: the LLM selects counterfactual driving futures before the final control instant, and a runtime reuses the selected forecast only while safety contracts remain valid. The generator builds three world-line roles: alpha nominal ego-conditioned futures, beta interaction counterfactuals around nearby vehicles, and gamma hazard-stress futures such as braking, cut-ins, or blocked corridors. The selected branch becomes a typed StrategicForecast with horizon, validity/abort conditions, fallback, and authority. On a within-subject, matched-seed normal-highway protocol with 10 seeds and 20 steps, GPT-5.4 mini reduces effective lag from +3.07 s at 1-second horizon to -0.01 s at 4-second horizon while preserving the measured no-collision safety boundary. The architecture's safety contribution comes from the atom-predicate runtime check, not from the drift score, which functions as a refresh-frequency knob.

</details>


### [153] [Quantifying Full-Body Immersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22521)
*Alihan Bakir,Ekrem Yüksel,Fabio Zuliani,Neil Chennoufi,Francesco Bruno,Jamie Paik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化机器人表面系统，通过全身体感交互（FBI）将虚拟环境转化为可物理感知的大规模动态空间，重塑了人类与虚拟世界交互和共生的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了打破传统虚拟现实中交互脱节的局限，解决如何通过物理层面实现虚拟与现实世界的无缝融合，并推动人机环境的深度共生。

Method: 提出了一种基于模块化机器人表面单元（Modular Robotic Surface Units）的分布式系统，通过实时调整空间形态、力和运动反馈，实现覆盖大范围空间的多人全身体感交互（FBI）。

Result: 构建了一个能够根据用户行为实时动态调整的物理仿真环境，实现了从个人到多用户、从视听反馈到全身体感交互的沉浸式体验，有效提升了虚拟环境的真实感与交互深度。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于模块化机器人表面单元的可扩展、自适应全身体感交互平台，通过音频、视觉、触觉和全身体感（FBI）的深度整合，实现了真实世界与虚拟世界的共生，为未来人类空间交互奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: Humanity is at the forefront of yet another digital revolution, where the lines between real and virtual worlds are dissolving, reshaping how we perceive and interact with our surroundings. In this context, we introduce a transformative paradigm for immersive virtual experiences centered around whole-body kinetic interactions. Our approach redefines immersion through three distinct levels: audio-visual immersion, capturing sensory realism; physical immersion, delivering haptic feedback; and full-body immersion (FBI), where dynamic bodily interaction integrates seamlessly with virtual environments. At the core of this innovation lies a scalable, distributable platform based on modular robotic surface units inspired by the adaptive designs of nature. These units enable the rendering of immersive environments at any scale, from intimate personal experiences to expansive multi-user settings, dynamically adapting to interactions in real-time. The modular system distributes force, shape, and motion feedback throughout entire spaces, replicating the physical characteristics of the environment and enabling new depth of engagement through FBI. By combining scalability, adaptability, and dynamic physical engagement, this framework bridges the gap between real and virtual worlds. It offers an unprecedented level of immersion where users can engage their entire bodies in symbiotic interactions with the virtual space. This work not only advances immersive technology but also redefines how humans and virtual environments coexist, setting a foundation for a new era of human-environment synthesis.

</details>


### [154] [SE3Kit: A Lightweight Python Library for Specialized Geometric Primitives in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22633)
*Daniyal Maroufi,Omid Rezayof,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SE3Kit 是一个专注于 SE(3) 和 SO(3) 群运算的轻量级 Python 库，通过仅依赖 NumPy 实现数学严谨性，旨在解决现有机器人库在嵌入式部署和快速原型开发中的复杂性与性能间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Python 机器人库要么过于庞大（如 SpatialMath, PyPose），要么缺乏机器人专用的数学严谨性（如 SciPy），导致在嵌入式和轻量级场景下难以使用。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于纯 Python 和 NumPy 的库，专注于 Lie 群运算，去除了高性能计算框架（如 PyTorch/TensorFlow）或大型机器人框架的复杂依赖。

Result: 提供了一款轻量级、算子完备且易于集成的 SE(3) 和 SO(3) 运算工具库。

Conclusion: SE3Kit 填补了机器人领域对轻量级、数学严谨的 SE(3)/SO(3) 库的需求，为嵌入式部署、教育及快速原型开发提供了一个高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The Python robotics ecosystem faces a challenge: while many libraries exist for rigid body transformations, few are both lightweight and mathematically strict. This paper introduces SE3Kit, a lightweight Python library efficient operations on the Special Euclidean Group SE(3) and the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3). Unlike established frameworks that require heavy dependencies (e.g., SpatialMath, PyPose) or general tools that lack robotics-specific features (e.g., SciPy), SE3Kit targets the gap between these extremes. It is designed for embedded deployment, rapid prototyping, and education while providing rigorous mathematical implementation. It provides a pure-Python, NumPy-only implementation of Lie Group operations, without the overhead of deep learning or other visualization software.

</details>


### [155] [Symmetries Here and There, Combined Everywhere: Cross-space Symmetry Compositions in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22639)
*Loizos Hadjiloizou,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨空间对称性组合框架，通过微分几何方法将配置空间与任务空间的多种对称性统一起来，利用联合等变性显著提高了机器人策略的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习方法通常孤立地处理单一对称性，未能充分利用机器人机械结构与其任务属性中同时存在的多种对称性，限制了算法的泛化潜力。

Method: 提出了一种跨空间对称性组合（cross-space symmetry compositions）框架。利用前向运动学映射的微分几何结构，实现对称性在配置空间与任务空间之间的双向映射（descending/lifting），从而实现多种对称性的联合等变性（joint equivariance）。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实双臂机器人实验中验证了该框架，结果表明联合利用多种对称性可显著提升策略的泛化表现。

Conclusion: 通过在统一表示空间内结合多种对称性，能够显著提升机器人策略的学习效率和泛化能力。实验证明，该框架在处理复杂任务（如双臂机器人操作）时优于孤立处理对称性的方法。

Abstract: Robots exhibit a rich variety of symmetries arising from their mechanical structure and the properties of their tasks. Although many robotics problems exhibit several symmetries simultaneously, existing approaches typically treat them in isolation, failing to exploit their combined potential. This paper introduces cross-space symmetry compositions, a framework for learning robot policies that are jointly equivariant to multiple symmetries across configuration and task spaces. Leveraging the differential-geometric structure of the forward kinematics map, we both descend symmetries from configuration to task space and lift symmetries from task to configuration space, enabling their composition within a unified representation space. We validate our framework on simulated and real-world experiments on a dual-arm robot, demonstrating that jointly leveraging multiple symmetries yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [156] [Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22748)
*Ismail Geles,Leonard Bauersfeld,Markus Wulfmeier,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多智能体强化学习训练四旋翼无人机，通过在复杂动态的竞技环境中学习群体交互，实现了超人类的竞速表现与更高的安全性，证明了多智能体交互训练是通往鲁棒性机器人共存的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主系统在孤立环境表现卓越，但在共享、动态的现实世界中表现脆弱，主要原因是传统的单智能体范式忽略了其他参与者的交互，无法实现有效的协作。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习（MARL）与联赛制自我博弈（League-based self-play），在高速四旋翼无人机竞速场景中进行训练，模型需学习处理复杂的空气动力学交互和策略性机动。

Result: 无人机在超过22 m/s的高速多智能体竞速中击败了人类冠军选手，并将碰撞率较最先进的单智能体基线降低了50%，同时实现了与人类交互的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 机器人安全且鲁棒的共存之路不在于孤立的安全约束，而在于通过多智能体交互的严苛需求进行训练。

Abstract: Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl

</details>


### [157] [GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22812)
*Wenxuan Guo,Ziyuan Li,Meng Zhang,Yichen Liu,Yimeng Dong,Chuxi Xu,Yunfei Wei,Ze Chen,Erjin Zhou,Jianjiang Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GesVLA 引入手势作为多模态输入，通过双 VLM 架构和合成数据流水线，解决了 VLA 模型在复杂环境下空间定位模糊的问题，提升了机器人操纵的精准度与交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型主要依赖文本指令，在处理多相似物体的复杂场景时存在空间歧义，难以准确引导机器人执行任务。

Method: 1. 架构：提出双 VLM 架构，将手势特征直接编码进潜在空间以实现感知与动作的深度耦合。
2. 数据：构建手势数据自动生成流水线（在真实场景图像上渲染手势模型），提供多样化的运动模式和指向标注。
3. 训练：采用两阶段训练策略，使模型同时具备手势感知与动作预测能力。

Result: 在受控块操纵以及产品挑选等真实场景实验中，GesVLA 在目标定位准确性和人机交互效率方面均表现出显著提升，尤其是在复杂和混乱的环境中。

Conclusion: GesVLA 通过引入手势作为辅助指令模态，有效解决了 VLA 模型在复杂场景下因文本描述模糊而导致的定位与操作难题，提升了机器人的人机交互效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于金融欺诈检测的 TCT 框架，该模型能通过自监督学习提取有效的行为表示，虽然目前未能显著优于手动特征工程，但展示了减少人工干预的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决金融交易欺诈检测中对复杂、耗时的手动特征工程（Domain-engineered features）的依赖，通过学习嵌入自动捕捉序列中的上下文时间动态。

Method: 提出 Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT) 架构；采用自监督对比学习目标（Self-supervised Contrastive Objective）生成交易序列的行为嵌入；将嵌入作为特征输入梯度提升分类器（Gradient Boosting Classifier）进行性能评估。

Result: TCT 生成的嵌入在独立测试中达到 0.8644 AUC，显示其具备捕捉时间结构的能力；但在与专家经验特征结合使用时，仅达到 0.9205 AUC，微弱低于基准线的 0.9245 AUC，表明其学习到的表示与现有特征存在高度冗余。

Conclusion: TCT 是金融交易时间序列表示学习的一种有前景的框架，能够自动捕捉行为模式并近似人工特征工程的效果，虽尚未超越强基准模型，但为降低对特征工程的依赖提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [159] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出利用小型语言模型作为“科研想法验证器”，通过 SFT 和 RLVR 训练，使其能够准确预测研究想法的实验成功率，从而克服自动化科学研究中想法评估的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型加速科学假说的生成与实现，如何高效评估和筛选海量AI生成的实验想法，避免冗长的无意义实验，成为了科研自动化过程中的核心瓶颈。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：使用 PapersWithCode 构建包含 11,488 个研究想法对的数据集，基于客观实验结果进行对比。 2. 模型训练：采用监督微调（SFT）大幅提升基准模型性能；引入基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），通过探索潜在推理路径实现推理过程的可解释化。 3. 评估指标：通过对比实验、消融实验以及跨领域时间切分和独立测试集进行稳健性验证。

Result: 1. 经过 SFT 的模型准确率达到 77.1%，显著优于 GPT-4 (61.1%) 和基准模型 (30%)。 2. 通过 RLVR 训练的模型在保持 71.35% 准确率的同时，具备了可解释的推理 justification。 3. 模型表现出极强的泛化能力，能够抵御表层启发式偏差，在不同领域和时间维度上均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 研究证明，经过专门训练（SFT/RLVR）的计算高效型小型语言模型，已能够有效地对科学研究想法的实证成功率进行预测，为实现自动化科学发现提供了一种可扩展且客观的验证机制。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [160] [The Attribution Impossibility: No Feature Ranking Is Faithful, Stable, and Complete Under Collinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21492)
*Drake Caraker,Bryan Arnold,David Rhoads*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过形式化验证揭示了XAI中的归因“不可能性定理”：当特征存在共线性时，模型无法同时兼顾忠实性、稳定性和完整性。研究提出了DASH方法来优化稳定性，并证明了现有SHAP类审计在共线性下极易失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决可解释人工智能（XAI）中特征归因方法在面对共线性特征时，容易产生不稳定、不可靠排序结果的问题，并试图定义归因方法的理论极限。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明了在共线性条件下，特征排序的不可能性及 attribution ratio 的量化特性；2. DASH算法：提出了一种协同SHAP归因的集成聚合方法；3. 形式化验证：使用Lean 4定理证明器，完成了305条定理的自动化验证；4. 实证分析：对77个公开数据集进行大规模稳定性调查。

Result: 1. 发现归因方法存在“忠实-完整”与“集成方法（DASH）”的二分法；2. 证明了在共线性下，大部分归因方法（如Lasso、梯度提升）存在严重的稳定性问题；3. DASH被证明在无偏聚合中达到Cramer-Rao方差界；4. 研究发现68%的公开数据集存在归因不稳定性，且表明基于SHAP的公平性审计在共线性下不可靠。

Conclusion: 在存在共线性特征时，特征归因方法存在内在的“不可能性”权衡——无法同时实现忠实性、稳定性和完整性。通过DASH（多样化SHAP聚合）方法可以在稳定性和归因合理性之间取得Pareto最优解。该研究不仅确立了XAI的归因设计空间二分法，还为公平性审计的局限性提供了形式化证明。

Abstract: No feature ranking can be simultaneously faithful, stable, and complete when features are collinear. For collinear pairs, ranking reduces to a coin flip. We prove this impossibility, quantify it for four model classes, resolve it via ensemble averaging (DASH), and machine-verify it with 305 Lean 4 theorems. We characterize the complete attribution design space: exactly two families of methods exist -- faithful-complete methods (unstable, with rankings that flip up to 50% of the time) and ensemble methods like DASH (stable, reporting ties for symmetric features) -- and no method lies outside this dichotomy. The impossibility is quantitative: the attribution ratio diverges as 1/(1-rho^2) for gradient boosting, is infinite for Lasso, and converges for random forests. DASH (Diversified Aggregation of SHAP) is provably Pareto-optimal among unbiased aggregations, achieving the Cramer-Rao variance bound with a tight ensemble size formula. In a survey of 77 public datasets, 68% exhibit attribution instability. Switching to conditional SHAP does not escape the impossibility when features have equal causal effects. The framework includes practical diagnostics -- a Z-test workflow and single-model screening tool -- and has direct consequences for fairness auditing: SHAP-based proxy discrimination audits are provably unreliable under collinearity. The design space theorem, diagnostics, and impossibility are mechanically verified in Lean 4 (305 theorems from 16 axioms, 0 sorry) -- to our knowledge, the first formally verified impossibility in explainable AI.

</details>


### [161] [Double descent for least-squares interpolation on contaminated data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21494)
*Tino Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，即便在受污染的数据集上，高度超参数化的最小二乘模型依然能够通过“双下降”现象实现优秀的泛化性能，甚至优于传统的鲁棒性估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计学认为过参数化会导致过拟合，但“双下降”现象表明模型复杂度增加反而能提升泛化性能；同时，在存在污染数据的情况下，传统上需要鲁棒估计器来避免偏差。本文旨在探究在有污染的数据中，过参数化模型是否依然存在“双下降”，以及其性能与鲁棒估计器相比如何。

Method: 通过在线性回归设置下，对比最小二乘插值估计器（高非鲁棒性）与多种鲁棒统计估计器在受污染（存在异常值）训练数据上的泛化性能。

Result: 研究发现大模型复杂度确实引发了双下降现象，使最小二乘插值法在处理受损分布时取得了卓越的泛化表现，超越了既有的鲁棒替代方案。

Conclusion: 在超参数化（即模型复杂度极高）的情况下，最小二乘插值估计器能够表现出“双下降”现象，其泛化性能甚至优于专门设计的鲁棒估计器。这挑战了传统统计学中认为高复杂度导致过拟合以及鲁棒性在存在污染数据时至关重要的观点。

Abstract: Overparametrized models can exhibit an excellent generalization performance, although they should be prone to overfitting according to classical statistical theory. The discovery of the "double descent", indicating that the generalization error decreases after a certain model complexity has been reached, opened a new line of research. Robust statistics considers statistical estimation on contaminated data, which, due to assumptions that do not hold on real data, let data points appear as outliers w.r.t. the assumed "ideal" distribution, potentially severely distorting any classical estimator. We address the question whether a double descent phenomenon can be observed in a linear regression setting with contaminated training data. We compare the performance of the highly non-robust least-squares interpolation estimator with several robust alternatives. It turns out that large overparametrization indeed allows for a double descent phenomenon, resulting in a very good generalization performance of the least-squares interpolator, surpassing that of the robust alternatives.

</details>


### [162] [HealthCraft: A Reinforcement Learning Safety Environment for Emergency Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21496)
*Brandon Dent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HealthCraft，这是首个用于急诊医学的轨迹级安全评估强化学习环境。研究发现现有的顶尖模型（如 Claude 和 GPT）在处理多步临床工作流时表现堪忧且安全性不足，且目前的评估框架尚未成熟到可直接用于模型训练的奖励机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态医学问答基准测试无法捕捉急诊医疗环境中的真实失败模式（如：轨迹级安全性崩溃、工具误用及在高压下的能力丧失）。现有架构缺乏对前沿模型在临床工作流中安全性评估的支撑。

Method: 构建了一个基于 FHIR R4 标准的强化学习评估环境（HealthCraft），适配自 Corecraft。该环境包含14种实体类型、3,987个种子实体、24个 MCP 工具，并定义了双层评分准则。通过发布195个跨6个类别的任务，利用确定性的 LLM-judge 进行评估，并对模型在长轨迹任务中的表现及安全性进行压力测试。

Result: 在两款前沿模型测试中，Claude Opus 和 GPT-5.4 的安全失败率分别高达 27.5% 和 34.0%。在多步工作流任务中，模型性能几乎崩溃（表现趋近 0%）。此外，实验发现评估基础设施的微小差异会影响模型强弱的排名结论。

Conclusion: HealthCraft 强调了在急诊医学背景下评估语言模型必须关注轨迹级安全。研究证明现有的语言模型在多步工作流中表现极差，且评估基础设施的保真度对模型能力评估的结论有决定性影响。作者指出当前的评估环境由于易被“博弈”（gameability），尚不能直接作为强化学习的训练奖励信号。

Abstract: Frontier language models are being deployed into clinical workflows faster than the infrastructure to evaluate them safely. Static medical-QA benchmarks miss the failure modes that matter in emergency medicine: trajectory-level safety collapse, tool misuse, and capitulation under sustained clinical pressure. We present HealthCraft, the first public reinforcement-learning environment that rewards trajectory-level safety under realistic emergency-medicine conditions, adapted from Corecraft. It is built on a FHIR R4 world state with 14 entity types and 3,987 seed entities, exposes 24 MCP tools, and defines a dual-layer rubric that zeroes reward whenever any safety-critical criterion is violated. We release 195 tasks across six categories, graded against 2,255 binary criteria (515 safety-critical); a post-hoc 10-task negative-class slate extends this to 205 tasks and 2,337 criteria. V8 results on two frontier models show Claude Opus 4.6 at Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4] and GPT-5.4 at 12.6% [10.2-15.6], with safety-failure rates of 27.5% and 34.0%. On multi-step workflows - the closest proxy to real emergency care - performance collapses to near zero (Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%) despite partial competence on individual steps. Six infrastructure bugs fixed between pilots v2 and v8 re-ordered which model "looks stronger," evidence that infrastructure fidelity is part of the measurement. A deterministic LLM-judge overlay bounds evaluator noise, and a 60-run negative-class smoke pilot shows the reward signal is not drop-in training-safe: restraint criteria pass at 0.929 prevalence, a gameability an eval harness can tolerate but a training reward cannot. We scaffold coupling to a Megatron+SGLang+GRPO loop per Corecraft Section 5.2 and leave training-reward ablations as future work. Environment, tasks, rubrics, and harness are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [163] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lumberjack 是一种新型差分隐私随机森林算法，通过“构建大树后严格剪枝”的策略以及创新的层级重击检测技术，有效平衡了隐私损耗与预测性能，达到了领域内新的最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私（DP）随机森林方法在处理敏感表格数据时，因隐私保护需求导致模型实用性（Utility）急剧下降，使其在实际应用中难以落地。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Lumberjack 的算法。该方法首先构建超大规模的随机决策树，随后应用侵入式（aggressive）的隐私保护剪枝（Pruning）策略以保留高置信度节点；同时引入了一种针对层级数据的创新型 $(\varepsilon,δ)$-差分隐私重击（Heavy Hitter）检测算法。

Result: 实验表明，Lumberjack 在基准数据集上持续领先于现有的 DP 随机森林方法，在实际隐私预算下取得了显著的性能提升，树的深度和表现力得到了大幅增强。

Conclusion: Lumberjack 通过创新的剪枝策略和层级数据重击寻找算法，显著提升了差分隐私随机森林的实用性，为后续高维敏感表格数据的隐私保护研究提供了重要范式。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [164] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对疾病预测中数据异质与不平衡难题，研究提出了yvsoucom-iterkit框架，通过全链路日志追踪揭示了流水线中组件的冗余性与关键驱动力，证明了集成模型可通过优化核心组件实现高稳健性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病风险预测方法面临数据特征异质性、样本量有限以及严重的类不平衡挑战，导致预测模型的准确性和可复现性难以保障。现有的自动化工具往往缺乏透明度与可解释性，难以评估组件对结果的具体贡献。

Method: 提出了一种名为 yvsoucom-iterkit 的确定性、日志驱动型自动机器学习（AutoML）框架。该框架将流水线优化处理为完全可复现的配置级系统，通过记录每个流水线的日志实体，实现了组件归因、交互作用分析、组件相似性评估及跨种子（cross-seed）稳健性验证。在Pima Diabetes和Stroke数据集上进行了超过18,000种配置的实验。

Result: 1. 识别出关键组件：数据增强（0.454）、模型选择（0.198）和类不平衡处理（0.101）是影响Pima数据集表现的关键；而在Stroke数据集上，类不平衡处理的影响显著（0.406）。2. 发现组件冗余：部分特征选择与增强技术表现出高相似性（RMS距离极低）。3. 性能表现：使用集成模型在Pima（Weighted-F1 0.89）和Stroke（Weighted-F1 0.94）上实现了强劲且稳定的性能。4. 稳健性：集成模型在跨种子分析中展现出比SVM更低的性能变异性。

Conclusion: AutoML的优化过程可以聚焦于一小部分高影响力的核心组件，而非穷尽所有搜索空间。有效的组件选择和集成策略对于解决疾病风险预测中的异质性、小样本及类不平衡问题至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [165] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文开发了SepsisAI-Orchestrator平台，通过优化容器编排与副本伸缩配置，解决了临床AI模型落地生产环境时存在的并发与延迟难题，并发现了临床AI推理中存在性能最优的U型扩展规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型虽预测性能优秀，但因数据异构性、部署流程缺乏标准化以及研究原型与医院实时/低延迟要求之间的矛盾，导致临床转化受阻。需建立适配医院生产环境的部署基础设施。

Method: 构建了一个开源模块化部署平台，整合了FHIR架构预处理、NoSQL存储、基于LightGBM的容器化推理服务以及Streamlit可视化面板，并利用Docker和Kubernetes进行编排。利用k6工具在50-1000个并发用户下进行压力测试，量化了副本数量对延迟和失败率的影响。

Result: 在12线程CPU上，将副本数量从3扩展至12可实现p95延迟从3.3秒降至1.41秒（缩减57.3%），并消除请求失败；过量配置（24或48个副本）会因调度竞争导致性能下降。该研究首次量化了临床AI推理负载下的U型扩展效应。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了SepsisAI-Orchestrator平台，确立了应对临床环境负载需求的部署最佳实践。研究发现存在“U型”扩展行为，即副本数量与物理主机CPU线程数的匹配对降低延迟和保证系统稳定性至关重要，盲目的大规模扩展会导致性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [166] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualOptim+ 是一种用于大语言模型遗忘任务的新型优化框架，通过引入基础状态与增量状态的动态调整机制，有效解决了遗忘与模型能力保持间的冲突，并提供高效的量化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）的遗忘任务中，遗忘特定数据与保留模型通用能力之间往往存在梯度冲突，现有的优化方法难以在两者之间取得理想的平衡。

Method: 提出了 DualOptim+ 框架，核心思想是将优化状态拆分为“基础状态”（共享表示）和“增量状态”（特定任务残差），并根据梯度方向冲突动态平衡两者。同时发布了 8-bit 量化版本以降低计算开销。

Result: 在虚构数据遗忘、真实场景遗忘、安全对齐以及多任务学习等任务中，DualOptim+ 均表现出优于基线方法的性能指标，证明了其在权衡不同优化目标方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DualOptim+ 通过解耦和动态桥接遗忘与保留任务的梯度，有效解决了大语言模型遗忘（Unlearning）中的多目标冲突问题，实现了遗忘效果与模型表现的更优平衡，并提供低内存消耗的量化版本。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [167] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对 LLM 基准测试中的数据污染难题，提出了 JECS 方法，通过共形推断技术实现了多模型联合去污染，并提供严格的全局假阳性控制证明，确保了模型基准测试的公平性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型（LLM）的基准测试存在严重的数据污染问题，现有基于得分的检测方法缺乏理论保证，而传统的单模型共形预测方法无法解决多模型评估中跨模型比较不公平的问题。

Method: 提出“联合包络共形选择”（Joint Envelope Conformal Selection, JECS）。通过计算各模型共形 p 值并取每项的最大值，构建最大 p 值零分布的保守包络（通过数据驱动阈值重建），并结合自适应 Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) 过程进行多模型基准的联合选择。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，JECS 在保持设定的全局污染控制率（GCR）的前提下，表现出了比最大 p 值基准线（max-p baseline）更高的检测能力。

Conclusion: JECS 是一种有效的多模型基准去污染方法，能够在提供全局假阳性控制（GCR）理论保证的同时，相比传统方法实现了更高的检测效能（power），从而确保了不同模型间公平、可靠的基准测试。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [168] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对协变量偏移下的置信度校准难题，本文提出了“期望一致性条件”并设计了高效的无监督ECL损失函数，实现了无需目标域标签的高精度校准，且计算稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法多基于IID假设，在协变量偏移（covariate shift）下表现不佳；且现有偏移校准方法主要依赖不稳定的重要性加权（importance weighting），容易在密度比过大时失效。

Method: 1. 推导出了置信度校准在协变量偏移下的充要条件——期望一致性条件 (Expectation Consistency Condition)。2. 基于该条件提出了一种无监督域适应损失函数——期望一致性损失 (ECL)。3. 构建了基于小批量的可训练优化方案，并证明了其样本复杂度与ECE相同。

Result: 在模拟数据集和真实世界协变量偏移数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效地校准模型在目标域上的置信度，并且兼容多种校准范式（正则、按类、Top-label）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的期望一致性损失（ECL）有效解决了协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题，无需标签即可实现对目标域的校准，且在多种校准指标下表现优异，弥补了传统权重法不稳定的缺陷。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [169] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了递归OCE框架下的风险敏感强化学习，明确了PAC可学习的效用函数条件，并提供了紧致的样本复杂度上下界，特别是在CVaR场景下实现了对参数τ依赖性的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的风险敏感强化学习在针对不同风险度量（如CVaR、熵风险等）进行统一框架分析时，缺乏关于其PAC可学习性及最优样本复杂度的精确界定，且部分风险度量的现有理论边界尚有优化空间。

Method: 采用基于模型的强化学习方法（model-based approach），通过生成模型对MDP环境进行建模，量化分析了在递归优化确定性等价（Recursive OCE）框架下的值学习（Value Learning）与策略学习（Policy Learning）样本复杂度，并推导了相应的上下界。

Result: 1. 证明了当效用函数u的定义域非全空间R时，对应的风险敏感优化问题不可PAC学习；2. 给出了价值学习与策略学习的PAC样本复杂度上下界；3. 在CVaR_τ中，将样本复杂度对τ的依赖从现有成果改进至1/τ²。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了递归OCE目标函数可PAC学习的效用函数精确条件（即定义域必须为实数域），并证明了其样本复杂度的最优性。特别是在CVaR_τ的分析中，将对τ的依赖度提升至1/τ²，实现了对现有最先进结果的优化。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [170] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个基于PAC-Bayesian和非精确概率理论的框架，量化了测试时适应（TTA）中的分布偏移，通过将MMD空间映射为置信集，实现了有效的不确定性评估及性能保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时适应（TTA）方法在处理分布偏移时虽能提升性能，但缺乏刻画偏移幅度与预测可靠性之间关联的理论保证。

Method: 1. 引入PAC-Bayesian框架，构建与最大均值差异（MMD）相关的泛化界。2. 利用Walley的非精确概率理论，将源分布附近的MMD空间解释为置信集。3. 结合RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设，推导有限样本下的风险界及测地线保持界。

Result: 1. 定量建立了MMD偏移惩罚的PAC-Bayesian泛化界。2. 实现了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性的分离。3. 证明了核引导的适应能够保护局部特征几何结构。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将分布偏移转化为置信集（credal sets），为测试时适应（TTA）提供了鲁棒的理论框架，量化了认知不确定性，并为何时进行适应提供了决策依据。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多广告位页面场景提出了一种新型保量广告（GD）分配优化框架，通过引入二分图匹配、合约轮盘机制及PV约束，显著提升了广告位分配效率、平台收益及合约履约能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保量广告（GD）分配方法大多基于“单广告位”假设，无法应对多广告位页面视图（Multi-slot PV）下的资源分配挑战，导致广告位冗余、合约履约不均衡及曝光过度集中等问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于离线二分图匹配的联合优化框架，主要包括：1. 引入“合约轮盘”（Contract Roulette）机制处理广告位排他性；2. 实施页面浏览（Page View）约束以实现曝光精准控制；3. 开发了可扩展的分配优化算法以支撑大规模在线部署。

Result: 美团广告平台在线A/B测试显示，在该框架下，ARPU（每用户平均收益）提升了28.99%；DID（双重差分）分析表明合同稳定性得到显著改善。

Conclusion: 该框架通过联合优化多广告位分配，有效解决了广告库存分配中的冗余、失衡与集中度问题，不仅显著提升了平台收益和广告主ROI，还增强了合约履行的稳定性，验证了在大规模工业生产环境中的高实用价值。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [172] [Three Costs of Amortizing Gaussian Process Inference with Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21798)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对神经过程（LNP）近似高斯过程（GP）的性能进行了理论量化，分析了三种误差来源（标签污染、信息瓶颈、摊销误差），并提出了改进架构设计的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经过程（Neural Processes）通过摊销推理将GP的$O(n^3)$复杂度降至$O(n)$，但其近似性能的理论基础与局限性尚不明确，需要从理论上量化其误差来源并寻找提升途径。

Method: 对隐变量神经过程（LNP）与高斯过程（GP）之间的KL散度进行了分解与理论界定，推导了信息瓶颈项（衰减率与表现维度d、核函数平滑度及输入维度有关）和标签污染项的阶数。

Result: 推导并定量了影响误差的三项指标：1. 标签污染（Label contamination）为$O(1)$，仅观测噪声部分随$n$减小；2. 信息瓶颈（Information bottleneck）随维度$d$衰减（针对不同核函数给出具体衰减率）；3. 摊销误差（Amortization error）。证实了表示维度、核函数平滑度和输入维度对模型性能的直接影响。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析揭示了神经过程（LNP）在近似高斯过程（GP）时的三类误差来源（标签污染、信息瓶颈、摊销误差），并由此提出改进建议，包括建议仅通过上下文位置而非标签来预测方差，以及使用二阶池化（second-order pooling）替代均值聚合来减小误差。

Abstract: Neural processes amortize Gaussian process inference, replacing the exact $O(n^3)$ posterior with a learned $O(n)$ map from context sets to predictive distributions. For a class of latent neural processes, we bound the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between the GP and LNP predictives, decomposing it into three interpretable sources, namely label contamination as the neural process uses label values to estimate a quantity that is label-independent in the exact GP, an information bottleneck because the finite-dimensional representation cannot resolve the full context geometry, and amortization error from a single encoder network shared across all contexts. The bottleneck truncation term decays in the representation dimension $d$ as $O(e^{-cd^{2/d_x}})$ for squared-exponential kernels on $\mathbb{R}^{d_x}$ where $c > 0$ is a kernel-dependent constant and as $O(d^{-2ν/d_x})$ for Matérn-$ν$ kernels, directly linking architecture sizing to kernel smoothness and input dimension. The label contamination term is $O(1)$ in general, with only the observation-noise component decaying as $O(1/n)$, identifying a persistent cost of routing uncertainty estimation through a label-dependent representation. These results characterize the costs of amortization within the analyzed class and yield architectural recommendations to predict variance from context locations alone in the GP-amortization regime, and replace mean aggregation with second-order pooling to close the dominant amortization gap.

</details>


### [173] [Aerodynamic force reconstruction using physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22111)
*Gledson Rodrigo Tondo,Igor Kavrakov,Guido Morgenthal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习方法，在噪声干扰下通过结构动力响应高效准确地重构气动荷载，无需正则化，且适用于多源多保真度数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的气动荷载模型往往基于简化假设，导致精度受限，且在真实物理系统中，面对噪声干扰和数据缺失时，模型验证十分困难，亟需一种抗噪性强、鲁棒性高的重构方法。

Method: 提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习（Probabilistic Physics-Informed Machine Learning）方法，能够利用噪声干扰下的结构动力响应数据，通过整合多源异构和多保真度数据进行荷载重建，且无需人为设定正则化方案。

Result: 通过大贝尔特东桥（Great Belt East Bridge）的线性非定常模拟实验验证了该方法，结果显示预测荷载与真实荷载在均方根误差（RMSE）、幅值、相位角及峰值方面均高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该方法为空气动力荷载的重建提供了一种高精度、鲁棒性强且无需正则化的新范式，具有模型验证、荷载预测及结构健康监测等广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [174] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离散组合优化任务，提出了一种CPU-GPU批量加速分支定界（BnB）框架，通过处理数据结构不规则性并优化数据流，大幅提升了求解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GPU在连续优化领域取得了巨大成功，但其在处理带有离散变量、组合结构和非线性目标的复杂问题（如带基数约束的广义线性模型）时，由于BnB算法中异构节点的序列化处理和频繁的CPU-GPU带宽瓶颈，性能表现不佳。

Method: 提出了一种通用的、模块化的CPU-GPU协作框架，通过在GPU上批量处理分支定界（BnB）节点，利用填充（padding）技术和轻量级自定义内核来弥补不规则节点数据结构带来的挑战，减少CPU与GPU间的数据传输。

Result: 在具有挑战性的实例测试中，该框架实现了1到2个数量级的速度提升，且能够保证零最优性间隙（zero optimality gap）。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅实现了显著的加速，还支持收集整个Rashomon集，为变量重要性分析和模型选择等下游统计需求提供了有力支持，证明了通用GPU加速分支定界方法的有效性。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [175] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间随机控制和线性规划的生成建模框架——价值驱动传输（VDT），通过优化价值函数实现高效的直线路径数据传输，在性能、推理速度及应用扩展性上优于主流的扩散与流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有生成模型（如流、扩散模型、薛定谔桥）在传输路径路径弯曲、计算开销大及控制灵活性等方面的局限性，寻求一种路径更直、计算更高效且易于集成的生成建模方案。

Method: 基于离散时间随机控制的测度传输框架，将生成建模问题表述为线性规划（LP），利用对偶变量求解最优价值函数，并导出“价值驱动传输”（VDT）策略。采用一种无需模拟（simulation-free）的原始-对偶算法来计算近似价值函数。

Result: 实验表明，VDT策略生成路径直线化程度高，实现了快速且稳健的模拟，同时易于集成条件生成、免分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）及成对/非成对数据翻译等功能，展现了良好的性能与可扩展性。

Conclusion: VDT提供了一种高效、稳健的生成建模新框架，能够实现直线路径传输，其在推理速度和灵活性上优于现有的扩散模型和流模型。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [176] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自步课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双层难度评估机制动态调度训练过程，有效提升了多模态对话情感识别在处理模态不对齐和数据不平衡时的鲁棒性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话多模态情感识别（MERC）方法在面临模态不对齐和样本/模态学习不平衡问题时，性能受到严重限制，难以充分挖掘多模态信息。

Method: 提出了一个基于自步课程学习（SPCL）的框架，包含双层难度评估器（Difficulty Measurer）：1. 话语级评分（建模细粒度模态特定难度）；2. 对话级评分（捕获对话结构、情感依赖及模态一致性）。学习调度器（Learning Scheduler）利用这些评分动态引导模型从易到难进行训练。

Result: 在 IEMOCAP 和 MELD 数据集上进行了广泛测试，在不同架构和模态设置下均表现出色。IEMOCAP 上的加权 F1 分数提升了约 1.2% 至 6.6%，MELD 上的提升幅度最高达 10.4%。

Conclusion: SPCL 框架作为一种轻量级、即插即用的模块，能有效解决 MERC 任务中的模态不对齐与不平衡学习问题，具有良好的泛化能力，能显著提升现有各类模型的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [177] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究从理论上证明了多任务算子学习（MNO）具有与单任务学习相同的缩放定律，意味着跨任务的知识共享不会增加额外的学习负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多任务算子学习（如MNO架构）在近似能力与统计复杂度方面的理论基础，特别是评估共享表示机制在多任务场景下的效率。

Method: 针对Lipschitz多算子映射类，理论推导近似与统计泛化能力的优化上界；建立参数复杂度下界以证明极小极大（minimax）速率。

Result: 证明了多任务算子学习的近似与统计泛化复杂度与单任务学习一致；确立了参数复杂度的极小极大速率；对比证明了MNO与简单输入拼接的DeepONet在最坏情况下的渐近表现无本质差异。

Conclusion: 多任务算子学习（MNO）遵循与单算子学习相同的缩放定律（scaling laws），即共享表示不会增加总体计算/统计成本。MNO架构与基于输入拼接的DeepONet多任务扩展在渐近复杂度上等价。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [178] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了CKD风险预测模型在内部测试中的高表现存在虚高风险；由于模型缺乏校准稳定性且难以应对外部数据的分布偏移，其临床部署就绪度极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾脏病（CKD）风险预测模型在内部数据上虽表现优异（高区分度），但常忽视校准和不确定性量化。临床医生缺乏关于模型输出概率准确性的可靠依据，且模型通常缺乏在外部数据集上的泛化验证。

Method: 基于UCI CKD数据集训练五种分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM、高斯朴素贝叶斯）；使用Platt缩放和保序回归（Isotonic Regression）进行校准；通过预期校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数评估校准质量；利用划分共形预测（Split Conformal Prediction）量化不确定性；最后在MIMIC-IV数据上进行分布压力测试。

Result: 虽所有模型在UCI内部测试中AUROC达到1.00，但在MIMIC-IV外部数据上表现急剧恶化（AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率远低于90%目标）。此外，所有模型在部署就绪检查清单中的得分均未超过4分（满分16分）。

Conclusion: 内部测试性能近乎完美并不代表模型具有临床部署的泛化能力。在临床应用前，必须在外部数据上对模型的校准稳定性及一致性预测（Conformal Coverage）进行严格评估。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [179] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化证据深度学习（EDL）目标函数的方法，通过使用基于Dirichlet均值的插件损失替代复杂的期望损失，有效降低了EDL的计算复杂度，同时保持了高质量的不确定性评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的实证深度学习（EDL）虽能进行单次推理的不确定性估计，但其基于Dirichlet分布的优化目标计算复杂，增加了实现的难度。

Method: 提出了一种对第一阶经验风险最小化问题目标函数的近似方法，使用Dirichlet均值处的插件损失替代复杂的Dirichlet期望目标，并证明了该近似误差随证据增加而衰减。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上验证了简化目标函数的有效性，实现了与传统EDL相当的预测准确性和选择性预测（selective prediction）性能，同时简化了训练管线。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于Dirichlet均值的插件损失（plug-in loss），简化了EDL的优化目标，在保持与传统EDL相当的预测与不确定性估计性能的同时，显著降低了计算与实现复杂度，并为Softmax分类器提供了不确定性估计的理论解释。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [180] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConTact 是一种新型抗体 CDR 设计架构，通过引入“先接触、后行动”的机制，将抗原接触推理与氨基酸序列设计明确解耦，从而在保持高序列恢复率的同时，显著提升了抗体结构的准确性和对抗原表位的靶向能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算抗体设计方法在处理“识别接触残基”和“选择氨基酸”这两个子任务时往往混为一谈，导致模型隐含地学习接触推理，稀释了抗原信号，进而影响设计精度。

Method: 提出 ConTact (Contact-then-Act) 架构，包含三个级联阶段：学习表面互补指纹、预测 CDR-抗原接触点、以及将接触门控后的抗原特征注入序列头部。引入距离偏置的交叉注意力模块编码几何先验，并使用接触加权的交叉熵损失函数。

Result: 在 CHIMERA-Bench 数据集上，ConTact 实现了最佳结构质量（RMSD 较次优基准提升 7%）及最佳表位感知（F1 分数较 GNN 基准提升 10%），序列恢复率（AAR）达到 0.38。

Conclusion: ConTact 通过明确区分“接触预测”与“序列设计”这两个子任务，显著提高了抗体 CDR-H3 设计的结构质量和对抗原表位的感知能力，证明了显式建模接触关系对提升生成模型性能的重要性。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析揭示了均匀扩散模型（UDM）中训练目标与参数化之间的不匹配问题，提出利用留一后验进行重参数化及吸收态重构，提升了离散扩散模型的推理质量和生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中清理数据预测的使用方式存在差异（如 MDM 与 UDM）。作者旨在解决 UDM 中插件 ELBO 与常用交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配问题，并理清参数化选择对模型性能的影响。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明了 UDM 的标准 plug-in 桥参数化由留一后验（leave-one-out posterior）优化，而非去噪后验。
2. 转换推导：推导了去噪器、留一后验和分数（score）之间的精确转换关系，解耦参数化与训练目标。
3. 架构优化：引入了 UDM 的吸收态重构（absorbing-state reformulation），简化了去噪后验并实现了更好的重掩码机制。
4. 推理改进：开发了基于留一预测器的改进型预测-校正采样和温度采样技术。

Result: 在语言建模任务中，使用留一法（leave-one-out）参数化始终能提升 UDM 的生成效果；新的吸收态重构方法使 UDM 在性能上能比肩甚至超过 MDM，验证了采样设计对离散扩散的重要性。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型（尤其是均匀扩散模型 UDM）的性能差距主要源于参数化和采样设计，而非边缘分布选择。留一法（Leave-one-out）参数化和吸收态重构显著提升了生成性能，弥合了与掩码扩散模型之间的表现差距。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [182] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为PW-OPSD的自蒸馏方法，通过引入位置加权策略（而非传统的熵加权），解决了在轨推理蒸馏中教师监督信号可靠性权重分配不均的问题，显著提升了模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在轨自蒸馏（OPSD）方法将所有令牌等同对待，而基于局部熵的权重调整方法在推理任务中难以区分“不确定性”与“多样性”，导致监督效果不佳。因此，作者旨在寻找更准确的评估指标来衡量教师令牌的可靠性。

Method: 1. 分支可行性诊断（Branch-viability diagnostic）：通过强制执行备选路径并测试结果正确性，评估教师令牌在不同位置的可靠性。
2. 位置加权机制（PW-OPSD）：基于诊断发现的“位置相关性”规律，在训练目标中引入递增的权重函数，引导学生模型更关注序列后期（靠近答案）的教师指导。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型上，位置得分预测教师可靠性的AUROC达到0.83，远高于局部不确定性得分（0.57）。在AIME 2024/2025评测中，PW-OPSD比标准OPSD提升了1.0-1.1分，并在不同模型家族（DeepSeek-R1, Olmo-3）上验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: 推理任务中，“教师标记的可靠性”具有轨迹结构特征。相比于局部熵，基于令牌在序列中位置的加权机制（PW-OPSD）能更有效地提升蒸馏效果，无需额外的教师推理算力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [183] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SDPM 提出一种基于扩散模型的新型生存分析生成框架，无需离散化或预设函数分布，实现了连续时间生存分布的高精度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法通常依赖于风险函数的结构假设或时间轴离散化，这限制了模型的灵活性并引入了近似误差，难以准确捕捉连续时间生存分布。

Method: 提出生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），利用去噪扩散模型对生存结果（时间 T 和删失指标 δ）的条件分布进行建模，引入目标空间变换（标准化对数时间及高斯混合表示）以优化模型收敛，并结合 Kaplan-Meier 估计器推导生存函数。

Result: 在十个真实生存数据集上，SDPM 在 C-index、集成时变 AUC 和集成 Brier 分数上均表现出卓越的预测能力；在合成数据实验中，证明了其相比非参数基线能更准确地恢复连续生存分布。

Conclusion: SDPM 提供了一种灵活的非参数化生存分析生成方法，通过扩散模型有效处理连续时间回归任务，在多个数据集上展现了优于传统基线模型的性能，并成功解决了生存分析中的非参数分布建模难题。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [184] [AgForce Enables Antigen-conditioned Generative Antibody Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21610)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgForce 解决了抗体生成模型在抗原信息利用上的局限性，通过架构优化和新型训练目标，显著提升了抗体设计的特异性和序列多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前抗体设计方法虽然以抗原结构为条件，但往往“抗原盲目”（忽略抗原信息，仅依赖抗体框架进行预测）、存在词汇坍缩（预测氨基酸多样性不足）及收敛到位置边缘分布的训练瓶颈。

Method: 提出了 AgForce 模型：1. 编码器端使用 GNN 结合框架丢弃、门控瓶颈和双曲交叉注意力，抑制对抗体框架的过度依赖（shortcut）；2. 解码器端用 Mixture Density Network (MDN) 和 annealed Multiple Choice Learning (aMCL) 替代传统的交叉熵损失，解决位置边缘化陷阱；3. 引入抗原循环一致性训练策略，强制模型利用抗原信息。

Result: 在 CHIMERA-Bench 数据集上，AgForce 实现了最佳的结合质量和序列恢复能力；氨基酸恢复率较最强的序列基线提升了 8%，在所有接口指标上均优于基线，并将 GNN 方法的有效词汇量几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: AgForce 通过专门设计的架构组件（框架丢弃、门控瓶颈、双曲交叉注意力）和新的训练目标（aMCL、抗原循环一致性），成功解决了抗体设计中的“抗原盲目”问题，实现了高保真度的序列-结构协同设计。

Abstract: Antibody design methods condition on antigen structure to generate complementarity-determining regions (CDR), yet a systematic evaluation of baseline methods reveals that they largely ignore the antigen input. We identify three failure modes that explain this behavior. Antigen blindness arises because models derive predictions from antibody framework context rather than antigen information, producing nearly identical CDRs regardless of the target. Vocabulary collapse reduces predicted amino acids to three to five per position, far below the ground truth distribution in native sequences. Moreover, any model trained with standard per-position cross-entropy converges to the positional marginal distribution, making it provably unable to produce antigen-specific sequence predictions. We propose a novel encoder-decoder architecture called AgForce, that uses a graph neural network (GNN) as the encoder and specialized decoders for sequence-structure co-design. Specifically, we apply framework dropout, gated bottlenecks, and hyperbolic cross attention that prevent the antibody shortcut path. In the decoder, a Mixture Density Network (MDN) sequence head with Potts-like pairwise coupling and annealed Multiple Choice Learning (aMCL) replaces the cross-entropy objective with a multi-component distribution whose optimal solution differs from the positional marginal. An antigen cycle consistency head routes gradients through the sequence decoder, forcing predicted distributions to encode antigen identity. AgForce achieves the best binding quality and sequence recovery simultaneously on the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, improving amino acid recovery by 8% over the strongest sequence baseline while surpassing the baselines across all interface metrics, and nearly doubling the effective vocabulary of GNN methods.
