Using RSA with identical model weights from our human study, we find: (1) all models achieve higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (rho = 0.15-0.30 at V1/V2) than with human fMRI (rho = 0.01-0.08), consistent with the higher signal-to-noise ratio of electrophysiology; (2) STDP and PC produce the highest macaque V1/V2 alignment (rho ~ 0.30 and 0.28), consistent with their leading position among trained rules in human V1; (3) at IT, learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species (Kendall's tau = 0.00, p = 1.00), though this null result is expected given that n = 5 provides power only at tau = +/-1.0, and is further confounded by stimulus set differences; (4) a pretrained ResNet-50 (ImageNet) achieves rho = 0.25 at macaque IT, substantially above all custom CNN conditions (rho = 0.07-0.14), suggesting IT alignment is limited by model capacity and training data rather than by the learning rule. Noise ceilings, multi-seed variability (5 seeds), and a stimulus-control analysis are reported. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

</details>


### [255] [AMUSE: Anytime Muon with Stable Gradient Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22432)
*Jueun Kim,Baekrok Shin,Jihun Yun,Beomhan Baek,Minhak Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了 Muon 优化器在“河谷”损失景观中的动力学机制，并提出了 AMUSE：一种通过动态插值结合 Muon 的高效更新与无调度平滑平均的新型优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 虽能有效提升训练速度，但其正交化操作会放大高曲率方向的噪声导致训练轨迹震荡；此外，现有的优化方法依赖预设调度，缺乏灵活性。

Method: AMUSE（Anytime MUon with Stable gradient Evaluation）：使用随时间变化的插值系数，在训练初期侧重于 Muon 的快速梯度更新，后期逐渐过渡到平滑的平均序列以抑制震荡。

Result: AMUSE 在保持 Muon 快速训练优势的同时，通过平均化机制显著提升了收敛的稳定性，并在多项基准测试中优于现有的优化器。

Conclusion: AMUSE 通过结合 Muon 的快速训练特性与无调度（Schedule-Free）平均法的稳定性，在无需预设学习率调度的情况下，在视觉任务和大型语言模型预训练中实现了比 AdamW、Schedule-Free AdamW 和 Muon 更优的性能-迭代帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）。

Abstract: Modern deep learning commonly relies on AdamW with prescribed learning rate schedules, but recent works challenge both components: Schedule-Free optimization removes explicit schedules via iterate averaging, and Muon improves the update geometry by orthogonalizing momentum for matrix parameters. Despite Muon's strong empirical performance, its underlying mechanism remains partially understood. We study Muon through the river-valley loss landscape, where useful training progress occurs along a flat, low-curvature bulk subspace (the river), while high-curvature dominant directions form steep valley walls that induce oscillations. We empirically show that while Muon's orthogonalization accelerates river progress by increasing the bulk component, it also amplifies dominant-direction noise, causing oscillatory trajectories. Building on this, we propose Anytime MUon with Stable gradient Evaluation (AMUSE), which integrates Muon's rapid bulk progress with the stabilizing effect of Schedule-Free averaging. AMUSE uses a time-varying interpolation coefficient that initially evaluates gradients near the fast Muon sequence for rapid adaptation, then gradually shifts toward the stable averaged sequence to suppress valley-wall oscillations. As a result, AMUSE requires no learning rate schedules and supports anytime training. Across vision tasks and large language model pretraining, AMUSE consistently improves the performance-iteration Pareto frontier over (Schedule-Free) AdamW and Muon.

</details>


### [256] [Lost in Tokenization: Fundamental Trade-offs in Graph Tokenization for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22471)
*Maya Bechler-Speicher,Gilad Yehudai,Gil Harari,Clayton Sanford,Amir Globerson,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Graph Transformer中Tokenization选择的本质重要性：不同方式的选择直接决定了模型拟合结构信息的难易程度与深度需求，不存在一种万能的Tokenization，而多视图结合通常是更优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前Transformer在图学习中广泛应用，但图结构的“Token化”方式对模型性能和表达能力的影响尚未得到系统性的理论研究。本文旨在阐明Tokenization选择如何成为制约图Transformer表达能力的底层因素。

Method: 作者从理论上分析并对比了三种基础图Tokenization（谱分解、随机游走、邻接矩阵），通过数学证明了它们在表达能力和深度需求上的差异，并利用合成与真实数据集进行了实证验证。

Result: 1. 证明了不同Tokenization诱导出不同的深度需求，即同一图计算在一种Tokenization下可能是浅层的，而在另一种下则需极深；2. 证明了随机游走Tokenization是有损的；3. 证明了谱Tokenization虽无损但对局部任务不友好；4. 证明了有限深度的Transformer通常无法在不同Tokenization家族间进行转换。

Conclusion: 图Transformer的表现力在很大程度上取决于Tokenization的选择。缺乏合适的Tokenization会导致Transformer即使增加深度也难以弥补结构信息缺失的局限性。因此，在任务中结合互补的Tokenizations往往能获得最佳性能。

Abstract: Transformers have become a central architecture for graph learning, but their application to graphs requires first choosing a tokenization: a graph-to-token map that determines which structural information is exposed at the input. In this work, we show that this choice is a fundamental component of transformer expressivity. We examine three tokenizations that serve as building blocks for many existing graph tokenizations: spectral, random-walk, and adjacency tokenizations. We prove that different tokenizations induce distinct depth regimes: the same graph computation may be realizable by a shallow transformer under one tokenization, while requiring substantially larger depth under another. For example, we prove that random-walk tokenization is lossy for any walk length, making it impossible in general to recover the graph from it, and that while spectral tokenization is lossless, it is ill-conditioned for local tasks. We further show that although both random-walk and spectral tokenizations are derived from adjacency information, it is impossible for a limited-depth transformer to convert between tokenization families in general. In particular, we establish lower bounds and impossibility results showing that unfavorable tokenizations may preclude the efficient recovery of more suitable structural representations. Finally, we complement our theory with controlled experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks, validating the predicted separations and showing that different tasks favor different structural views, and combining complementary tokenizations allows the transformer to leverage distinct signals from each representation.

</details>


### [257] [Represented Is Not Computed: A Causal Test of Candidate Algorithmic Intermediates in a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22488)
*Ishita Darade,Sushrut Thorat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过算术任务对比了“线性探针（Probe）”与“因果分析（Causal Analysis）”的结果，发现探针显示模型似乎使用了分步算法，但因果干预证明这些中间结果并非实际输出通路的一部分，警示了单一方法论在可解释性研究中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer在执行结构化任务时，其内部表征是否真的如线性探针所显示的那样执行了分步算法，并揭示探针分析与因果分析之间可能存在的偏差。

Method: 1. 训练Transformer解决“基数提取”任务（$\lfloor N/B^D \rfloor \bmod B$）；2. 使用线性探针（Linear Probes）检测内部是否存储中间结果；3. 使用因果测试（Causal Tests）和稀疏电路搜索（Sparse Circuit Search）定位实际执行计算的信号通路。

Result: 模型能够以99.83%的准确率完成任务；线性探针成功解码出算法中间值；然而，因果分析发现实际计算并不通过这些中间值的传递实现，电路搜索发现$N$、$B$和$D$的路径在后期才结合，反驳了分步算法的假设。

Conclusion: 在该算术任务中，模型内部虽然能够表征出算法中间值（证明了探针技术的有效性），但这些表征并没有通过因果电路传输到输出端。该研究强调了仅依赖探针分析可能导致错误的结论，因果干预分析对于理解模型真实的内部算法至关重要。

Abstract: Structured prompts require integrating components according to task-relevant relations. How a network implements this integration is often hard to judge in language or vision, where those relations are rarely specified precisely enough to define a candidate internal algorithm. Arithmetic offers a cleaner setting. We study a Transformer trained on base-digit extraction: given $N$, $B$, and $D$, it must report the coefficient of $B^D$ in the base-$B$ expansion of $N$. The closed-form solution, $\lfloor N/B^D \rfloor \bmod B$, provides explicit candidate algorithmic intermediates. Across three seeds, the model reaches 99.83% exact-answer accuracy on held-out number-base intersections, establishing reliable task competence. Linear probes decode the intermediates, making staged arithmetic computation plausible. Causal tests then separate representation from use: within the localized route from the stream with $D$ as input to the output positions, behavior depends on early $D$-selective communication, independent of $N$ and $B$. Relatedly, a sparse circuit search finds mostly separate $N$, $B$, and $D$ routes that combine late rather than the staged route suggested by the probes. Thus, the model represents the intermediates that make the closed-form solution plausible, but the identified localized causal route does not transmit them to the output stream. This case shows that probe-based conclusions can diverge sharply from causal observations, even when explicit algorithmic hypotheses are available.

</details>


### [258] [The Neural Compiler: Program-to-Network Translation for Hybrid Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22498)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The Neural Compiler 是一种能将物理符号公式自动转化为高精度、可微 PyTorch 模块的系统，通过消除手动编码需求，极大地提高了科学机器学习中混合建模的效率与系统组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学机器学习方法在结合已知物理规律与未知数据依赖项时，往往存在忽略物理结构、采用软惩罚导致精度受限，或需要繁琐的手动编码代码等痛点。

Method: 构建了一个基于第一类 Scheme 风格表达式语言的编译器（The Neural Compiler），将符号化的物理公式自动转换为精确、可微的 PyTorch 模块，支持 51 种原子操作。

Result: 实验表明，该编译器生成的模块在精度上与手写代码完全一致，相比 PINN 基准模型在参数量仅有 1-4 个的情况下，能以极高的精度恢复物理常数，且在复杂组合模型中展现出零误差的优异性能。

Conclusion: Neural Compiler 实现了科学计算中物理公式的自动化、精确且可微的转换，解决了传统方法在处理混合建模时需要手动编码或精度不足的问题，为大型语言模型提供了有效的科学计算执行接口。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often requires combining known physics with unknown parameters or correction terms learned from data. Existing approaches either ignore known structure, encode it as a soft penalty, or require hand-written PyTorch code for each equation. We present The Neural Compiler, a system that translates programs written in a first-order Scheme-like expression language into frozen, differentiable PyTorch modules. These modules match the source program to floating-point precision and provide gradients through autograd. In hybrid models, the compiled module encodes known physics exactly while learned components model the unknown remainder. We evaluate the compiler across six experiment domains: Feynman physics equations, Lotka-Volterra dynamics, a damped pendulum, a one-dimensional heat equation, three-dimensional vector mechanics, and compositional generalization. Compiled modules match hand-coded PyTorch implementations numerically for single equations, showing no accuracy loss from compilation. With only 1 to 4 trainable parameters, compiled models recover physical constants to less than 1 percent error in most cases, while standard PINN baselines with more than 8500 parameters show 7 to 93 percent error. Compiled modules also compose with zero error, while neural approximations can accumulate large errors in deep composition chains. The main value of the compiler is not improved accuracy over hand-coded equations, but systematic composability: it generates correct, differentiable modules from symbolic specifications without rewriting each equation by hand. The system supports 51 primitive operations, including vector and matrix algebra, enabling PDE discretizations and hybrid scientific models. This string-in, module-out interface also provides a natural target for large language models that translate scientific descriptions into executable differentiable modules.

</details>


### [259] [The Signal in the Noise: OOD Detection Through Goodness-of-Fit Testing in Factorised Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22496)
*Philipp Bomatter,Jack Geary,Henry Gouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SITN 方法，通过分析连续归一化流在噪声先验下的分布差异，解决了生成模型进行 OOD 检测时似然度不可靠和复杂度偏差严重的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型（基于似然度）在 OOD 检测中效果不佳，且容易受到数据复杂度偏差的影响，导致无法准确区分领域内和领域外数据。

Method: 提出了“Signal in the Noise (SITN)”方法，利用连续归一化流的特性，分析 OOD 样本在噪声先验下的非典型分布特征，而非直接依赖似然度判断。

Result: 在标准基准测试和合成扰动测试中证明了 SITN 的有效性；该方法无需 OOD 数据，计算开销小，并能严格控制假阳性率。

Conclusion: SITN 通过利用连续归一化流的微分同胚和保质特性，有效解决了深度生成模型在 OOD 检测中存在的似然度不可靠及复杂度偏差问题，为单样本 OOD 检测提供了一种高效、稳健的方案。

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a natural foundation for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, yet prior work has shown that their assigned likelihoods are notoriously unreliable indicators for in- vs out-of-distribution data. In this paper, we address this problem by leveraging the diffeomorphic and mass-preserving properties of continuous normalising flows. Our analysis shows that OOD samples are mapped to noise samples that are highly atypical under the noise prior in ways not captured by the likelihood. Based on this observation, we propose a new method -- Signal in the Noise (SITN) -- for OOD detection on the single-sample level. SITN requires no access to OOD data, incurs minimal computational overhead, and provides strict control of false positive rates. Comprehensive evaluations through standard benchmarks and synthetic perturbations highlight the method's effectiveness and the absence of the complexity bias inherent to likelihood-based methods.

</details>


### [260] [Disentanglement Beyond Generative Models with Riemannian ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22531)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现代表示学习中缺乏全局生成模型理论支撑的问题，本文提出黎曼 ICA（RICA），通过引入基于 Hessian 矩阵和 Ricci 曲率的“解纠缠张量”，实现了无需全局假设的特征点向解纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解纠缠理论（如 ICA）过度依赖于全局生成模型和统计独立假设，难以直接应用于缺乏此类假设的现代预训练编码器。研究旨在弥补理论与实践之间的差距，为现代表示学习中的特征解纠缠提供理论支撑。

Method: 提出了黎曼 ICA (RICA) 理论，用局部几何结构取代了传统 ICA 的全局生成模型；利用黎曼几何形式化了数据点与潜在空间轴对齐线之间的映射关系；开发了“解纠缠张量”来度量点向解纠缠，该张量结合了数据对数似然的 Hessian 矩阵和模型诱导的 Ricci 曲率。

Result: 在受控的源恢复实验中，RICA 表现出比传统 ICA 更好的性能，且其恢复能力不依赖于观察数据的特定坐标系表示，证明了其在多种流形结构下的有效性。

Conclusion: Riemannian ICA (RICA) 为在无需假设全局生成模型的前提下研究局部解纠缠奠定了理论基础，通过引入解纠缠张量，实现了一种更具普适性的特征可解释性框架。

Abstract: There is a gap between the theoretical foundations of disentanglement and the practice of modern representation learning. Existing theoretical frameworks, particularly Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and its nonlinear variants, assume a generative model with statistically independent latent variables underlying the data so that disentanglement amounts to identifying the latents that could have generated the data. This generative framework is interpretable and theoretically justified, but its strong assumptions make it difficult to apply to modern representation learning. Modern pretrained encoders often learn features that exhibit disentangled properties without making generative assumptions, yet there is no general theory for interpreting these features as independent factors of variation. We take a step toward such a theory by introducing Riemannian ICA (RICA), which replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure. RICA is founded on the observation that in ICA, the factors of variation underlying a data point can be understood through radial curves emanating from the point that map to axis-aligned lines in the latent space. We formalize this perspective using Riemannian geometry and introduce our theory in a way that is consistent with the existing generative approach. Our main contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes a second-order notion of disentanglement that we call pointwise disentanglement. This tensor depends on the Hessian of the data log likelihood as well as the Ricci curvature induced by the model. In a controlled source recovery setting with known ground-truth sources, RICA recovers sources across several manifolds, while the success of ICA baselines depends on the coordinates used to represent the observations. Our work provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model.

</details>


### [261] [Relational Linear Properties in Language Models: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22532)
*Giovanni Valer,Luigi Gresele,Marco Bronzini,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL散度的高效探测方法，用于验证大语言模型中的“关系线性”假设，发现该特性在层级分布上具有一致性但受模型差异和查询表述影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在大语言模型表示中实验性验证“关系线性”假设（对于特定关系，主语与宾语之间存在线性映射关系）的挑战，并改进现有基于雅可比矩阵估计的低效方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于KL散度的探测方法，用于评估给定关系下主语嵌入映射至宾语去嵌入（unembedding）的线性度，并对比了不同层级和同义改写查询的影响。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验结果表明，关系线性不仅在不同模型间存在差异，且在模型层级上呈现出此前观测到的语言信息分布模式，同时对关系的语义改写表现出明显的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究证实了关系线性假设在模型表示中的存在性，并发现其表现受模型架构、层级位置及查询句式的影响，同时通过KL散度方法证明了该探测方法比传统的雅可比近似法更高效。

Abstract: Linear properties are ubiquitous in the representations of language models; however, testing them experimentally remains a challenging task. This work focuses on relational linearity: the hypothesis that, for a fixed relation (e.g., "plays"), the unembedding of an object (e.g., "trumpet") can be predicted from the embedding of its subject (e.g.,"Miles Davis") by a linear map. We present an experimental method to test the formulation of relational linearity by Marconato et al. (2025). Specifically, we introduce a probing method, based on Kullback-Leibler divergence, to evaluate this property and examine its variation across layers and paraphrased relational queries. It is also more efficient than previous work; for example, it avoids the crude Jacobian approximations used in Linear Relational Embeddings by Hernandez et al. (2024). Our findings across four datasets show that relational linearity varies across models, exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior observations about linguistic information in model representations, and is differently affected by changes in how the relation is phrased.

</details>


### [262] [F-TIS: Harnessing Diverse Models in Collaborative GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22537)
*Nikolay Blagoev,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Wendelin Boehmer,Lydia Yiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 分布式 GRPO 训练中因计算资源不均匀导致的异构模型难以协作问题，本文提出了 F-TIS 框架，通过重要性采样与过滤机制，在保障训练收敛性的同时，实现了异构模型的高效协作并提升了泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式 GRPO 训练主要假设节点计算能力是同构的，以维持同策略训练。然而在去中心化系统中，节点资源的异构性使得该假设难以成立；直接引入异构模型会导致严重的离策略问题，从而损害模型的训练效果。

Method: 提出了“过滤截断重要性采样”（Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling, F-TIS）方法。该方法允许不同计算能力和偏好的异构模型参与 GRPO 训练，通过过滤和重要性加权机制处理离策略样本，降低了通信开销，并缓解了离策略样本对 GRPO 收敛的负面影响。

Result: F-TIS 在多种异构设置下实现了与纯同策略训练相同的收敛效果，并且在某些场景中，模型在分布外（OOD）任务上的泛化性能提升最高可达 12%。

Conclusion: F-TIS 框架通过引入筛选和重要性采样机制，有效解决了去中心化分布式训练中异构模型带来的严重离策略（off-policy）问题，在实现高效协作的同时，达到了与同策略（on-policy）训练相当的收敛性，并在某些场景下提升了模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO have seen great popularity in LLM post-training. In GRPO, models produce completions to a set of prompts, which are rewarded, and the policy is updated towards the relatively high reward completions. Due to the auto-regressive nature of models, the generation phase of such style of training can be extremely time consuming. As a solution, prior work has sought to distribute the inference step across many nodes, working parallel. These works assume primarily homogeneous models in the training in order to keep samples as close to on-policy as possible. This assumption may be impractical in decentralized systems, where parties with various computes and preferences may wish to collaborate on the same task. Thus, decentralized training requires an approach that can handle heterogeneous models - different models collaborating on the same tasks. However, this leads to highly off-policy samples presented during training, which prior work has identified that off-policy samples can hurt GRPO convergence. To enable heterogeneity, we propose Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS) - a GRPO-style training paradigm that can use off-policy samples to improve local model's learning. Our framework allows various models to collaborate in the same RL training run while being communication efficient. We extensively evaluate F-TIS in various heterogeneous setups and we show that it exhibits identical final model convergence to purely on-sample training. Furthermore, we observe in some setups better generalization on out-of-distribution tasks than on-policy training, increasing model's performance by up to 12\%.

</details>


### [263] [ImplicitTerrainV2: Wavelet-Guided Spatially Adaptive Neural Terrain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22556)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ImplicitTerrainV2 通过小波自适应技术和导数感知监督，实现了高效、高保真的连续性地形表示与压缩，解决了现有地形 INR 训练成本高、精度不足及缺乏梯度结构控制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统栅格 DEM 依赖插值和有限差分处理，存在局限性；现有的地形 INR 研究缺乏频率控制、忽视梯度结构且计算/存储成本高，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出基于隐式神经表示（INR）的框架：1. 引入小波复杂场（WCF）实现空间自适应频率控制和训练采样；2. 采用导数感知监督（gradient-aware supervision）以保持地形流形平滑性；3. 应用混合精度量化和熵编码进行模型压缩。

Result: 在 50 个瑞士地形瓦片上取得了 66.25 dB 的 PSNR，比现有工作提升 5.70 dB，参数量减少 3.2 倍，单瓦片训练仅需 55 秒，存储需求压缩至 1.23 bpp。

Conclusion: ImplicitTerrainV2 提供了一种高效、紧凑的连续地形表示方法，不仅在重建精度和压缩比上优于现有模型，且支持闭式导数计算和分辨率无关的重构，具备实际 GIS 应用潜力。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) underpin terrain analysis in Geographic Information Systems (GIS), but in their common raster form, they rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling and finite-difference operators for derivative-based analysis. Implicit neural representations (INRs) offer a continuous alternative, but prior terrain INRs lack explicit frequency control, neglect the gradient structure of terrain, and remain too large and costly to train for practical deployment. We present ImplicitTerrainV2, which advances terrain INRs toward a compact, efficient neural terrain data format by combining a spectral control mechanism with wavelet-guided spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware supervision, and post-training model compression. At its core, a wavelet complexity field (WCF) derives spatially-adaptive frequency masks from analytically computed wavelet coefficients, localizing high-frequency capacity to complex terrain regions. The same field guides complexity-aware adaptive sampling that concentrates training in high-complexity regions, while gradient matching applies extra supervision to enforce the smooth manifold structure of terrain DEMs for improved derivative fidelity. Post-training mixed-precision quantization and entropy coding reduce storage to 1.23 bpp with a 0.28 dB PSNR drop. On 50 Swiss terrain tiles, ImplicitTerrainV2 reaches 66.25 dB end-to-end PSNR, improving over the prior work by 5.70 dB while using 3.2x fewer parameters and training in 55 s per tile on a single GPU. Our compressed neural format is competitive with several established DEM codecs in rate-distortion performance, while additionally supporting off-grid point queries, closed-form derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction, which may benefit many downstream GIS applications.

</details>


### [264] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离线目标条件强化学习中的冗余问题，提出通过层次化策略实现“绝对抽象”，利用相对化选项提升经验复用能力，从而显著增强算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的目标条件强化学习（GCRL）任务在状态-目标对之间存在大量对称性和共享结构，导致冗余；作者旨在通过层次化策略实现绝对抽象，以解决离线GCRL中的样本效率与经验复用问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用层次化策略实现绝对抽象的框架，引入了“相对化选项”以及针对不同层次的差异化表征学习方法，并设计了两个相应的算法。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的引入归纳偏置（相对化选项与层次化抽象）的方法在离线GCRL任务中能够显著提升学习性能。

Conclusion: 层次化结构结合相对化选项（relativised options）能有效利用状态空间的相似性，实现经验重用，从而显著提升离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）的性能。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [265] [Regret-Based $(ε,δ)$-optimal Stopping Criteria for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22561)
*Haowei Wang,Jingyi Wang,Qiyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了GP-UCB的瞬时悔界理论，并据此提出了一套具备概率保障的贝叶斯优化停止准则，实现了性能与计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贝叶斯优化通常依靠固定评估预算进行终止，缺乏有效的停止准则，导致成本浪费且无法保证解的最优性。现有研究虽有经验性的进展，但缺乏理论完备的停止机制。

Method: 1. 推导并提出了针对GP-UCB的更紧致的瞬时悔界（instantaneous regret bounds）；2. 基于该理论界设计了贝叶斯优化的停止准则，以保证算法在满足特定收敛条件时终止。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提停止准则的有效性和效率，证明了其在确保解质量的同时能够优化计算预算的使用。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于更紧致悔界（regret bound）的停止准则在理论上是可靠的，能够确保在给定的高概率下达到 ε-最优解，有效解决了贝叶斯优化中缺乏理论支撑的停止准则问题。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used iterative black-box optimization method that utilizes Gaussian process (GP) surrogate models. In practice, BO is typically terminated after a fixed evaluation budget is exhausted, which can incur unnecessary cost and provides no optimality guarantee on solution quality. Recent research in developing a practical stopping criterion has made empirical progress, yet a theoretically sound stopping criterion remains a work in progress. In this work, we present provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds for GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) at any given iteration. Then, we propose stopping criteria for GP-UCB based on this tighter bound that ensures an $ε$-optimal solution with high probability $1-δ$ upon termination. Numerical experiments are performed to validate and demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our stopping criteria.

</details>


### [266] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过匹配全后验协方差来大幅降低扩散模型（DDPM）的路径KL误差（从Ω(1/T)至O(1/T^2)），并设计了高效的Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS）来解决全协方差计算的高昂成本问题，实现了无需训练且高性能的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯扩散模型（DDPM）在路径空间KL散度上存在Ω(1/T)的不可避免误差，限制了需要依赖整个轨迹过程的应用（如分类器引导）的性能。研究旨在突破这一误差下界并寻找高效的实现手段。

Method: 1. 理论上推导引入全后验协方差匹配以提升收敛阶数；2. 提出Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），一种无需训练、无需存储稠密矩阵的矩阵无关（matrix-free）采样方法，利用雅可比向量积（JVP）实现最优后验协方差的采样。

Result: 路径KL误差达到O(1/T^2)的理论阶数提升；LGS算法的逼近误差随Lanczos迭代次数呈指数级衰减；实验证明LGS即便在仅进行三次迭代时，样本质量也优于OCM-DDPM等现有的对角协方差方法。

Conclusion: 通过匹配完整的后验协方差，可以将路径KL散度误差从传统的Ω(1/T)降低至O(1/T^2)。提出的LGS方法使得全协方差匹配在计算上具有可行性，且在仅需三次Lanczos迭代的情况下即可在图像基准上超过强基线模型。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [267] [GraphFlow: A Graph-Based Workflow Management for Efficient LLM-Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22566)
*Ao Li,Shangpeng Yang,Fahao Chen,Tianheng Xu,Peng Li,Zhou Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 GraphFlow 的 AI 智能体工作流管理系统，通过构建统一的 wGraph 图结构，实现了任务自适应的工作流生成和高效的 KV 缓存管理，在提升系统性能的同时大幅降低了计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于工作流的智能体服务系统多依赖预定义的模板和浅层匹配机制，难以捕捉深层语义关系，缺乏对未见任务的泛化能力。

Method: 1. 引入 wGraph：一种表示工作流的统一图结构，以原子操作为节点。
2. 自适应工作流生成：根据任务语义和约束条件从 wGraph 中动态实例化任务特定的工作流。
3. 基于结构的工作流状态管理：利用 wGraph 的图结构属性对 KV 缓存进行优化，减少冗余计算。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，GraphFlow 显著优于当前最优方法，平均性能提升约 4.95%，同时减少了约 4 倍的内存占用。

Conclusion: GraphFlow 通过引入 wGraph 统一图结构，成功提升了 LLM 智能体在复杂任务中的推理执行效率。该系统在性能（提升 4.95 个百分点）和内存优化（提升 4 倍）方面均优于现有最优方法，证明了基于图结构进行工作流管理和 KV 缓存优化的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents demonstrate strong reasoning and execution capabilities on complex tasks when guided by structured instructions, commonly referred to as workflows. However, existing workflow-assisted agent serving systems typically rely on predefined templates and shallow matching mechanisms, which limit their ability to capture deep semantic relationships and generalize to previously unseen tasks. To address these limitations, we propose a new workflow management paradigm that represents workflows using a unified graph, termed wGraph, where each node corresponds to an atomic operation. wGraph serves as a shared substrate from which task-specific workflows are dynamically instantiated. Building on wGraph primitives, we introduce GraphFlow, a system that efficiently integrates workflows into agent serving through two key designs. First, adaptive workflow generation dynamically constructs workflows from wGraph based on task semantics and constraint requirements. Second, workflow state management exploits wGraph structure to efficiently manage Key-Value (KV) caches, reducing redundant computation during agent serving. Extensive experiments across five benchmark datasets show that GraphFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding an average performance improvement of approximately 4.95 percentage points, while achieving an approximately 4$\times$ reduction in memory footprint.

</details>


### [268] [Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization for LLM-Guided Program Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22613)
*Halil Alperen Gozeten,Xuechen Zhang,Emrullah Ildiz,Ege Onur Taga,Tara Javidi,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EMO-STA 框架，通过跨任务演化共享程序库后再进行适应，在提高算法发现性能的同时，有效地提升了计算效率并缓解了过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 指导演化搜索通常独立优化每个任务，未能利用任务间的共享结构，导致计算效率低下且在少量数据下易过拟合。

Method: 提出 EMO-STA（Shared-Then-Adapt）框架：第一阶段为跨任务演化共享程序库，第二阶段将共享程序适应到特定目标任务。探讨了多种适应策略（热启动、最佳平均值、最佳局部适应等）。

Result: 在八个任务族上表现优于单任务演化；STA Best-Local 适应性最强，STA Best-Shared 迁移能力最稳健；研究证明平衡共享与适应的计算预算是关键。

Conclusion: EMO-STA 是一个有效的多任务演化框架，通过在任务族中演化共享程序库并进行定向适应，不仅提升了算法发现的性能，还通过强制泛化缓解了过拟合问题。

Abstract: Recent LLM-guided evolutionary search methods have shown that iterative program mutation can discover strong algorithms, but they typically optimize each task independently, even when related tasks share reusable structure. We introduce Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization (EMO) for LLM-guided program discovery, and propose EMO-STA (Shared-Then-Adapt), a two-stage framework that first evolves a shared archive of executable programs across a task family and then adapts selected shared candidates to each target task. Within EMO-STA, we explore multiple adaptation strategies, including warm-starting from the shared archive, adapting the best average shared program, and adapting the shared program that performs best on each target task. Across eight task families spanning continuous optimization, geometric construction, modeling, and algorithmic optimization, EMO-STA improves over matched-compute single-task evolution in most settings, with STA Best-Local providing the strongest in-distribution adaptation and STA Best-Shared yielding robust transfer to unseen tasks. Compute-allocation experiments show that allocating a substantial fraction of the family-level budget to shared evolution is consistently beneficial, with roughly balanced shared and adaptation budgets often being optimal. Beyond compute efficiency, we show that shared evolution can mitigate overfitting in low-evidence settings (e.g. few training data), including ARC tasks and time-series feature engineering, by favoring programs that generalize across all tasks rather than exploiting task-specific brittle artifacts.

</details>


### [269] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出了CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器和ProFine微调策略，成功将临床ECG基础模型迁移至可穿戴设备，实现了高效、高精度的跨个体认知负荷评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决实时认知负荷评估中存在的标注数据匮乏、跨个体泛化性能差，以及现有临床ECG基础模型因传感器配置与任务差异无法直接应用于可穿戴设备的问题。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心模块：1. LeadBridge：一个可学习的适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为临床标准的12导联表示；2. ProFine：一种渐进式微调策略，通过分层解冻编码器层来优化模型性能，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公开数据集上进行“留一法”交叉验证，CogAdapt表现显著优于从头训练的基准模型，宏观F1得分分别达到0.626和0.768。

Conclusion: CogAdapt框架通过适配临床心电图(ECG)基础模型，有效解决了可穿戴设备进行认知负荷评估时数据稀缺和跨个体泛化能力差的问题，为基于可穿戴传感器进行准确的跨个体认知负荷评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [270] [A note on convergence of Wasserstein policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22622)
*David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 Wasserstein 策略优化（WPO）算法提供了理论基础，证明了在熵正则化设置下，WPO 可实现线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Wasserstein 策略优化（WPO）在实验中表现良好，但在连续状态和动作空间环境下的理论收敛性尚未得到充分证明。

Method: 利用平均场分析（mean-field analysis）方法，结合对数-索博列夫不等式（log-Sobolev inequalities），证明了梯度流的单调能量耗散及局部对数-索博列夫不等式。

Result: 作者证明了在这种数学框架下，价值函数能够线性收敛至全局最优解。

Conclusion: 在熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程架构下，WPO 算法能够实现线性收敛。

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [271] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MambaGaze利用XMD编码和双向Mamba-2架构，解决了实时眼动认知负荷评估中数据缺失和时序建模的难题，在保证高精度的同时实现了边缘设备上的高效实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知负荷评估系统在处理眼动数据的频繁缺失（闪烁、跟踪失败）以及低效建模长时序依赖方面存在不足，导致难以应用于实时安全关键场景（如驾驶员监控、飞行辅助）。

Method: 1) XMD编码：通过融合观测掩码（observation masks）和时间差（time-deltas）来增强原始特征，显式建模数据不确定性以处理缺失数据；2) 双向Mamba-2：利用状态空间模型捕获时序依赖，具备线性计算复杂度和高效的长程建模能力。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上，MambaGaze的表现超过CNN、Transformer等基准模型（准确率分别提升4-12个百分点）；在NVIDIA Jetson平台上实现了43-68 FPS的实时推理，且功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze通过结合XMD编码和双向Mamba-2架构，有效解决了眼动追踪数据缺失及长时序依赖建模问题，在保持高准确率的同时实现了低功耗边缘侧实时部署，是认知负荷监测的理想解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [272] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了β-VAE中的后验坍塌本质上是一种自动的频谱剪枝，并利用朗道稳定性分析证明了潜在模式会按效用高低被β参数级联剥离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释β-VAE中后验坍塌现象的机理，并探讨β值如何影响潜在模式的保留与剔除。

Method: 利用朗道稳定性分析（Landau stability analysis）推导损失函数；定义了一个潜在重缩放不变的序参数（order parameter）用于对活跃潜在模式排序。

Result: 在线性高斯情况下，坍塌谱、效用谱与归一化PCA谱相一致；通过WorldClim数据集验证了上述理论预测。

Conclusion: β-VAE中的后验坍塌（posterior collapse）是一种自动的频谱剪枝机制；不同β值形成的平衡解揭示了潜在模式从最不重要到最重要的级联坍塌过程，且线性高斯情形下坍塌遵循平均场定律。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [273] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 在 RLVR 训练中因硬截断导致的信号丢失问题，本文提出了 NSR 方法，通过随机保留近边界信息，提升了模型训练的稳定性和收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析发现 RLVR（如 GRPO）中硬截断机制会导致模型在近边界区域丢失重要信息，从而引发训练不稳定和收敛受限。

Method: 提出“近边界随机抢救”（Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue, NSR）机制：在 GRPO 训练中，针对因硬截断而丢弃的近边界信息，引入随机采样机制，将其部分保留以作为补充信号。

Result: 在 7B 到 30B 参数规模（包括稠密模型与 MoE 架构）的实验中，NSR 显著提升了训练稳定性，并超越了 DAPO 和 GSPO 等强基线方法。

Conclusion: NSR 是一种简单、即插即用的方案，通过对越界信号进行随机保留，有效缓解了 RLVR 训练中的不稳定性，优于确定性的梯度衰减方法，在多种模型架构和规模上均表现出卓越的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [274] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究开发了一套轻量级审计管线，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）定位GPT-2在特定任务上的错误特征。结果证实SAE能提供可解释的关联，但强调了相关并不代表因果，且SAE在预测能力上与原始残差流相比并无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过审计稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征，探索GPT-2小模型在特定任务（IOI）中执行失败时的内部神经机制，并评估SAE在提升模型可解释性方面的实际效能及局限性。

Method: 1. 选取GPT-2 small模型及IOI任务数据集（300条提示词）；2. 利用Bloom (2024)的Layer-8残差流SAE提取特征；3. 通过统计推断（Holm校正、Cohen's d）筛选与模型错误相关的特征；4. 通过因果消融（Causal Ablation）、代表性基准（Logistic Regression）、种子鲁棒性检查（Seed-robustness check）对发现的相关性进行验证。

Result: 1. 识别出146个与任务失败显著相关的SAE特征；2. 特征17491被定位为与“keys”对象相关的最强相关器，但在因果消融实验中无法复现模型失败；3. 对比实验显示，SAE特征的预测能力与原始残差流的逻辑回归基准持平；4. 种子鲁棒性测试表明特定特征的关联具有波动性，证实了单一审计结果的局限。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要物理贡献在于提供了一套轻量级、模型不可知的机械可解释性审计管线（Audit Pipeline），而非发现单一特征。研究证明了通过审计稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征能有效定位与模型性能相关联的神经元/特征，但同时也强调了“相关性”不等于“因果性”，SAE特征在提供可解释性的同时，并不一定比原始维度具备更强的预测能力。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [275] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三元决策树，通过在拆分节点处引入自适应的不确定性区域，允许模型对接近决策边界的样本采用加权融合预测，从而显著提升了分类准确性并提供了重要的不确定性告知功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树使用硬阈值进行空间划分，无法区分边界样本与核心样本，导致对所有样本的预测给予相同的置信度，且缺乏对边界区域不确定性的量化能力。

Method: 提出了“三元决策树”框架：在每个拆分节点引入宽度为 delta 的不确定性区域。在此区域内的样本通过子树加权融合进行预测并被标记为“边界不确定”。同时提出了五种 delta 计算方法：质量平原（quality-plateau）、类别重叠（class-overlap）、增益比（gain-ratio）、节点引导抽样（node-bootstrap）和边距（margin）。

Result: 在 72 个 OpenML-CC18 数据集上的测试表明，五种方法均显著优于标准 CART。Margin 方法综合效率最高。实验证明该方法在医疗和金融等实际场景中能有效识别高风险样本。

Conclusion: 三元决策树通过在分类边界引入不确定性区域，能有效识别模棱两可的样本，显著提升了模型的判别准确率并增强了预测的可信度。其中，Margin方法以其无需超参数且高效的特性表现最优。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [276] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SeqLoRA，这是一种通过双层优化联合训练 LoRA 因子并基于理论界限减轻灾难性遗忘的框架，解决了多概念扩散模型个性化中表示干扰与表达力之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散模型中，多概念组合存在表示干扰问题。现有的模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后处理融合，要么冻结适应子空间，导致表达能力和概念保真度受限。为了解决这一权衡，研究提出了 SeqLoRA。

Method: 提出了 Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA)，这是一种基于双层优化（bilevel optimization）的受限持续学习框架，旨在联合优化 LoRA 的两个低秩因子，并利用矩阵次高斯过程及其残差层激活建模来理论地推导出抗遗忘界限。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA 在最多 101 个概念的场景下提升了身份保持度和可扩展性，同时规避了昂贵的后处理融合，并有效减少了生成过程中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA 实现了通过联合优化 LoRA 因子来平衡多概念组合的表示，避免了干扰，且比固定基底方法更具表达力。它为在大规模多概念定制生成任务中实现高效、高质量的个性化提供了理论支持和实践可行性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [277] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Recuriosity，通过在线3D重建提供空间记忆，并利用基于序列模型的策略提供情景上下文，解决了复杂3D环境中好奇心驱动探索效率低下的问题，实现了更强的环境概括和任务适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励、长时程的3D任务中，传统好奇心驱动的强化学习易受噪声影响或陷入局部循环（即对已遗忘的区域持续获得奖励），导致在复杂、逼真的环境中探索效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种结合两部分设计的框架：1. 使用在线3D重建作为世界的持久模型（解决空间持久性问题）；2. 将智能体策略参数化为RGB观测的序列模型（维护情景记忆，即长程上下文）。训练过程仅依赖好奇心内在奖励。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上，该方法优于基于RL的主动映射基线；展现了对Gibson和AI生成环境的零样本概括能力；在苹果采摘和图像目标导航等下游任务中，相比从头训练的基线表现出更优的适应效率。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式空间持久化模型（通过在线3D重建）和情景记忆（通过序列模型），成功克服了现有好奇心驱动RL在复杂3D环境中遇到的局部循环和重复奖励问题，实现了高效的零样本概括和下游任务适应。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [DeltaBox: Scaling Stateful AI Agents with Millisecond-Level Sandbox Checkpoint/Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22781)
*Yunpeng Dong,Jingkai He,Yuze Hou,Dong Du,Zhonghu Xu,Si Yu,Yubin Xia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 针对 AI Agent 频繁状态切换导致的高延迟问题，DeltaBox 引入增量式状态备份与恢复机制（DeltaState），实现了毫秒级的沙箱状态切换，解决了深度搜索任务下的系统瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的 AI Agent 在进行深度搜索（如树搜索、强化学习）时，频繁的完整沙箱状态（文件、内存、上下文）备份与回滚会导致严重的性能延迟，制约了搜索效率。

Method: 提出 DeltaState OS 级抽象：1. DeltaFS：利用分层文件系统和写入时复制（CoW）实现文件状态的增量管理；2. DeltaCR：通过增量内存转储及基于“冻结模板进程”的直接 fork() 机制，实现进程状态的快速恢复。两者共同集成为 DeltaBox 沙箱。

Result: 在 SWE-bench 和 RL 微基准测试中，DeltaBox 将检查点和回滚延迟降低至毫秒级，相比传统机制实现了显著的性能提升，使 AI Agent 在有限时间内能够探索更多的任务路径。

Conclusion: DeltaBox 通过 DeltaFS 和 DeltaCR 实现了毫秒级的检查点（14ms）与回滚（5ms），有效消除了 AI Agent 在高频状态探索中的性能瓶颈，显著提升了搜索深度与规模。

Abstract: LLM-powered AI agents require high-frequency state exploration (e.g., test-time tree search and reinforcement learning), relying on rapid checkpoint and rollback (C/R) of the complete sandbox state, including files and process state (e.g., memory, contexts, etc.). Existing mechanisms duplicate the entire state, causing hundreds of milliseconds to seconds of latency per C/R, which severely bottlenecks deep search and large-scale fan-outs.
  This paper observes that subsequent checkpoints in AI agents are highly similar. Therefore, instead of full duplication, a sandbox should only duplicate the changes between consecutive checkpoints (Key Insight). However, it is non-trivial to realize the idea, mainly due to the missing OS supports. This paper proposes a new OS-level abstraction, DeltaState, to enable the change-based transactional C/R for AI agents with two co-designed OS mechanisms. First, DeltaFS enables change-based filesystem C/R by organizing the file states into layers and dynamically freezing the writable layer and inserting a new one during checkpoint, reducing file updates to copy-on-write, and making rollback a simple layer switch. Second, DeltaCR enables change-based process state C/R using incremental dumps, and accelerates rollback by bypassing traditional pipelines to directly fork() from a frozen template process. We then present DeltaBox, a novel agent sandbox achieving millisecond level C/R through the two new mechanisms. Evaluations on SWE-bench and RL micro-benchmarks show DeltaBox completes checkpoint and rollback in millisecond-level latency (14ms and 5ms, respectively), empowering agents to explore substantially more nodes under fixed time budgets.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DynaFlow 是一个新型 ML 框架，它通过解耦逻辑定义与物理调度，让用户能够透明、灵活地在设备内部实现算子并行，从而在保持低工程成本的同时提升了 ML 系统的吞吐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备内并行化（intra-device parallelism）能提升 ML 系统资源利用率，但现有的框架执行模型僵化（顺序性），导致开发者必须进行侵入式、针对特定模型的代码重写，维护成本极高且难以适应多变的硬件和任务上下文。

Method: 提出 DynaFlow 框架，包括：1. 一个带有图分区标注的灵活前端，支持定义自定义并行化策略；2. 一个高效的异步执行后端，整合了自定义内存管理以减少拷贝开销，并兼容 CUDA Graphs 和 TorchInductor 等现有优化技术。

Result: DynaFlow 在 6 个先进的 ML 系统中成功集成了多种并行化策略，仅需极少的代码更改，最高可实现 1.29 倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DynaFlow 通过解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度，有效解决了机器学习框架中实现设备内并行化（intra-device parallelism）的高工程成本和僵化问题，为提升高性能模型训练与推理效率提供了一种通用且易于集成的方法。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


### [280] [Secure and Parallel Determinant Computation for Large-Scale Matrices in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22039)
*Prajwal Panth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 SPDC 的边缘计算框架，通过 CED 加密和无交互的并行 LU 分解，在保护数据隐私的前提下，高效实现了资源受限场景下的矩阵行列式计算，并提供了低成本的结果完整性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物联网边缘计算环境中，矩阵行列式计算（MDC）计算复杂度高（立方级），且存在隐私泄露风险和计算资源受限问题，现有算法难以满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出了“安全并行行列式计算”（SPDC）框架，包括：1. 组合元素混淆（CED）加密机制，通过元素级混淆（EWO）和 Panth 旋转定理（PRT）隐藏矩阵内容；2. 基于并行 LU 分解的分布式计算模型，无需服务器间通信；3. 设计了两种结果完整性验证算法（Q_2 概率算法与 Q_3 确定性算法）。

Result: 数学分析表明，SPDC 框架在保证强隐私保护和结果完整性的前提下，显著降低了计算与通信开销，实现了高效、可扩展且安全的分布式行列式计算。

Conclusion: SPDC 框架通过将隐私保护方案（CED）、并行化计算（并行 LU 分解）与高效的结果验证算法相结合，成功解决了边缘计算中复杂矩阵行列式计算的实时性、安全性与隐私性挑战，适用于资源受限的物联网环境。

Abstract: The advent of edge computing has enabled resource-constrained clients to delegate intensive computational tasks to distributed edge servers, especially within Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Among such tasks, Matrix Determinant Computation (MDC) remains critical for applications in control systems, cryptography, and machine learning. However, the cubic complexity of traditional determinant algorithms makes them unsuitable for real-time processing in constrained edge scenarios.
  We propose a Secure Parallel Determinant Computation (SPDC) framework, which provides strong security guaranties, including privacy-preserving MDC, across N distributed edge servers. The framework achieves privacy through Composite Element Distortion (CED) - a lightweight encryption method that combines Element-wise Obfuscation (EWO) and the Panth Rotation Theorem (PRT) to conceal both structural and numerical matrix content while preserving determinant properties. Parallel LU decomposition is used to distribute encrypted matrix blocks across an arbitrary number of untrusted edge servers, enabling efficient and scalable determinant computation. A one-way communication model further reduces coordination overhead by eliminating inter-server interactions. To ensure result integrity with minimal client burden, we further introduce two verification algorithms: Q_2, a probabilistic scalar method, and Q_3, a deterministic and low-complexity alternative.
  Mathematical analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework provides strong privacy and security guaranties, low computational overhead, and deployment flexibility - making it well-suited for secure, scalable, and real-time MDC in distributed edge-assisted systems.

</details>


### [281] [Nf-PEAK: Process-Based Energy Attribution for Nextflow Workflows on Kubernetes Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22393)
*Philipp Thamm,Somayeh Mohammadi,Kathleen West,Knut Reinert,Lauritz Thamsen,Ulf Leser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nf-PEAK 是一种基于容器的能耗分析方法，通过结合 cgroup 元数据和非线性归因模型，解决了在 Kubernetes 集群中精确测量异质科学工作流任务级能耗的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流任务具有高度异质性，而现有的 Kubernetes 监控方案（如 Kepler）难以解决集群中多任务并发带来的资源竞争与噪声干扰，从而无法精确地评估单个工作流任务的能耗，这限制了能源优化成本与可持续性决策。

Method: 提出一种名为 Nf-PEAK 的容器化方法，具体步骤包括：(1) 在 Kubernetes 集群中标识工作流 Pod；(2) 通过 cgroup 元数据将 Pod 映射到宿主机进程；(3) 同步采样 Intel RAPL 能耗数据及各进程性能计数器；(4) 应用非线性能量归因模型，将节点级能耗细粒度地分配至各任务。

Result: 在 Kubernetes 环境下对三个 nf-core 工作流进行测试。结果显示，Nf-PEAK 的平均平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）在隔离运行时为 6.6%，在存在干扰负载（饱和 8/32 线程）时为 10.9%，且在跨节点扩展时表现稳定；相比 Kepler，其在处理共存负载时的估计误差显著更低。

Conclusion: Nf-PEAK 是一种在 Kubernetes 上进行任务级能耗分析的有效工具，在隔离环境和存在干扰负载的情况下，其测量精度优于现有主流工具 Kepler，为科学工作流的节能优化提供了可靠的方法支撑。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are pipelines of interdependent tasks. They are increasingly executed on shared Kubernetes clusters via workflow engines such as Nextflow. Their energy consumption matters for both cost and sustainability. It is necessary to examine and optimize workflow tasks individually, because they can be very heterogeneous. However, estimating task-level energy on clusters is difficult: Intel RAPL counters report only node-level energy, access to counters and host process information is typically restricted, and concurrent workloads introduce resource contention and measurement noise. We present Nf-PEAK, a containerized method to attribute CPU-package and DRAM energy to individual processes and Nextflow tasks. Nf-PEAK (i) identifies workflow pods, (ii) maps pods to host processes via cgroup metadata, (iii) samples RAPL and per-process performance counters, and (iv) applies a non-linear energy-credit model before aggregating results at task level. On a Kubernetes cluster, we evaluate three nf-core workflows under controlled co-located CPU load. Nf-PEAK reaches an average Mean Absolute Percentage Error of 6.6% in isolated runs and 10.9% when an unrelated workload saturates 8 of 32 hardware threads per node, and remains stable across 2, 3, 4, and 8 nodes. Compared to the state-of-the-art Kubernetes tool Kepler, Nf-PEAK yields lower error on average, particularly under co-located load.

</details>


### [282] [AI-Driven Multi-Region Provisioning for Cloud Services Using Spot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22778)
*Javier Fabra,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的点实例（Spot Instance）多区域配置系统，能在部署前精准预估成本并利用跨区域定价差异，在保持原有调度逻辑的同时，实现了高达64%的成本优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务平台的动态工作负载依赖弹性基础设施，点实例虽成本低廉但存在价格波动、资源不确定性及被中断风险。现有的AWS EC2 Spot服务无法在部署前预估成本且局限于单区域，缺乏跨区域的全局成本优化能力。

Method: 提出了一种AI驱动的配置服务，该方法结合了配置计划监控与预测模型，在实例启动前对机群配置和价格进行预估，从而辅助实现跨区域的成本感知决策。

Result: 该系统在多达1500个vCPU的机群试验中，与EC2 Spot服务相比达到了99.79%的预测准确率，并利用区域间的价格差异实现了高达64%的潜在成本节省。

Conclusion: 通过人工智能驱动的多区域点实例（Spot Instances）配置服务，能够在保持原有EC2服务行为特性的前提下，实现跨区域的最优成本部署，有效解决了单一区域部署的局限性并大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Cloud service platforms increasingly rely on elastic infrastructures to support dynamic workloads. Spot instances provide discounted computing resources but introduce uncertainty due to dynamic pricing, resource availability, and interruption risks that vary across geographical regions. In Amazon Web Services, the EC2 Spot Service simplifies fleet provisioning through allocation strategies, but it cannot estimate fleet costs before deployment and restricts provisioning to a single region. This paper presents an AI-driven provisioning service for multi-region spot fleets. The proposed approach combines monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch, enabling cost-aware deployment decisions across regions while preserving the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service. The system was validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs. Experimental results show a prediction accuracy of 99.79% compared to the EC2 Spot Service and potential cost savings of up to 64% by exploiting regional price variability.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [Throughput-Optimal Multiresource-Job Scheduling with Continuous Requirement Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21715)
*Heyuan Yao,Willow Kowalik,Izzy Grosof*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对现实中资源需求呈连续分布的问题，本文首次提出了适用于此类场景的吞吐量最优调度策略族，通过动态离散化技术，在保证理论最优性的同时，在大规模数据中心负载中实现了性能领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多资源队列模型多依赖于“类簇”（Class-based）假设，即要求资源需求种类较少，无法有效模拟现实中资源需求值高度离散且连续的复杂实际场景。

Method: 提出了基于离散化（Discretization）方法的调度策略族，通过根据系统负载和资源需求分布调整离散粒度，实现了在连续多资源环境下的吞吐量最优，并包含了抢占式和非抢占式变体。

Result: 所提出的策略在多种参数化分布及真实的Google Borg数据中心轨迹测试中表现出色，不仅证明了在连续模型下的吞吐量最优性，且相比现有的索引策略，在计算效率和性能上均达到了最先进（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一系列针对连续多资源作业（MRJ）模型的吞吐量最优调度策略，弥补了现有理论在处理连续分布资源需求时的空白，并验证了其在大规模数据中心调度中的优越性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern computing systems process jobs with resource requirements such as CPU and memory, which are described by multiresource jobs (MRJ) queueing models. In practice, job resource requirements are spread out over so many values, that it is rare to see the same value twice. This pattern is best modeled by a continuous distribution of requirement values. However, the existing theoretical work on stability or throughput-optimality focuses on queueing models with class-based resource requirements. In class-based models, the number of distinct resource requirements must be small to demonstrate strong empirical performance, making them a poor match for these practical systems.
  We introduce the first throughput-optimal family of scheduling policies for the continuous MRJ model, with both preemptive and nonpreemptive variants. We further introduce several efficient policy families, which remain throughput-optimal while considerably improving computational efficiency, under some distributional assumptions. We use a discretization approach, where we choose the discretization granularity based on the system load and the distribution of resource requirements. We validate the real-world applicability of our policies by comparing them against existing index-based policies on parametrized distributions and on datacenter trace data from the Google Borg scheduler, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [284] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需预处理或因果充分性假设的局部协变量选择方法，通过在定义的局部边界内高效搜索，实现了高维数据下因果效应的有效估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于全局因果结构学习（维度灾难）、因果充分性假设（存在隐藏混淆因子）或预处理假设（限制协变量选择），这些在实际复杂应用场景中往往难以满足且计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出了一种局部学习方法（Local Learning Method）：首先刻画一个包含有效调整集的“局部边界（Local Boundary）”，随后在边界内开发局部的因果识别程序，以高效筛选协变量。

Result: 通过合成数据和真实世界数据集验证，该方法在保持因果效应估计准确性的同时，相比全局学习方法大幅提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需“预处理假设”和“因果充分性”的前提下，能够实现因果效应的无偏估计。研究证明了该方法的完备性和可靠性，且在处理高维数据时展现出显著的计算效率优势。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [285] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的离线广告保留价决策框架，通过保守的认证机制取代单纯的点估计排序，有效降低了离线评估中的风险并提升了策略验证的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的回放表（Replay tables）和离线评估方法虽能识别高收益策略，但会忽略弱支持、多重比较效应、子组危害及出价者响应不确定性，无法直接回答现有证据是否足够支持上线验证。

Method: 开发了一种支持感知的离线决策框架。该框架不直接输出单一最优策略，而是将日志证据转化为包含“已认证策略”、“统计上占优的替代方案”以及“需进一步验证的候选方案”的保守决策对象，并引入了同时不确定性控制（Simultaneous Uncertainty Control）和保守支持门限（Conservative Support Gates）。

Result: 在iPinYou数据集实验中，该框架将19个策略缩减为2个验证候选项，并认证了44个细分市场的无害性；核心保留规则实现了47.66%的回放提升，以及40.71%的同时下界提升。

Conclusion: 离线广告保留价评估应从简单的点估计排序转向提供认证的验证决策，以平衡收益获取与风险控制。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [286] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过在线博弈策略的财富动态分析，提供了一种导出概率论中迭代对数律（LIL）的替代性证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过可操作的算术化方法（博弈论视角）重新构建经典概率论中迭代对数律的证明，以提供一种更具直观性和计算性的理解方式。

Method: 回顾并推导了基于在线博弈策略的财富增长界限，利用鞅（Martingales）和概率论中的对数法则，确立了该博弈策略在长期表现中的收敛性质。

Result: 成功利用在线博弈策略的财富积累过程证明了迭代对数律，展示了该策略在风险管理与概率界限推导上的有效性。

Conclusion: 表明通过在线博弈策略（Online Betting Strategy）的财富增长保证，可以推导出迭代对数律（Law of the Iterated Logarithm, LIL），建立起博弈论方法与经典概率论极限法则之间的联系。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [287] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复一级价格拍卖中“托”（shilling）对学习者的负面影响，开发了一种结合稳健消除与乐观去偏的算法，有效解决了受干扰反馈下的投标策略学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在重复的一级价格拍卖中，当存在“托”（shilling）行为且这种行为仅影响反馈信息（而非拍卖结果）时，机器学习模型如何克服干扰并学习最优投标策略。

Method: 提出了一个结合两种策略的算法：(1) 稳健区间消除分支（robust interval-elimination）：忽略受干扰的报告，实现 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$ 的收敛速率；(2) 乐观分支（optimistic branch）：去偏（debias）失败后的观测值，利用可靠信息实现 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ 的收敛速率。并引入验证与竞争（validation and racing）机制来自动化选择机制。

Result: 证明了该算法在不同条件下均能获得最优的遗憾界（regret bound），并提供了匹配的下界，证明了该算法在单活动区域情况下的最优性。

Conclusion: 即使是仅影响反馈（而非分配）的“托”行为，也会显著增加重复拍卖中学习最优出价的统计难度，导致收敛速率产生本质性变化。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [288] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了 mHSIC 和 mdHSIC 两种统计量，通过鞅构造规避了昂贵的计算置换过程，不仅提升了独立性检验的计算速度（25-60倍），还在高维多变量联合独立性检验中保持了优越的统计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 HSIC 和 dHSIC 统计量属于退化的 V-统计量，其零分布依赖于数据，必须使用计算开销巨大的置换检验（permutation calibration）来确定阈值，这限制了其在大样本和高维场景下的应用。

Method: 受双样本检验中鞅 MMD 构造的启发，提出两种学生化（studentised）统计量：1. mHSIC：采用自归一化的上三角和构造，避免了置换；2. mdHSIC：采用半样本分割（half-sample split）技术，在处理多变量联合独立性时实现线性复杂度。

Result: 在合成数据集上的实验表明，这两种方法在保持第一类错误率和检测功效的同时，计算速度达到了传统置换法的 25 到 60 倍，且 mdHSIC 在多变量联合检测中展现出对变量维度极佳的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 研究人员提出了两种新的统计量（mHSIC 和 mdHSIC），通过鞅差序列构造，在保证与传统基准相当的检测功效的前提下，消除了对置换检验的依赖，显著降低了计算开销，特别是在高维联合独立性检验中表现优异。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [289] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于一步生成模型的“保守漂移方法”，通过使用KDE梯度场取代传统的位移速度场，从理论上解决了非保守性问题，并提供了严格的有限粒子收敛速率分析及生成质量保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一步生成模型位移漂移方法存在非保守性问题，且缺乏严格的有限粒子收敛理论分析。

Method: 提出了一种基于核密度估计（KDE）梯度的一步生成模型保守漂移方法，该方法通过由平滑数据得分与平滑模型得分之差构建的梯度场，解决了通用位移漂移场缺乏保守性的问题。

Result: 建立了保守漂移方法的有限粒子收敛界，证明了在特定正则条件下可达到 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ 或更高阶的收敛速率，并定量分析了带宽（bandwidth）对残差速度的影响。

Conclusion: 证明了该保守漂移方法在连续时间下的有限粒子收敛界，明确了带宽依赖关系，并推导出了单步生成保证；此外，对原有的非保守方法进行了分解分析，指出了其必然存在的残差项。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>
